7	// LEVEL 2 ("The Drone Bay") — the same island, revisited.
8	//
9	// The day-to-day agentic loop, played: delegate → review → accept.
10	// Five ship systems are dark; five drones can fix them in parallel while you
11	// can only be in one place. One new command on top of what L1 taught:
12	//
13	//   walk to a dark system, press E      → a drone takes the job, you're free
14	//   the drone finishes while you're away → its work seals under a checkpoint
15	//   walk up → `entire checkpoint explain <id>` is PRE-FILLED — press Enter
16	//   read what it actually did            → ADD TO SHIP (Y) → system online
17	//   the FIFTH accept keeps the terminal open: type `entire dispatch` right
18	//   there — the day's report writes itself from the checkpoints. Finish line.
19	//
20	// Drones don't repair — they IMPROVISE (a bent dish comes back as a signal
21	// spire). The transformation is what makes you NEED the explanation.
22	//
23	// PRESSURE: one countdown. Watching one drone at a time can't beat the clock;
24	// running the whole fleet in parallel can. Nothing forces it — the math does.
25

28	const TOTAL_TIME = 150;      // seconds for the whole run (tunable)
29	const WORK_TIME = 18;        // seconds a drone spends fixing a system
30	const LOW_TIME = 45;         // clock turns urgent under this
31	const CRIT_TIME = 15;        // clock goes CRITICAL under this
32	const PANIC_TIME = 45;       // the SKY starts shifting toward panic-red
33	const DRONE_SPEED = 26;      // world units / second in flight

123	function withWarnLight(g) {
124	  // A small red blinking bulb so broken things read as "broken" up close.
125	  const bulb = new THREE.Mesh(
126	    new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.22, 10, 10),
127	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x401512, emissive: 0xff3b2e, emissiveIntensity: 1.6 })
128	  );
129	  bulb.position.y = g.userData.warnY ?? 3.2;
130	  g.add(bulb);
131	  g.userData.warn = bulb;
132	  return g;
133	}
134	function buildBrokenCoils() {
135	  const g = new THREE.Group();
136	  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
137	    const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.1 - i * 0.18, 1.2 - i * 0.18, 1.0, 10), i ? CHARRED() : SCORCH());
138	    c.position.y = 0.5 + i * 1.05;
139	    c.rotation.z = (i % 2 ? -1 : 1) * 0.12 * (i + 1);   // the stack leans, sadly
140	    g.add(c);
141	  }
142	  g.userData.warnY = 3.6;
143	  return withWarnLight(g);
144	}

195	function buildPlasmaRing() {
196	  const g = new THREE.Group();

208	function buildStarDome() {
209	  const g = new THREE.Group();
210	  const ped = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.9, 1.2, 1.2, 8), SCORCH());
211	  ped.position.y = 0.6;
212	  const stars = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
213	  const pts = new Float32Array(120 * 3);
214	  for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {
215	    // random points in a dome above the pedestal
216	    const r = 1.2 + Math.random() * 1.4;
217	    const th = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
218	    const ph = Math.random() * Math.PI * 0.5;
219	    pts[i * 3] = Math.cos(th) * Math.sin(ph) * r;
220	    pts[i * 3 + 1] = 3.2 + Math.cos(ph) * r * 0.9;
221	    pts[i * 3 + 2] = Math.sin(th) * Math.sin(ph) * r;
222	  }
223	  stars.setAttribute("position", new THREE.BufferAttribute(pts, 3));
224	  const field = new THREE.Points(stars, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
225	    color: 0xbfe9ff, size: 0.16, transparent: true, opacity: 0.95,
226	    blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false,
227	  }));
228	  const orbit = new THREE.Mesh(
229	    new THREE.TorusGeometry(2.0, 0.05, 8, 40),
230	    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.7 })
231	  );
232	  orbit.position.y = 3.6;
233	  orbit.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2.4;
234	  g.add(ped, field, orbit);
235	  g.userData.anim = { type: "dome", field, orbit };
236	  return g;
237	}

244	  spire.position.y = 3.75;
245	  const beam = new THREE.Mesh(
246	    new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.4, 0.4, 70, 10, 1, true),

248	      map: beamTex, color: 0x9af0ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.55,

258	function buildGardenPod() {
259	  const g = new THREE.Group();
260	  const tray = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.9, 2.1, 0.6, 12), SCORCH());
261	  tray.position.y = 0.3;
262	  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
263	    const plant = new THREE.Mesh(
264	      new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.28, 0.9 + (i % 3) * 0.35, 6),

266	    );
267	    const a = (i / 5) * Math.PI * 2;
268	    plant.position.set(Math.cos(a) * 1.0, 1.0, Math.sin(a) * 1.0);
269	    g.add(plant);

275	      thickness: 1.2, transparent: true, opacity: 0.4,
276	    })
277	  );
278	  domeGlass.position.y = 0.6;

309	function buildDrone() {
310	  const g = new THREE.Group();
311	  const body = new THREE.Mesh(
312	    new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.7, 0),
313	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x2c3140, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.3, emissive: 0x2a8aa6, emissiveIntensity: 1.0 })
314	  );
315	  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(
316	    new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.0, 0.09, 8, 24),
317	    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.8 })
318	  );
319	  ring.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
320	  g.add(body, ring);
321	  g.userData = { body, ring };
322	  return g;

345	export function createDroneBayView(renderer, { onExit, onComplete, onNext } = {}) {
346	  const canvas = renderer.domElement;
347	
348	  // ---------- scene & sky ----------
349	  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
350	  scene.background = SKY_CALM.clone();
351	  scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(SKY_CALM.clone(), 90, 320);
352	
353	  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(
354	    62, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 2000
355	  );
356	
357	  // ---------- lighting ----------
358	  scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xcdbcff, 0x3a2f5e, 0.7));
359	  const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(SUN_CALM, 1.5);
360	  sun.position.set(60, 90, 40);
361	  scene.add(sun);
362	  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x6a5a92, 0.3));
363	
364	  const dome = new THREE.Mesh(
365	    new THREE.SphereGeometry(900, 32, 16),
366	    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: DOME_CALM.clone(), side: THREE.BackSide, fog: false })
367	  );
368	  scene.add(dome);
369	
370	  // ---------- terrain (the same island) ----------
371	  const terrain = createTerrain({ size: 200, segments: 220, maxHeight: 26, seed: 1337 });
372	  scene.add(terrain.mesh, terrain.water);
373	
374	  const beamTex = makeBeamTexture();
375	
376	  // ---------- the five work sites ----------
377	  const sites = SYSTEMS.map((sys, idx) => {
378	    const anchor = new THREE.Group();
379	    anchor.position.set(sys.pos.x, terrain.heightAt(sys.pos.x, sys.pos.z), sys.pos.z);
380	
381	    const BUILD = {
382	      buildBrokenCoils, buildBrokenNav, buildBrokenDish, buildBrokenVent, buildBrokenStrut,
383	      buildPlasmaRing, buildStarDome, buildGardenPod, buildGravSkid,
384	      buildSignalSpire: () => buildSignalSpire(beamTex),
385	    };
386	    const brokenModel = BUILD[sys.broken]();
387	    const upgradeModel = BUILD[sys.upgrade]();
388	    upgradeModel.visible = false;
389	    anchor.add(brokenModel, upgradeModel);
390	
391	    // Status beam — readable from across the island:
392	    // amber = broken, cyan pulse = drone at work, ice-blue = sealed (review me).
393	    const beam = new THREE.Mesh(
394	      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.6, 0.6, 60, 12, 1, true),
395	      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
396	        map: beamTex, color: 0xff8a5c, transparent: true, opacity: 0.3,
397	        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide,
398	        fog: false,
399	      })
400	    );
401	    beam.position.y = 30;
402	    anchor.add(beam);
403	
404	    // The checkpoint seal: ice + floating trailer id, like Level 1's bank.
405	    const ice = makeIceBlock();
406	    ice.scale.setScalar(1.25);
407	    ice.position.y = 2.6;
408	    ice.visible = false;
409	    anchor.add(ice);
410	    const sprite = makeIdSprite(sys.ckpt);
411	    sprite.position.y = 8.4;
412	    sprite.visible = false;
413	    anchor.add(sprite);
414	
415	    scene.add(anchor);
416	    return {
417	      sys, idx, anchor, brokenModel, upgradeModel, beam, ice, sprite,
418	      state: "broken",      // broken → working → sealed → added
419	      workT: 0,
420	      meltT: 0,             // ice melt animation after ADD TO SHIP
421	    };
422	  });
423	
424	  // ---------- the drone bay: five parked drones by the console ----------
425	  const drones = SYSTEMS.map((_, i) => {
426	    const d = buildDrone();
427	    const x = BAY_ROW.x0 + i * BAY_ROW.dx;
428	    const home = new THREE.Vector3(x, terrain.heightAt(x, BAY_ROW.z) + 1.2, BAY_ROW.z);
429	    d.position.copy(home);
430	    scene.add(d);
431	    return {
432	      model: d, home,
433	      state: "bay",         // bay → flying → working → returning
434	      site: null,
435	      from: new THREE.Vector3(), to: new THREE.Vector3(),
436	      t: 0, dur: 1,
437	      bobOff: Math.random() * 10,
438	    };
439	  });
440	
441	  // Work beam shown under a working drone (one per drone, toggled).
442	  const workBeams = drones.map(() => {
443	    const m = new THREE.Mesh(
444	      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.25, 1.2, 6, 10, 1, true),
445	      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
446	        color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.5,
447	        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide,
448	      })
449	    );
450	    m.visible = false;
451	    scene.add(m);
452	    return m;
453	  });
454	
455	  // ---------- the ship console ----------
456	  const consoleAnchor = new THREE.Group();
457	  consoleAnchor.position.set(
458	    CONSOLE_POS.x, terrain.heightAt(CONSOLE_POS.x, CONSOLE_POS.z), CONSOLE_POS.z
459	  );
460	  consoleAnchor.add(makeConsole());
461	  const consoleGlow = new THREE.PointLight(0xffd27a, 5, 28, 2);
462	  consoleGlow.position.y = 5;
463	  consoleAnchor.add(consoleGlow);
464	  scene.add(consoleAnchor);
465	
466	  // ---------- first-person controller ----------
467	  const fp = createFirstPerson(camera, canvas, {
468	    heightAt: terrain.heightAt,
469	    radius: terrain.radius * 0.96,
470	    eyeHeight: 2.6,
471	    speed: 24,
472	  });
473	  scene.add(fp.controls.object);
474	
475	  // Bird's-eye map (M) — see all five beams at once, keep walking meanwhile.
476	  const overhead = createOverhead(scene, terrain, camera);
477	  function setMap(on) {
478	    if (overhead.on === on) return;
479	    overhead.set(on);
480	    fp.setAlwaysMove(on);                       // arrows work without pointer lock
481	    scene.fog.near = on ? 500 : 90;             // don't fog the map out
482	    scene.fog.far = on ? 1400 : 320;
483	    crosshair?.classList.toggle("hidden", on || !fp.isLocked);
484	  }
485	
486	  // ---------- HUD elements (shared FP set + this level's own) ----------
487	  const promptEl = document.getElementById("fp-prompt");
488	  const controlsEl = document.getElementById("fp-controls");
489	  const crosshair = document.getElementById("crosshair");
490	  const tutorialEl = document.getElementById("tutorial");
491	  const fpShared = document.getElementById("fp-shared");
492	  const termEl = document.getElementById("terminal");
493	  const termHint = document.getElementById("term-hint");
494	  const termInput = document.getElementById("term-input");
495	  const termList = document.getElementById("term-list");
496	  const termMsg = document.getElementById("term-msg");
497	  const termCta = document.getElementById("term-cta");
498	  const dbHud = document.getElementById("db-hud");
499	  const briefingEl = document.getElementById("db-briefing");
500	  const briefingStartBtn = document.getElementById("db-briefing-start");
501	  const countdownEl = document.getElementById("db-countdown");
502	  const countdownTime = document.getElementById("db-countdown-time");
503	  const systemsEl = document.getElementById("db-systems-rows");
504	  const winEl = document.getElementById("db-win");
505	  const winSub = document.getElementById("db-win-sub");
506	  const failEl = document.getElementById("db-fail");
507	
508	  let active = false;
509	  let started = false;
510	  let failed = false;
511	  let reportSent = false;       // `entire dispatch` has been run — the win
512	  let promptText = null;
513	  let controlsLocked = null;
514	  const taught = new Set();
515	  let tutorialTimer = null;
516	  let msgTimer = null;
517	  let winTimer = null;
518	
519	  // Level countdown — the single source of pressure for the whole run.
520	  let timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
521	  let timerRunning = false;
522	
523	  // Terminal state. Two modes share the one terminal:
524	  //  "review" — pre-filled `entire checkpoint explain <id>` at a sealed site
525	  //  "report" — type `entire dispatch` (the finish line). Opens in place when
526	  //             the FIFTH system is accepted; reopens at any online system or
527	  //             the bay console if the player wandered off first.
528	  let terminalOpen = false;
529	  let termMode = null;
530	  let reviewSite = null;        // the sealed site being reviewed
531	  let explained = false;        // Enter pressed — the card is showing
532	  let buffer = "";
533	  let dismissedSite = null;     // site the player Esc'd out of (until they leave)
534	  let reportDismissed = false;
535	
536	  const addedCount = () => sites.filter((s) => s.state === "added").length;
537	  const allAdded = () => addedCount() >= sites.length;
538	
539	  // ---------- HUD helpers ----------
540	  function setPrompt(text) {
541	    if (!promptEl || text === promptText) return;
542	    promptText = text;
543	    if (text) { promptEl.textContent = text; promptEl.classList.remove("hidden"); }
544	    else promptEl.classList.add("hidden");
545	  }
546	  function showTutorial(text, ms = 5500) {
547	    if (!tutorialEl) return;
548	    clearTimeout(tutorialTimer);
549	    tutorialEl.textContent = text;
550	    tutorialEl.classList.remove("hidden");
551	    if (ms > 0) tutorialTimer = setTimeout(() => tutorialEl.classList.add("hidden"), ms);
552	  }
553	  function teachOnce(key, text, ms) {
554	    if (taught.has(key)) return;
555	    taught.add(key);
556	    showTutorial(text, ms);
557	  }
558	  function setControls(locked) {
559	    if (!controlsEl) return;
560	    if (locked === controlsLocked && !controlsEl.classList.contains("hidden")) return;
561	    controlsLocked = locked;
562	    controlsEl.innerHTML = `
563	      <span class="control-item">
564	        <span class="control-label">To move</span>
565	        <span class="arrow-keys" aria-label="Arrow keys">
566	          <span class="key key-up">↑</span>
567	          <span class="key key-left">←</span>
568	          <span class="key key-down">↓</span>
569	          <span class="key key-right">→</span>
570	        </span>
571	      </span>
572	      ${locked ? `
573	        <span class="control-item">
574	          <span class="control-label">To look around</span>
575	          <span class="mouse-hint">
576	            <span class="mouse-icon" aria-hidden="true"></span>
577	            <span>move mouse</span>
578	          </span>
579	        </span>
580	      ` : ""}
581	      <span class="control-item">
582	        <span class="control-label">Send a subagent</span>
583	        <span class="key">E</span>
584	      </span>
585	      <span class="control-item">
586	        <span class="control-label">Bird's-eye view</span>
587	        <span class="key">M</span>
588	      </span>
589	      <span class="control-item">
590	        <span class="control-label">Return to orbit</span>
591	        <span class="key">B</span>
592	      </span>
593	    `;
594	    controlsEl.classList.remove("hidden");
595	  }
596	  function hideControls() {
597	    controlsLocked = null;
598	    controlsEl?.classList.add("hidden");
599	  }
600	
601	  // ---------- systems status panel ----------
602	  const STATE_LABEL = {
603	    broken: "BROKEN", working: "SUBAGENT AT WORK", sealed: "READY TO REVIEW", added: "ONLINE",
604	  };
605	  function updateSystems() {
606	    if (!systemsEl) return;
607	    systemsEl.innerHTML = sites.map((s) =>
608	      `<div class="db-sys is-${s.state}">` +
609	      `<span class="db-sys-name">${s.sys.name}</span>` +
610	      `<span class="db-sys-state">${STATE_LABEL[s.state]}</span></div>`
611	    ).join("");
612	  }
613	
614	  // ---------- level countdown ----------
615	  function fmtTime(s) {
616	    s = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(s));
617	    return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
618	  }
619	  function updateClock() {
620	    if (countdownTime) countdownTime.textContent = fmtTime(timeLeft);
621	    countdownEl?.classList.toggle("is-low", timerRunning && timeLeft <= LOW_TIME);
622	    countdownEl?.classList.toggle("is-critical", timerRunning && timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME);
623	  }
624	  function panicFactor() {
625	    if (failed) return 1;
626	    if (!timerRunning || timeLeft > PANIC_TIME) return 0;
627	    let p = (PANIC_TIME - timeLeft) / PANIC_TIME;
628	    p *= p;
629	    if (timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME) {
630	      const throb = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 85);
631	      p += 0.14 * throb;
632	    }
633	    return Math.min(1, p);
634	  }
635	  function applyPanicSky() {
636	    const p = reportSent ? 0 : panicFactor();
637	    scene.background.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(SKY_PANIC, p);
638	    scene.fog.color.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(FOG_PANIC, p);
639	    dome.material.color.copy(DOME_CALM).lerp(DOME_PANIC, p);
640	    sun.color.copy(SUN_CALM).lerp(SUN_PANIC, p * 0.85);
641	  }
642	
643	  // ---------- briefing / win / fail / reset ----------
644	  function showBriefing() {
645	    timerRunning = false;
646	    fp.detach();
647	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
648	    briefingEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
649	  }
650	  function startLevel() {
651	    if (started) return;
652	    started = true;
653	    briefingEl?.classList.add("hidden");
654	    fp.attach();
655	    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
656	    timerRunning = true;
657	    updateClock();
658	    showTutorial("Five systems are dark — the amber beams. Walk to one and press E: a subagent takes the job while you move on.", 8500);
659	  }
660	  briefingStartBtn?.addEventListener("click", startLevel);
661	
662	  function failLevel() {
663	    if (failed || reportSent) return;
664	    failed = true;
665	    timerRunning = false;
666	    setMap(false);
667	    closeTerminal(false);
668	    fp.detach();
669	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
670	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
671	    failEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
672	  }
673	  function resetLevel() {
674	    failed = false;
675	    reportSent = false;
676	    dismissedSite = null;
677	    reportDismissed = false;
678	    clearTimeout(winTimer);
679	    for (const s of sites) {
680	      s.state = "broken";
681	      s.workT = 0; s.meltT = 0;
682	      s.brokenModel.visible = true;
683	      s.upgradeModel.visible = false;
684	      const anim = s.upgradeModel.userData.anim;
685	      if (anim?.type === "spire") anim.beam.visible = false;
686	      s.ice.visible = false;
687	      s.ice.material.opacity = 0.62;
688	      s.sprite.visible = false;
689	    }
690	    for (let i = 0; i < drones.length; i++) {
691	      drones[i].state = "bay";
692	      drones[i].site = null;
693	      drones[i].model.position.copy(drones[i].home);
694	      workBeams[i].visible = false;
695	    }
696	    failEl?.classList.add("hidden");
697	    winEl?.classList.add("hidden");
698	    updateSystems();
699	    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
700	    timerRunning = true;
701	    updateClock();
702	    if (active) fp.attach();
703	    showTutorial("New attempt — the fleet works in parallel. Use all five subagents.", 6000);
704	  }
705	
706	  // ---------- drone dispatching ----------
707	  function assignDrone(site) {
708	    const drone = drones.find((d) => d.state === "bay");
709	    if (!drone) return;                 // shouldn't happen: one drone per system
710	    site.state = "working";
711	    site.workT = 0;
712	    drone.state = "flying";
713	    drone.site = site;
714	    drone.from.copy(drone.model.position);
715	    drone.to.set(site.anchor.position.x, site.anchor.position.y + 6.5, site.anchor.position.z);
716	    drone.t = 0;
717	    drone.dur = Math.max(1, drone.from.distanceTo(drone.to) / DRONE_SPEED);
718	    updateSystems();
719	    teachOnce("assigned",
720	      "The subagent's got it — you're free. Send the others while it works; the clock won't wait.", 7000);
721	  }
722	  function sealSite(site) {
723	    site.state = "sealed";
724	    site.brokenModel.visible = false;
725	    site.upgradeModel.visible = true;   // built — but dormant until ADD TO SHIP
726	    site.ice.visible = true;
727	    site.ice.material.opacity = 0.62;
728	    site.sprite.visible = true;
729	    updateSystems();
730	    teachOnce("sealed",
731	      "A subagent finished while you were away — its work is sealed under a checkpoint. Walk up and see what it actually did.", 8000);
732	  }
733	
734	  // ---------- terminal ----------
735	  function flashTerminal(text, ok) {
736	    if (!termMsg) return;
737	    clearTimeout(msgTimer);
738	    termMsg.textContent = text;
739	    termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
740	    termMsg.classList.add(ok ? "show-ok" : "show-err");
741	    msgTimer = setTimeout(() => termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err"), 3600);
742	  }
743	  function renderTerminal() {
744	    if (!terminalOpen) return;
745	    if (termMode === "review") {
746	      if (!explained) {
747	        // The command comes pre-filled — you never type a checkpoint id.
748	        termHint.textContent = "# what did the subagent actually do? ask the checkpoint";
749	        termInput.textContent = `entire checkpoint explain ${reviewSite.sys.ckpt}`;
750	        termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
751	        if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
752	          `<span class="cta-label">PRESS</span>` +
753	          `<kbd class="cta-key cta-key-yes">Enter</kbd><span class="cta-note">run it</span>`;
754	      } else {
755	        termHint.textContent = "# reviewed — your call";
756	        termInput.textContent = `entire checkpoint explain ${reviewSite.sys.ckpt}`;
757	        termInput.classList.add("is-dim");
758	        if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
759	          `<button id="db-add-btn" type="button">ADD TO SHIP</button>` +
760	          `<span class="cta-note">or press <kbd class="cta-key cta-key-yes">Y</kbd></span>`;
761	      }
762	    } else if (termMode === "report") {
763	      termHint.textContent = reportSent
764	        ? "# dispatch sent — the day is on the record"
765	        : "# five jobs accepted — send the day's report";
766	      termInput.textContent = buffer;
767	      termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
768	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML = reportSent ? "" :
769	        `<span class="cta-label">TYPE</span><span class="cta-cmd">entire dispatch</span>`;
770	    }
771	  }
772	  function openTerminal(mode, site) {
773	    if (terminalOpen) return;
774	    terminalOpen = true;
775	    termMode = mode;
776	    reviewSite = site || null;
777	    explained = false;
778	    buffer = "";
779	    fp.detach();
780	    termMsg?.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
781	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
782	    termEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
783	    renderTerminal();
784	  }
785	  function closeTerminal(dismiss) {
786	    if (!terminalOpen) return;
787	    if (dismiss) {
788	      if (termMode === "review" && reviewSite) dismissedSite = reviewSite;
789	      if (termMode === "report") reportDismissed = true;
790	    }
791	    terminalOpen = false;
792	    termMode = null;
793	    reviewSite = null;
794	    explained = false;
795	    buffer = "";
796	    termEl?.classList.add("hidden");
797	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
798	    if (active && !failed) fp.attach();
799	  }
800	
801	  // ---------- entire checkpoint explain (pre-filled, Enter to run) ----------
802	  function runExplain() {
803	    if (!reviewSite || explained) return;
804	    explained = true;
805	    const s = reviewSite;
806	    if (termList) {
807	      termList.innerHTML =
808	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">checkpoint</span>` +
809	        `<span class="tl-title">${s.sys.ckpt}</span></div>` +
810	        s.sys.card.map(([k, v]) =>
811	          `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">${k}</span>` +
812	          `<span class="tl-title">${v}</span></div>`
813	        ).join("");
814	      termList.classList.remove("hidden");
815	    }
816	    flashTerminal(`explained ${s.sys.ckpt} — ${s.sys.name.toLowerCase()} is now a ${s.sys.became}`, true);
817	    teachOnce("explained",
818	      "That's the story behind the checkpoint — work you didn't watch is never a mystery. If it's good, add it to the ship.", 8000);
819	    renderTerminal();
820	  }
821	
822	  // ---------- ADD TO SHIP ----------
823	  function addToShip() {
824	    if (!reviewSite || !explained) return;
825	    const s = reviewSite;
826	    s.state = "added";
827	    s.meltT = MELT_DUR;                 // the seal melts; the upgrade wakes up
828	    const anim = s.upgradeModel.userData.anim;
829	    if (anim?.type === "spire") anim.beam.visible = true;
830	    updateSystems();
831	    if (allAdded()) {
832	      // The fifth accept doesn't close the terminal — the finish line is the
833	      // next command at the prompt you're already at, like the real CLI.
834	      termMode = "report";
835	      reviewSite = null;
836	      explained = false;
837	      buffer = "";
838	      termList?.classList.add("hidden");
839	      flashTerminal("all five systems online", true);
840	      showTutorial("Every system accounted for — send the day's report: type `entire dispatch`.", 0);
841	      renderTerminal();
842	    } else {
843	      closeTerminal(false);
844	      showTutorial(`${s.sys.name} online (${addedCount()} / ${sites.length}).`, 5000);
845	    }
846	  }
847	  // The ADD TO SHIP button (terminal CTA is re-rendered, so delegate the click).
848	  termCta?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
849	    if (!active) return;
850	    if (e.target?.id === "db-add-btn") addToShip();
851	  });
852	
853	  // ---------- the finish line: entire dispatch ----------
854	  // "Generate a dispatch summarizing recent agent work" — five drones just
855	  // worked; the report writes itself from their checkpoints.
856	  function sendDispatch() {
857	    reportSent = true;
858	    timerRunning = false;
859	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
860	    if (termList) {
861	      termList.innerHTML =
862	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">DISPATCH</span>` +
863	        `<span class="tl-title">drone bay — day report</span></div>` +
864	        sites.map((s) =>
865	          `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">·</span>` +
866	          `<span class="tl-title">${s.sys.name.toLowerCase()} → ${s.sys.became} (${s.sys.ckpt.slice(0, 6)}…)</span></div>`
867	        ).join("") +
868	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">filed</span>` +
869	        `<span class="tl-title">from 5 checkpoints · crew: 1 human, 5 subagents</span></div>`;
870	      termList.classList.remove("hidden");
871	    }
872	    flashTerminal("dispatch sent — look how much got done without you", true);
873	    renderTerminal();
874	    if (winSub) {
875	      winSub.textContent = "five jobs in parallel — the report wrote itself";
876	    }
877	    winTimer = setTimeout(() => {
878	      closeTerminal(false);
879	      tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
880	      winEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
881	    }, 4000);
882	    onComplete?.();               // Level 2 cleared — opens the launch window
883	  }
884	  function submitCommand() {
885	    const n = normalizeCmd(buffer);
886	    buffer = "";
887	    if (!n) { renderTerminal(); return; }
888	    if (/^entire dispatch$/.test(n)) { sendDispatch(); return; }
889	    if (/^entire (checkpoint|cp) list$/.test(n)) {
890	      // L1's command still works — the raw log, then the nudge to report.
891	      if (termList) {
892	        termList.innerHTML = sites.map((s) =>
893	          `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-id tl-id-short">${s.sys.ckpt}</span>` +
894	          `<span class="tl-title">subagent fix: ${s.sys.name.toLowerCase()} → ${s.sys.became}</span></div>`
895	        ).join("");
896	        termList.classList.remove("hidden");
897	      }
898	      flashTerminal("the raw log — now turn it into the day's report:  entire dispatch", true);
899	      renderTerminal();
900	      return;
901	    }
902	    flashTerminal("command not recognized — try:  entire dispatch", false);
903	    renderTerminal();
904	  }
905	
906	  // ---------- input ----------
907	  function onCanvasClick() {
908	    if (!started || failed) return;
909	    if (active && !fp.isLocked && !terminalOpen) fp.lock();
910	  }
911	  function onKeyDown(e) {
912	    if (!active) return;
913	
914	    // Briefing is up — Enter/Space begins the run, nothing else.
915	    if (!started) {
916	      if (e.code === "Enter" || e.code === "Space") { startLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }
917	      return;
918	    }
919	
920	    // The clock ran out — only R (retry) does anything.
921	    if (failed) {
922	      if (e.code === "KeyR") { resetLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }
923	      return;
924	    }
925	
926	    // While the terminal is open, all keys feed it.
927	    if (terminalOpen) {
928	      if (e.code === "Escape") { closeTerminal(true); e.preventDefault(); return; }
929	      if (termMode === "review") {
930	        if (!explained && e.code === "Enter") { runExplain(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
931	        if (explained && (e.key === "y" || e.key === "Y")) { addToShip(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
932	        e.preventDefault();
933	        return;
934	      }
935	      // report mode — type the dispatch command (the finish line)
936	      if (reportSent) { e.preventDefault(); return; }  // only Esc closes the report
937	      if (e.code === "Enter") { submitCommand(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
938	      if (e.code === "Backspace") { buffer = buffer.slice(0, -1); renderTerminal(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
939	      if (e.key && e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {
940	        buffer += e.key;
941	        renderTerminal();
942	        e.preventDefault();
943	      }
944	      return;
945	    }
946	
947	    // Walking around.
948	    // Level cleared → Enter boards the ship; failure never advances (R only).
949	    if (reportSent && e.code === "Enter") { onNext?.(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
950	    if (e.code === "KeyE" && nearSite?.state === "broken") { assignDrone(nearSite); e.preventDefault(); return; }
951	    if (e.code === "KeyM") { setMap(!overhead.on); e.preventDefault(); return; }
952	    if (e.code === "Escape" && fp.isLocked) fp.unlock();
953	    if (e.code === "KeyB") onExit?.();
954	  }
955	  fp.controls.addEventListener("lock", () => crosshair?.classList.remove("hidden"));
956	  fp.controls.addEventListener("unlock", () => crosshair?.classList.add("hidden"));
957	
958	  // ---------- HUD per-frame ----------
959	  let nearSite = null;
960	  let nearConsole = false;
961	
962	  function refreshHud() {
963	    if (!started || terminalOpen) {
964	      setPrompt(null);
965	      hideControls();
966	      return;
967	    }
968	    setControls(fp.isLocked);
969	    if (overhead.on) { setPrompt("Bird's-eye view — ↑↓←→ to move · M to return"); return; }
970	    if (!fp.isLocked) { setPrompt("Click to look around"); return; }
971	    if (reportSent) { setPrompt("The day is dispatched — press Enter to board the ship"); return; }
972	    if (nearSite) {
973	      if (nearSite.state === "broken") setPrompt(`${nearSite.sys.name} is down — press E to send a subagent`);
974	      else if (nearSite.state === "working") setPrompt("Subagent at work — you're free, go assign another");
975	      else if (nearSite.state === "added" && !allAdded()) setPrompt(`${nearSite.sys.became} online — accepted into the ship`);
976	      return;
977	    }
978	    if (allAdded()) { setPrompt("Walk to any online system — send the day's report: `entire dispatch`"); return; }
979	    if (sites.some((s) => s.state === "sealed")) {
980	      setPrompt("An ice-blue beam means finished work — go review it");
981	      return;
982	    }
983	    if (sites.some((s) => s.state === "broken")) {
984	      setPrompt("Amber beams are broken systems — send the subagents (E)");
985	      return;
986	    }
987	    setPrompt("The fleet is working — wait for an ice-blue beam, then go review");
988	  }

990	  // ---------- per-frame ----------
991	  function update(dt, t) {
992	    fp.update(dt);
993	
994	    // Level countdown — one clock for the whole run.
995	    if (active && timerRunning && !failed && !reportSent) {
996	      timeLeft = Math.max(0, timeLeft - dt);
997	      updateClock();
998	      if (timeLeft <= 0) failLevel();
999	    }
1000	    applyPanicSky();
1001	
1002	    // Drones fly, work, and head home.
1003	    for (let i = 0; i < drones.length; i++) {
1004	      const d = drones[i];
1005	      const beam = workBeams[i];
1006	      if (d.state === "bay") {
1007	        d.model.position.y = d.home.y + Math.sin((t + d.bobOff) * 2) * 0.15;
1008	        d.model.userData.ring.rotation.z += dt * 1.2;
1009	      } else if (d.state === "flying" || d.state === "returning") {
1010	        d.t += dt;
1011	        const k = Math.min(1, d.t / d.dur);
1012	        const ease = k * k * (3 - 2 * k);
1013	        d.model.position.lerpVectors(d.from, d.to, ease);
1014	        d.model.position.y += Math.sin(k * Math.PI) * 3;   // arc over the island
1015	        d.model.userData.ring.rotation.z += dt * 6;
1016	        if (k >= 1) {
1017	          if (d.state === "flying") d.state = "working";
1018	          else { d.state = "bay"; d.model.position.copy(d.home); }
1019	        }
1020	      } else if (d.state === "working") {
1021	        const s = d.site;
1022	        d.model.position.set(
1023	          s.anchor.position.x + Math.cos(t * 1.4) * 1.2,
1024	          s.anchor.position.y + 6.5 + Math.sin(t * 2.2) * 0.4,
1025	          s.anchor.position.z + Math.sin(t * 1.4) * 1.2
1026	        );
1027	        d.model.userData.ring.rotation.z += dt * 9;
1028	        beam.visible = true;
1029	        beam.position.set(d.model.position.x, d.model.position.y - 3.2, d.model.position.z);
1030	        beam.material.opacity = 0.35 + Math.sin(t * 11) * 0.2;
1031	
1032	        // The actual work happens whether you watch or not.
1033	        if (active && started && !failed) {
1034	          s.workT += dt;
1035	          if (s.workT >= WORK_TIME) {
1036	            sealSite(s);
1037	            beam.visible = false;
1038	            d.state = "returning";
1039	            d.site = null;
1040	            d.from.copy(d.model.position);
1041	            d.to.copy(d.home);
1042	            d.t = 0;
1043	            d.dur = Math.max(1, d.from.distanceTo(d.to) / DRONE_SPEED);
1044	          }
1045	        }
1046	      }
1047	    }
1048	
1049	    // Sites: status beams, warning blinks, seals, and live upgrades.
1050	    for (const s of sites) {
1051	      if (s.state === "broken") {
1052	        s.beam.material.color.setHex(0xff8a5c);
1053	        s.beam.material.opacity = 0.24 + Math.sin(t * 1.6 + s.idx) * 0.08;
1054	        s.beam.visible = true;
1055	        const warn = s.brokenModel.userData.warn;
1056	        if (warn) warn.material.emissiveIntensity = Math.sin(t * 5 + s.idx) > 0 ? 1.8 : 0.2;
1057	      } else if (s.state === "working") {
1058	        s.beam.material.color.setHex(0x6fe3ff);
1059	        s.beam.material.opacity = 0.4 + Math.sin(t * 6) * 0.18;
1060	        s.beam.visible = true;
1061	      } else if (s.state === "sealed") {
1062	        s.beam.material.color.setHex(0x8fe3ff);
1063	        s.beam.material.opacity = 0.42;
1064	        s.beam.visible = true;
1065	      } else {
1066	        s.beam.visible = false;       // added — the upgrade itself is the landmark
1067	        if (s.meltT > 0) {            // the checkpoint seal melts away
1068	          s.meltT = Math.max(0, s.meltT - dt);
1069	          s.ice.material.opacity = 0.62 * (s.meltT / MELT_DUR);
1070	          if (s.meltT <= 0) s.ice.visible = false;
1071	        }
1072	        // The improvised upgrades live a little.
1073	        const anim = s.upgradeModel.userData.anim;
1074	        if (anim?.type === "ring") {
1075	          anim.ring.rotation.y += dt * 0.8;
1076	          anim.ring.position.y = 3.4 + Math.sin(t * 1.6) * 0.2;
1077	        } else if (anim?.type === "dome") {
1078	          anim.field.rotation.y += dt * 0.25;
1079	          anim.orbit.rotation.z += dt * 0.6;
1080	        } else if (anim?.type === "skid") {
1081	          anim.pad.position.y = 1.6 + Math.sin(t * 1.8) * 0.25;
1082	        }
1083	      }
1084	    }
1085	
1086	    // Proximity: nearest site, and the ship console.
1087	    nearSite = null;
1088	    let nd = Infinity;
1089	    for (const s of sites) {
1090	      const d = Math.hypot(
1091	        camera.position.x - s.anchor.position.x,
1092	        camera.position.z - s.anchor.position.z
1093	      );
1094	      if (d < nd) { nd = d; if (d <= INTERACT_DIST) nearSite = s; }
1095	    }
1096	    nearConsole = Math.hypot(
1097	      camera.position.x - CONSOLE_POS.x, camera.position.z - CONSOLE_POS.z
1098	    ) <= CONSOLE_DIST;
1099	    consoleGlow.intensity = allAdded() && !reportSent ? 8 + Math.sin(t * 4) * 3 : 5;
1100	
1101	    // Terminals open themselves at the right places, like Level 1. After the
1102	    // fifth accept the report prompt is already open in place; if the player
1103	    // wanders off, any ONLINE system (or the bay console) reopens it.
1104	    const atReportSpot = nearConsole || nearSite?.state === "added";
1105	    if (active && started && !failed) {
1106	      if (dismissedSite && nearSite !== dismissedSite) dismissedSite = null;
1107	      if (reportDismissed && !atReportSpot) reportDismissed = false;
1108	      if (!terminalOpen) {
1109	        if (nearSite?.state === "sealed" && nearSite !== dismissedSite) {
1110	          openTerminal("review", nearSite);
1111	        } else if (atReportSpot && allAdded() && !reportSent && !reportDismissed) {
1112	          openTerminal("report", null);
1113	        }
1114	      } else if (termMode === "review" &&
1115	                 (nearSite !== reviewSite || reviewSite.state !== "sealed")) {
1116	        // walked away mid-review (ADD closes it itself)
1117	        if (nearSite !== reviewSite) closeTerminal(false);
1118	      } else if (termMode === "report" && !atReportSpot && !reportSent) {
1119	        closeTerminal(false);
1120	      }
1121	    }
1122	
1123	    overhead.update(t);
1124	    if (active) refreshHud();
1125	  }
1126	
1127	  // ---------- lifecycle ----------
1128	  function enter() {
1129	    active = true;
1130	    fp.groundAt(0, terrain.radius * 0.75);
1131	    camera.lookAt(CONSOLE_POS.x, terrain.heightAt(CONSOLE_POS.x, CONSOLE_POS.z) + 5, CONSOLE_POS.z);
1132	    canvas.addEventListener("click", onCanvasClick);
1133	    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
1134	    dbHud?.classList.remove("hidden");
1135	    fpShared?.classList.remove("hidden");
1136	    setControls(false);
1137	    crosshair?.classList.add("hidden");
1138	    updateSystems();
1139	    if (!started) {
1140	      showBriefing();
1141	    } else if (reportSent) {
1142	      fp.attach();
1143	      winEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
1144	    } else if (failed) {
1145	      resetLevel();                 // came back after a wipe → fresh run
1146	    } else {
1147	      fp.attach();
1148	      timerRunning = true;          // resume the level clock
1149	      showTutorial("The subagents kept working while you were in orbit.", 5000);
1150	    }
1151	    updateClock();
1152	    applyPanicSky();
1153	  }
1154	  function exit() {
1155	    active = false;

> AGENT

I read through `droneBayView.js` end to end. Before throwing ideas out, here's the thing that jumps at me:

**You already solved this exact problem in Level 1.** The L1 rebuild ditched "walk around to items" for "stand in one place, things come to you, act from a fixed spot." L2 still has you trekking first-person across six far-flung coordinates `(-34,-28)`, `(40,-18)`, `(28,38)`… on bumpy terrain, under a clock. That's the navigation pain. The fix is the same move that already worked once.

And here's the lucky part: the subagent fiction *wants* you to stay put. Real delegation means **you don't go to the work — the work goes away from you.** So pinning the player down isn't a compromise, it's the metaphor.

## The core idea: Mission Control

You never leave the ship console. The console *is* the level — it's the "one dialogue-box channel" from your Bug Bash simplicity bar. The island becomes a thing you *watch*, not a maze you cross.

- **Dispatch from one spot.** A list of 5 dark systems at the console. Pick one, press E → a drone launches and flies off across the island. No walking. You can fire off all five in seconds.
- **The work happens *over there*, out of reach.** You literally watch drones working at distant beams you can't get to. That's the "behind the scenes" feeling made physical — you see *that* something's happening, not *what*.
- **The reveal comes home to you.** Drone returns with a sealed checkpoint. `entire checkpoint explain` runs right at the console. The recovery is the payoff, in your hands, where you already are.

## The piece I think you're actually reaching for: the *blind* reveal

You said the heart of it is "you don't know what they did until Entire recovers it." But the current code **shows the finished upgrade the moment it seals** (`upgradeModel.visible = true` on `sealSite`) — you can squint across the island and basically see the plasma ring before you ever run explain. That undercuts the whole emotional beat.

What if the returned work is genuinely **shrouded** — iced over, a featureless sealed pod — and `checkpoint explain` is the *only* thing that unwraps it? The drone comes back carrying a mystery box. Explain is the reveal. ADD TO SHIP is you accepting it. That turns explain from "extra step" into the dramatic high point — which is the lesson you're trying to teach.

Mission Control kills the walking; the blind reveal makes explain *matter*. They're independent, but together they're the level you're describing.

A lighter-touch alternative, if you don't want to rebuild the layout: keep the island but **dispatch from the overhead map** (M already exists) — click systems on the bird's-eye view to send drones, and only walk in to do reviews. Less radical, but it still leaves some trekking.

A couple of things I want to pin down before sketching either one:

> AGENT

Got it — you're not asking "what layout," you're asking "what's the *verb*." L1's verb is **shoot**. L2 needs its own verb that *is* delegation — and yeah, the work being hidden until `explain` recovers it isn't a question, that's just the point of the level. So let me think in mechanics, not maps.

Here are genuinely different ways to frame the L2 verb:

**1. Launch & forget — shooting, inverted.**
Same kinetic feel as L1, flipped in meaning. In L1 you shoot to *bank what's yours*. Here you aim at a dark system and **fire a drone out** — you launch work *away*. You can spray all five out fast (that's the parallelism). Then you can't watch them. They come back as sealed checkpoints, and the *second* verb is cracking one open = `explain`. Shares L1's muscle memory, opposite meaning. Clean contrast to teach against.

**2. Catch the returns — make the recovery the game.**
This one leans hardest into your core ("you don't know what they did until Entire recovers it"). Sending is trivial — one button, drones swarm out. The *game* is what flies **back at you**, exactly like L1's falling records: sealed checkpoints come home and you have to `explain` each one to see what the subagent improvised, then decide keep/toss. The verb is *recovering under pressure*. The thing you said matters most literally becomes the gameplay.

**3. Dispatch board — juggling, not aiming.**
No aim at all. A control panel: systems on one side, a pool of drones. The fun is parallel juggling — fire jobs off, they return needing review, and you're explaining-and-accepting while still dispatching. This is the **Diner Dash** loop from L1's original design, reborn as air-traffic control. Skill = timing and overlap, which is *exactly* the real feeling of running multiple agents.

My lean: **#2**, with a dash of #1's launch. Because the whole reason this level exists is the recover-what-you-didn't-watch beat — so make *that* the thing the player's hands are doing, not a menu step after a walk. Sending should be a quick flourish; the drama is the return.

One thing that'd help me push the right one further — when you picture the *fun moment* in L2, is it the **chaos of sending a swarm out** (#1/#3), or the **payoff of cracking open work you didn't see** (#2)?

Let me see what the player is actually staring at during that moment. Right now, when you're typing `git add`, the terminal stacks **four** text channels:

1. `#term-hint` — `# ADD` (12.5px, dim)
2. `#term-line` — `crashlog:~$ git add▮` (16px) ← the thing you type
3. `#term-cta` — `TYPE  git add` (the command shown *again*)
4. `#term-msg` — feedback (11.5px)

So the screen literally shows "git add" **twice** plus "ADD" a third time — and the biggest font in there is only 16px, behind an 11-character `crashlog:~$` prompt that eats the left third of the line before your command even starts. That's your "too small + too crowded": the hero (the command you type) is small, and it's drowned by redundant chrome saying the same thing three ways.

The fix is really two moves: **make the typed command the biggest thing in the box**, and **kill the duplication** around it. Here are three ways to land that:

I have a complete picture. Here is my report.

---

# Level 1 END/WIN Screen — Investigation & Redesign Proposal

## 1. There is no single "results container" for the Level 1 win

This is the key finding. Unlike Levels 2 and 3, **Level 1's win has no dedicated win card.** When the player clears the minimum, `passRun()` (`src/islandView.js:715-720`) does NOT open a results panel. Instead it stitches together three existing HUD elements:

1. **`#tutorial`** — a thin top banner with the line: `"Time. ${banked} records recovered. Run \`entire checkpoint list\` to review your haul."` (`islandView.js:719`)
2. **`#terminal`** (bottom) — after the player types `entire checkpoint list`, `showCheckpointList()` (`islandView.js:686-702`) fills `#term-list` with one `.term-list-row` per record (`<span class="tl-id"><span class="tl-key">Entire-Checkpoint:</span> ID</span><span class="tl-title">recovered: TITLE</span>`) and flashes `#term-msg`: `"${banked} checkpoints linked · ship memory restored"`.
3. **`#level-mode-prompt`** in handoff mode — `showModePrompt("next")` shows the title-screen-style card with head `"LEVEL 1 COMPLETE"`, action button `"START LEVEL 2"`, note `"Ship memory restored — the drone bay just woke up."` (`MODE_PROMPTS.next`, `islandView.js:78-83`).

So "the end screen text is too small" almost certainly refers to the **terminal haul list at the bottom** and/or the **tutorial line at the top** — both are genuinely tiny. The big readable element is only the mode-prompt button.

The HTML markup lives at `index.html:131` (`#tutorial`), `index.html:138-147` (`#terminal` block), and `index.html:173-179` (`#level-mode-prompt`).

The separate **fail** screen IS a real card: `#level-fail` (`index.html:207-211`), populated by `failRun()` (`islandView.js:721-728`) with title `"NOT ENOUGH MEMORY"` and sub `"You recovered ${banked}. The ship needs at least ${MIN_TO_PASS} to power up."`.

## 2. Current sizes / layout (real values)

Color tokens (`style.css:2-4`): `--accent: #6fe3ff` (cyan), `--accent-warm: #ffb86b`, `--ink: #e8f4ff`.

**`#tutorial`** (`style.css:1286-1304`): top-anchored at `top: 132px`, `max-width: min(560px, 100vw-48px)`, `padding: 10px 18px`, **`font-size: 12.5px`**, `line-height: 1.5`, faint `rgba(8,14,26,0.62)` bg. (Mobile override: `11.5px`, `style.css:1850-1854`.)

**`#terminal`** (`style.css:1338-1352`): bottom-anchored `bottom: 70px`, `width: min(560px, 100vw-40px)`, `padding: 14px 16px`, cyan border.
- `#term-line` prompt: `16px` (`style.css:1362-1367`)
- `#term-hint`: `12.5px` (`style.css:1354`)
- `#term-list`: `max-height: 168px`, scrolls; rows `.term-list-row` are **`font-size: 12.5px`** (`style.css:1465-1469`); `.tl-id min-width: 300px`
- `#term-msg` (the "ship memory restored" line): **`font-size: 11.5px`**, `opacity: 0.92` (`style.css:1490-1499`) — this is the smallest, faintest text on the win screen.

**`#level-mode-prompt`** (`style.css:803-865`): full-screen overlay. In `.is-handoff` mode the menu is top-anchored (`margin-top: clamp(48px,12vh,150px)`), gradient dims downward so the haul list stays visible.
- Head "LEVEL 1 COMPLETE": `clamp(12px, 1.3vw, 15px)`, gold `#f6c763` (`style.css:841-848`)
- Button "START LEVEL 2": `.level-mode-menu .ts-item` → `clamp(19px, 3vw, 30px)` (`style.css:852-856`) — the one large element
- Note: `clamp(12px, 1.4vw, 15px)` (`style.css:858-864`)

So the actual "you recovered N records" content is all rendered at **11.5–12.5px**, in a half-transparent bottom terminal box capped at 560px wide — that is the cramped/small feeling.

## 3. Sharing analysis (important — what's safe to touch)

- **`#tutorial`** — SHARED. It is the single "dialogue-box channel" used everywhere in both island levels for teaching lines (`showTutorial()` is called all over `islandView.js`, e.g. lines 719, 973). Changing its font/size affects every tutorial beat, not just the win line.
- **`#terminal` / `#term-list` / `.term-list-row` / `.tl-*` / `#term-msg`** — HEAVILY SHARED. Same terminal is used for the `git add` / `git commit` / link-checkpoint banking steps during normal play, AND it is reused by Level 2's Drone Bay / Archive views (`.tl-id-short` at `style.css:1476`, `.tl-exp-key` at `style.css:1478` are L2-specific row variants). Bumping `.term-list-row` font size changes the in-play terminal and L2 too.
- **`#level-mode-prompt` / `.level-mode-*` / `.ts-item`** — SHARED within L1 (used for tutorial start, level start, and the handoff) and `.ts-item` is the title-screen menu button style used across the whole game (`style.css:120`). The `.is-handoff` variant (`style.css:818-830`) is win-specific, though.
- **`#level-fail`** — SHARED selector group with `#l2-fail, #db-fail, #lc-fail` (`style.css:1115-1118`); `.lf-*` classes are L1-only in markup but the box rule is the shared red fail-card style.
- **`#l2-win, #db-win, #lc-win` + `.lw-title`/`.lw-sub`** (`style.css:1239-1268`) — the green win-card pattern that Levels 2 and 3 DO have but Level 1 does NOT. This is the natural template to copy.

Bottom line: almost everything currently composing the L1 win is shared, so the cleanest fix is to **add a dedicated L1 win card** rather than enlarge the shared terminal/tutorial text.

## 4. Redesign proposals

### Proposal A (recommended): Give Level 1 a real win card, matching L2/L3
Level 1 is the only level without its own win panel — it improvises one from shared parts, which is exactly why it reads small. Add an `#island-win` card modeled on the existing `#l2-win/#db-win/#lc-win` green card (`style.css:1239-1268`):
- Box: `width: min(520px, 88vw)`, `padding: 30px 32px`, green border/glow (reuse the `.lw-*` look), centered.
- Title `.lw-title`-style at **24-28px** (vs current 11.5px): `"SHIP MEMORY RESTORED"`.
- Sub at **15-16px** monospace cyan: `"You recovered N records."` plus a small stat row.
- Move the recovered-records list INTO this card as a styled `.win-haul` block at **14px** rows (its own class, not shared `.term-list-row`), with the cyan `Entire-Checkpoint:` IDs.
- Footer button reuses `.ts-item` (already large) → `START LEVEL 2`.
This keeps the win self-contained and readable, costs nothing to the in-play terminal, and makes L1 consistent with L2/L3. It does change the current "keep terminal list visible behind a handoff card" choreography — worth confirming with the user since that layered look was a deliberate design (memory note: keep the haul list visible behind the card).

### Proposal B (lowest risk): Win-only size overrides via the `.is-handoff` scope
Keep the existing three-part composition but scope larger type to the win state only, so nothing else changes. Since the handoff adds `.level-mode-prompt.is-handoff`, but that's a separate element from the terminal, instead add a body/HUD state class (e.g. `#island-hud.is-review`) toggled in `passRun()`/`showCheckpointList()` and write:
- `#island-hud.is-review .term-list-row { font-size: 15px; gap: 16px; }`
- `#island-hud.is-review #term-msg { font-size: 14px; }`
- `#island-hud.is-review #terminal { width: min(680px, 100vw-40px); padding: 20px 24px; }`
- bump the `#tutorial` win line by scoping similarly.
Pro: minimal, preserves the layered look. Con: still a terminal box, still bottom-anchored and semi-transparent; it's "less small" but not "something better."

### Proposal C (middle ground): Promote the haul list into a centered "manifest" panel
Replace the bottom-terminal haul display on win with a centered, opaque CRT "MISSION RECORDS" manifest (its own element), while keeping the `#level-mode-prompt` button below or inside it:
- Centered panel `width: min(560px, 90vw)`, solid `rgba(4,10,18,0.95)`, cyan border + scanline texture to match the CRT frame.
- Header **22px** cyan `"MISSION RECORDS RECOVERED — N"`.
- Record rows at **15px**, generous `gap: 8px`, each with a small gold record glyph.
- `START LEVEL 2` button (`.ts-item`) docked at the bottom of the panel.
This directly answers "maybe it should be something else entirely" — it reframes the haul as a proud end-of-mission manifest rather than terminal output, fits the sci-fi/CRT language, and uses bigger type throughout. More work than B, less templated than A.

**My recommendation:** Proposal A for consistency with the rest of the game, or Proposal C if the user wants the win to feel distinct and more celebratory. Either way, define new classes rather than enlarging the shared `#tutorial`, `#terminal`, and `.term-list-row` styles, since those are used during play and in Level 2.

> AGENT

Terminal change is done. Here's what Level 1's terminal now does:

- **Command line is the hero** — bumped 16px → **24px** (19px on mobile).
- **Prompt shrunk** `crashlog:~$` → `$`, so your command starts almost at the left edge instead of behind 11 characters of chrome.
- **Killed the duplication** — the cryptic `# ADD` hint is gone, and the duplicate `TYPE git add` chip is replaced by one quiet plain-language helper (`type to stage this record` / `…freeze it as a snapshot` / `…review your haul`). So `git add` now appears **once**, big, where you type it.

The bigger font carries into L2's terminal too (beneficial), and L2's review hints still work since they set real text.

---

Now the **end screen**, with the subagent's findings. The big discovery: **Level 1 has no real win screen.** L2 and L3 each have a proper win card; L1 fakes one by stitching together three shared pieces, all tiny:

- the **tutorial banner** at top (12.5px): *"Time. N records recovered…"*
- the **terminal haul list** at bottom (12.5px rows) + a *"ship memory restored"* line at **11.5px** — the smallest, faintest text on the screen
- the handoff button (the one big element)

That's exactly your complaint — small text in a cramped half-transparent terminal box. And the fix isn't "enlarge the terminal," because that terminal is shared with gameplay and L2; blowing it up there would wreck the typing UI. The right move is to give L1 its **own** win screen. Three ways to do it:

645	        lessonNarrating = true;

649	      bankState = "frozen";

652	        closeTerminal();
653	        lessonNarrating = true;

656	    }
657	  }
658	  function confirmCheckpoint(yes) {
659	    if (bankState !== "frozen") return;
660	    const teaching = lessonPaused;
661	    if (!yes) {
662	      clearBankPiece();
663	      banking = false;
664	      bankState = "done";

678	    // shatter the ice + dismiss the banked record with a little sparkle

682	    bankState = "done";

707	  // ---------- end of run ----------
708	  // The clock hit 0:00 — branch on whether the minimum was cleared.

710	    timerRunning = false;
711	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
712	    // a record mid-bank when time expired doesn't count
713	    banking = false;

> AGENT

Here's exactly what happens. Pressing **N** at *"Link this record to a checkpoint?"* runs `confirmCheckpoint(false)` (`islandView.js:658-671`):

```js
if (!yes) {
  clearBankPiece();   // removes the ice + record mesh from the scene
  banking = false;
  bankState = "done";
  closeTerminal();
  if (teaching) { finishBankLesson(); showModePrompt("level"); }
  return;
}
```

**In both modes, the shared outcome is:** the frozen record is **discarded** — `clearBankPiece()` deletes the ice block and the record mesh from the scene. `banked` is **not** incremented, nothing is pushed to `bankedRecords`, so **no checkpoint is created and you get no credit** toward your tally. The terminal closes. There's no feedback message — it's silent.

The only difference is what comes next:

- **Regular gameplay** (`teaching = false`): it just drops you back into the falling-records loop. You did `git add` + `git commit`, and then the record vanishes with nothing to show for it — no tally bump, no list entry.
- **Tutorial** (`teaching = true`): same discard, then `finishBankLesson()` + `showModePrompt("level")` fire, so the tutorial **completes and the real level starts anyway**. You can finish onboarding by declining — but you never actually witness a checkpoint getting linked, which is the one thing that step is meant to teach, and you enter the level at 0 banked.

A few things worth flagging, since this is probably why you're asking:

1. **N throws the whole record away.** That's harsh and also drifts from the real CLI — per the workflow, declining to link a checkpoint still keeps your commit; it just skips the link. Here the entire record evaporates.
2. **No feedback on N.** Y gets a sparkle; N gets nothing, so it's easy to not realize you just lost the record.
3. **The tutorial is completable by declining**, which lets a player skip the actual checkpoint-link payoff and still advance.

Want me to change any of this — e.g. make N keep the commit but skip only the link (count it, no checkpoint id), add a feedback line, or have the tutorial require **Y** so the lesson always lands?

> DEVELOPER

i want to throw the record aaway but yeah i dont like how there's no feedback on n and it's still completable..what are your ideas before you make any changes

> AGENT

Good — keep the toss, just make it *land* and stop the tutorial from being skippable. Two separate problems, so here are ideas for each.

## 1. Feedback when you press N (both modes)

Right now Y gets a cyan sparkle and N gets silence, so a discard feels identical to nothing happening. I'd give the discard its own distinct "loss" beat:

- **A visual that reads as *loss*, not success.** Instead of the bright cyan `spawnSpark`, the iced record **cracks and crumbles** — a dull gray/amber shatter as it's removed, clearly different from the cyan "linked!" sparkle. Uses the existing `spawnSpark` with a drab color, so it's cheap.
- **One short line, in the dialogue channel.** A brief `showTutorial("Record discarded — no checkpoint linked.")` (or amber term-msg held for a beat before the terminal closes). Keeps it on your single dialogue-box channel rather than inventing a new UI.

I'd do both — the crumble sells the feeling, the line says the consequence.

## 2. The tutorial shouldn't be completable by declining

The link step is the *one* thing onboarding exists to teach, and N currently lets you skip it and still start the level. Two ways to fix, depending on whether you want the tutorial to also *teach* the toss:

- **B — Rail it to Y (simplest).** During the tutorial only, N doesn't advance. Either hide the N key from the CTA entirely (one guided path), or keep it visible but pressing it just nudges: *"Press Y to link it — that's the whole point."* The player always experiences a successful link, then discovers declining for real in free play. Clean, on-rails, matches the 15-second-onboarding bar.
- **C — Teach the toss, then require the link.** N *works* in the tutorial: the record crumbles with the new feedback, the game says *"Gone — that's what skipping costs. Here's one more: this time, link it,"* spawns a fresh record, and won't let you out until you press Y. Teaches both the consequence *and* the link. More satisfying, but it's more flow plumbing (respawn a tutorial record and re-run the link step).

My lean: **feedback = both effects**, and **tutorial = B** for now — it's the smaller change and keeps onboarding tight; C is a nice upgrade later if you want the toss to be part of the lesson. But C is genuinely the better teacher if you don't mind the extra step.

Which way for the tutorial — rail it to Y (B), or teach the toss then require Y (C)?
