325	// The ship console the terminal lives in.
326	function makeConsole() {
327	  const g = new THREE.Group();
328	  const pedestal = new THREE.Mesh(
329	    new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.6, 4.6, 1.6),
330	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1c2230, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.35 })
331	  );
332	  pedestal.position.y = 2.3;
333	  const screen = new THREE.Mesh(
334	    new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2.1, 1.3),
335	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
336	      color: 0x0c2030, emissive: 0x2a8aa6, emissiveIntensity: 1.4, roughness: 0.3,
337	    })
338	  );
339	  screen.position.set(0, 4.0, 0.82);
340	  screen.rotation.x = -0.28;
341	  g.add(pedestal, screen);
342	  return g;
343	}
344	
345	export function createDroneBayView(renderer, { onExit, onComplete, onNext, onNewGame } = {}) {
346	  const canvas = renderer.domElement;
347	
348	  // ---------- scene & sky ----------
349	  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
350	  scene.background = SKY_CALM.clone();
351	  scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(SKY_CALM.clone(), 90, 320);
352	
353	  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(
354	    62, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 2000
355	  );
356	
357	  // ---------- lighting ----------
358	  scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xcdbcff, 0x3a2f5e, 0.7));
359	  const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(SUN_CALM, 1.5);
360	  sun.position.set(60, 90, 40);
361	  scene.add(sun);
362	  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x6a5a92, 0.3));
363	
364	  const dome = new THREE.Mesh(
365	    new THREE.SphereGeometry(900, 32, 16),
366	    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: DOME_CALM.clone(), side: THREE.BackSide, fog: false })
367	  );
368	  scene.add(dome);
369	
370	  // ---------- terrain (the same island) ----------
371	  const terrain = createTerrain({ size: 200, segments: 220, maxHeight: 26, seed: 1337 });
372	  scene.add(terrain.mesh, terrain.water);
373	
374	  const beamTex = makeBeamTexture();
375	
376	  // ---------- the five work sites ----------
377	  const sites = SYSTEMS.map((sys, idx) => {
378	    const anchor = new THREE.Group();
379	    anchor.position.set(sys.pos.x, terrain.heightAt(sys.pos.x, sys.pos.z), sys.pos.z);
380	
381	    const BUILD = {
382	      buildBrokenCoils, buildBrokenNav, buildBrokenDish, buildBrokenVent, buildBrokenStrut,
383	      buildPlasmaRing, buildStarDome, buildGardenPod, buildGravSkid,
384	      buildSignalSpire: () => buildSignalSpire(beamTex),
385	    };
386	    const brokenModel = BUILD[sys.broken]();
387	    const upgradeModel = BUILD[sys.upgrade]();
388	    upgradeModel.visible = false;
389	    anchor.add(brokenModel, upgradeModel);
390	
391	    // Status beam — readable from across the island:
392	    // amber = broken, cyan pulse = drone at work, ice-blue = sealed (review me).
393	    const beam = new THREE.Mesh(
394	      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.6, 0.6, 60, 12, 1, true),
395	      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
396	        map: beamTex, color: 0xff8a5c, transparent: true, opacity: 0.3,
397	        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide,
398	        fog: false,
399	      })
400	    );
401	    beam.position.y = 30;
402	    anchor.add(beam);
403	
404	    // The checkpoint seal: ice + floating trailer id, like Level 1's bank.
405	    const ice = makeIceBlock();
406	    ice.scale.setScalar(1.25);
407	    ice.position.y = 2.6;
408	    ice.visible = false;
409	    anchor.add(ice);
410	    const sprite = makeIdSprite(sys.ckpt);
411	    sprite.position.y = 8.4;
412	    sprite.visible = false;
413	    anchor.add(sprite);
414	
415	    scene.add(anchor);
416	    return {
417	      sys, idx, anchor, brokenModel, upgradeModel, beam, ice, sprite,
418	      state: "broken",      // broken → working → sealed → added
419	      workT: 0,
420	      meltT: 0,             // ice melt animation after ADD TO SHIP
421	    };
422	  });
423	
424	  // ---------- the drone bay: five parked drones by the console ----------
425	  const drones = SYSTEMS.map((_, i) => {
426	    const d = buildDrone();
427	    const x = BAY_ROW.x0 + i * BAY_ROW.dx;
428	    const home = new THREE.Vector3(x, terrain.heightAt(x, BAY_ROW.z) + 1.2, BAY_ROW.z);
429	    d.position.copy(home);
430	    scene.add(d);
431	    return {
432	      model: d, home,
433	      state: "bay",         // bay → flying → working → returning
434	      site: null,
435	      from: new THREE.Vector3(), to: new THREE.Vector3(),
436	      t: 0, dur: 1,
437	      bobOff: Math.random() * 10,
438	    };
439	  });
440	
441	  // Work beam shown under a working drone (one per drone, toggled).
442	  const workBeams = drones.map(() => {
443	    const m = new THREE.Mesh(
444	      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.25, 1.2, 6, 10, 1, true),
445	      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
446	        color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.5,
447	        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide,
448	      })
449	    );
450	    m.visible = false;
451	    scene.add(m);
452	    return m;
453	  });
454	
455	  // ---------- the ship console ----------
456	  const consoleAnchor = new THREE.Group();
457	  consoleAnchor.position.set(
458	    CONSOLE_POS.x, terrain.heightAt(CONSOLE_POS.x, CONSOLE_POS.z), CONSOLE_POS.z
459	  );
460	  consoleAnchor.add(makeConsole());
461	  const consoleGlow = new THREE.PointLight(0xffd27a, 5, 28, 2);
462	  consoleGlow.position.y = 5;
463	  consoleAnchor.add(consoleGlow);
464	  scene.add(consoleAnchor);
465	
466	  // ---------- first-person controller ----------
467	  const fp = createFirstPerson(camera, canvas, {
468	    heightAt: terrain.heightAt,
469	    radius: terrain.radius * 0.96,
470	    eyeHeight: 2.6,
471	    speed: 24,
472	  });
473	  scene.add(fp.controls.object);
474	
475	  // Bird's-eye map (M) — see all five beams at once, keep walking meanwhile.
476	  const overhead = createOverhead(scene, terrain, camera);
477	  function setMap(on) {
478	    if (overhead.on === on) return;
479	    overhead.set(on);
480	    fp.setAlwaysMove(on);                       // arrows work without pointer lock
481	    scene.fog.near = on ? 500 : 90;             // don't fog the map out
482	    scene.fog.far = on ? 1400 : 320;
483	    crosshair?.classList.toggle("hidden", on || !fp.isLocked);
484	  }
485	
486	  // ---------- HUD elements (shared FP set + this level's own) ----------
487	  const promptEl = document.getElementById("fp-prompt");
488	  const controlsEl = document.getElementById("fp-controls");
489	  const crosshair = document.getElementById("crosshair");
490	  const tutorialEl = document.getElementById("tutorial");
491	  const fpShared = document.getElementById("fp-shared");
492	  const termEl = document.getElementById("terminal");
493	  const termHint = document.getElementById("term-hint");
494	  const termInput = document.getElementById("term-input");
495	  const termList = document.getElementById("term-list");
496	  const termMsg = document.getElementById("term-msg");
497	  const termCta = document.getElementById("term-cta");
498	  const dbHud = document.getElementById("db-hud");
499	  const briefingEl = document.getElementById("db-briefing");
500	  const briefingStartBtn = document.getElementById("db-briefing-start");
501	  const countdownEl = document.getElementById("db-countdown");
502	  const countdownTime = document.getElementById("db-countdown-time");
503	  const systemsEl = document.getElementById("db-systems-rows");
504	  const winEl = document.getElementById("db-win");
505	  const winSub = document.getElementById("db-win-sub");
506	  const failEl = document.getElementById("db-fail");
507	
508	  let active = false;
509	  let started = false;
510	  let failed = false;
511	  let reportSent = false;       // `entire dispatch` has been run — the win
512	  let promptText = null;
513	  let controlsLocked = null;
514	  const taught = new Set();
515	  let tutorialTimer = null;
516	  let msgTimer = null;
517	  let winTimer = null;
518	
519	  // Level countdown — the single source of pressure for the whole run.
520	  let timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
521	  let timerRunning = false;
522	
523	  // Terminal state. Two modes share the one terminal:
524	  //  "review" — pre-filled `entire checkpoint explain <id>` at a sealed site
525	  //  "report" — type `entire dispatch` (the finish line). Opens in place when
526	  //             the FIFTH system is accepted; reopens at any online system or
527	  //             the bay console if the player wandered off first.
528	  let terminalOpen = false;
529	  let termMode = null;
530	  let reviewSite = null;        // the sealed site being reviewed
531	  let explained = false;        // Enter pressed — the card is showing
532	  let buffer = "";
533	  let dismissedSite = null;     // site the player Esc'd out of (until they leave)
534	  let reportDismissed = false;
535	
536	  const addedCount = () => sites.filter((s) => s.state === "added").length;
537	  const allAdded = () => addedCount() >= sites.length;
538	
539	  // ---------- HUD helpers ----------
540	  function setPrompt(text) {
541	    if (!promptEl || text === promptText) return;
542	    promptText = text;
543	    if (text) { promptEl.textContent = text; promptEl.classList.remove("hidden"); }
544	    else promptEl.classList.add("hidden");
545	  }
546	  function showTutorial(text, ms = 5500) {
547	    if (!tutorialEl) return;
548	    clearTimeout(tutorialTimer);
549	    tutorialEl.textContent = text;
550	    tutorialEl.classList.remove("hidden");
551	    if (ms > 0) tutorialTimer = setTimeout(() => tutorialEl.classList.add("hidden"), ms);
552	  }
553	  function teachOnce(key, text, ms) {
554	    if (taught.has(key)) return;
555	    taught.add(key);
556	    showTutorial(text, ms);
557	  }
558	  function setControls(locked) {
559	    if (!controlsEl) return;
560	    if (locked === controlsLocked && !controlsEl.classList.contains("hidden")) return;
561	    controlsLocked = locked;
562	    controlsEl.innerHTML = `
563	      <span class="control-item">
564	        <span class="control-label">To move</span>
565	        <span class="arrow-keys" aria-label="Arrow keys">
566	          <span class="key key-up">↑</span>
567	          <span class="key key-left">←</span>
568	          <span class="key key-down">↓</span>
569	          <span class="key key-right">→</span>
570	        </span>
571	      </span>
572	      ${locked ? `
573	        <span class="control-item">
574	          <span class="control-label">To look around</span>
575	          <span class="mouse-hint">
576	            <span class="mouse-icon" aria-hidden="true"></span>
577	            <span>move mouse</span>
578	          </span>
579	        </span>
580	      ` : ""}
581	      <span class="control-item">
582	        <span class="control-label">Send a subagent</span>
583	        <span class="key">E</span>
584	      </span>
585	      <span class="control-item">
586	        <span class="control-label">Bird's-eye view</span>
587	        <span class="key">M</span>
588	      </span>
589	      <span class="control-item">
590	        <span class="control-label">Return to orbit</span>
591	        <span class="key">B</span>
592	      </span>
593	    `;
594	    controlsEl.classList.remove("hidden");
595	  }
596	  function hideControls() {
597	    controlsLocked = null;
598	    controlsEl?.classList.add("hidden");
599	  }
600	
601	  // ---------- systems status panel ----------
602	  const STATE_LABEL = {
603	    broken: "BROKEN", working: "SUBAGENT AT WORK", sealed: "READY TO REVIEW", added: "ONLINE",
604	  };
605	  function updateSystems() {
606	    if (!systemsEl) return;
607	    systemsEl.innerHTML = sites.map((s) =>
608	      `<div class="db-sys is-${s.state}">` +
609	      `<span class="db-sys-name">${s.sys.name}</span>` +
610	      `<span class="db-sys-state">${STATE_LABEL[s.state]}</span></div>`
611	    ).join("");
612	  }
613	
614	  // ---------- level countdown ----------
615	  function fmtTime(s) {
616	    s = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(s));
617	    return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
618	  }
619	  function updateClock() {
620	    if (countdownTime) countdownTime.textContent = fmtTime(timeLeft);
621	    countdownEl?.classList.toggle("is-low", timerRunning && timeLeft <= LOW_TIME);
622	    countdownEl?.classList.toggle("is-critical", timerRunning && timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME);
623	  }
624	  function panicFactor() {
625	    if (failed) return 1;
626	    if (!timerRunning || timeLeft > PANIC_TIME) return 0;
627	    let p = (PANIC_TIME - timeLeft) / PANIC_TIME;
628	    p *= p;
629	    if (timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME) {
630	      const throb = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 85);
631	      p += 0.14 * throb;
632	    }
633	    return Math.min(1, p);
634	  }
635	  function applyPanicSky() {
636	    const p = reportSent ? 0 : panicFactor();
637	    scene.background.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(SKY_PANIC, p);
638	    scene.fog.color.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(FOG_PANIC, p);
639	    dome.material.color.copy(DOME_CALM).lerp(DOME_PANIC, p);
640	    sun.color.copy(SUN_CALM).lerp(SUN_PANIC, p * 0.85);
641	  }
642	
643	  // ---------- briefing / win / fail / reset ----------
644	  function showBriefing() {
645	    timerRunning = false;
646	    fp.detach();
647	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
648	    briefingEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
649	  }
650	  function startLevel() {
651	    if (started) return;
652	    started = true;
653	    briefingEl?.classList.add("hidden");
654	    fp.attach();
655	    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
656	    timerRunning = true;
657	    updateClock();
658	    showTutorial("Five systems are dark — the amber beams. Walk to one and press E: a subagent takes the job while you move on.", 8500);
659	  }
660	  briefingStartBtn?.addEventListener("click", startLevel);
661	
662	  function failLevel() {
663	    if (failed || reportSent) return;
664	    failed = true;
665	    timerRunning = false;
666	    setMap(false);
667	    closeTerminal(false);
668	    fp.detach();
669	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
670	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
671	    failEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
672	  }
673	  function resetLevel() {
674	    failed = false;
675	    reportSent = false;
676	    dismissedSite = null;
677	    reportDismissed = false;
678	    clearTimeout(winTimer);
679	    for (const s of sites) {
680	      s.state = "broken";
681	      s.workT = 0; s.meltT = 0;
682	      s.brokenModel.visible = true;
683	      s.upgradeModel.visible = false;
684	      const anim = s.upgradeModel.userData.anim;
685	      if (anim?.type === "spire") anim.beam.visible = false;
686	      s.ice.visible = false;
687	      s.ice.material.opacity = 0.62;
688	      s.sprite.visible = false;
689	    }
690	    for (let i = 0; i < drones.length; i++) {
691	      drones[i].state = "bay";
692	      drones[i].site = null;
693	      drones[i].model.position.copy(drones[i].home);
694	      workBeams[i].visible = false;
695	    }
696	    failEl?.classList.add("hidden");
697	    winEl?.classList.add("hidden");
698	    updateSystems();
699	    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
700	    timerRunning = true;
701	    updateClock();
702	    if (active) fp.attach();
703	    showTutorial("New attempt — the fleet works in parallel. Use all five subagents.", 6000);
704	  }
705	
706	  // ---------- drone dispatching ----------
707	  function assignDrone(site) {
708	    const drone = drones.find((d) => d.state === "bay");
709	    if (!drone) return;                 // shouldn't happen: one drone per system
710	    site.state = "working";
711	    site.workT = 0;
712	    drone.state = "flying";
713	    drone.site = site;
714	    drone.from.copy(drone.model.position);
715	    drone.to.set(site.anchor.position.x, site.anchor.position.y + 6.5, site.anchor.position.z);
716	    drone.t = 0;
717	    drone.dur = Math.max(1, drone.from.distanceTo(drone.to) / DRONE_SPEED);
718	    updateSystems();
719	    teachOnce("assigned",
720	      "The subagent's got it — you're free. Send the others while it works; the clock won't wait.", 7000);
721	  }
722	  function sealSite(site) {
723	    site.state = "sealed";
724	    site.brokenModel.visible = false;
725	    site.upgradeModel.visible = true;   // built — but dormant until ADD TO SHIP
726	    site.ice.visible = true;
727	    site.ice.material.opacity = 0.62;
728	    site.sprite.visible = true;
729	    updateSystems();
730	    teachOnce("sealed",
731	      "A subagent finished while you were away — its work is sealed under a checkpoint. Walk up and see what it actually did.", 8000);
732	  }
733	
734	  // ---------- terminal ----------
735	  function flashTerminal(text, ok) {
736	    if (!termMsg) return;
737	    clearTimeout(msgTimer);
738	    termMsg.textContent = text;
739	    termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
740	    termMsg.classList.add(ok ? "show-ok" : "show-err");
741	    msgTimer = setTimeout(() => termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err"), 3600);
742	  }
743	  function renderTerminal() {
744	    if (!terminalOpen) return;
745	    if (termMode === "review") {
746	      if (!explained) {
747	        // The command comes pre-filled — you never type a checkpoint id.
748	        termHint.textContent = "# what did the subagent actually do? ask the checkpoint";
749	        termInput.textContent = `entire checkpoint explain ${reviewSite.sys.ckpt}`;
750	        termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
751	        if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
752	          `<span class="cta-label">PRESS</span>` +
753	          `<kbd class="cta-key cta-key-yes">Enter</kbd><span class="cta-note">run it</span>`;
754	      } else {
755	        termHint.textContent = "# reviewed — your call";
756	        termInput.textContent = `entire checkpoint explain ${reviewSite.sys.ckpt}`;
757	        termInput.classList.add("is-dim");
758	        if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
759	          `<button id="db-add-btn" type="button">ADD TO SHIP</button>` +
760	          `<span class="cta-note">or press <kbd class="cta-key cta-key-yes">Y</kbd></span>`;
761	      }
762	    } else if (termMode === "report") {
763	      termHint.textContent = reportSent
764	        ? "# dispatch sent — the day is on the record"
765	        : "# five jobs accepted — send the day's report";
766	      termInput.textContent = buffer;
767	      termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
768	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML = reportSent ? "" :
769	        `<span class="cta-label">TYPE</span><span class="cta-cmd">entire dispatch</span>`;
770	    }
771	  }
772	  function openTerminal(mode, site) {
773	    if (terminalOpen) return;
774	    terminalOpen = true;
775	    termMode = mode;
776	    reviewSite = site || null;
777	    explained = false;
778	    buffer = "";
779	    fp.detach();
780	    termMsg?.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
781	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
782	    termEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
783	    renderTerminal();
784	  }
785	  function closeTerminal(dismiss) {
786	    if (!terminalOpen) return;
787	    if (dismiss) {
788	      if (termMode === "review" && reviewSite) dismissedSite = reviewSite;
789	      if (termMode === "report") reportDismissed = true;
790	    }
791	    terminalOpen = false;
792	    termMode = null;
793	    reviewSite = null;
794	    explained = false;
795	    buffer = "";
796	    termEl?.classList.add("hidden");
797	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
798	    if (active && !failed) fp.attach();
799	  }
800	
801	  // ---------- entire checkpoint explain (pre-filled, Enter to run) ----------
802	  function runExplain() {
803	    if (!reviewSite || explained) return;
804	    explained = true;
805	    const s = reviewSite;
806	    if (termList) {
807	      termList.innerHTML =
808	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">checkpoint</span>` +
809	        `<span class="tl-title">${s.sys.ckpt}</span></div>` +
810	        s.sys.card.map(([k, v]) =>
811	          `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">${k}</span>` +
812	          `<span class="tl-title">${v}</span></div>`
813	        ).join("");
814	      termList.classList.remove("hidden");
815	    }
816	    flashTerminal(`explained ${s.sys.ckpt} — ${s.sys.name.toLowerCase()} is now a ${s.sys.became}`, true);
817	    teachOnce("explained",
818	      "That's the story behind the checkpoint — work you didn't watch is never a mystery. If it's good, add it to the ship.", 8000);
819	    renderTerminal();
820	  }
821	
822	  // ---------- ADD TO SHIP ----------
823	  function addToShip() {
824	    if (!reviewSite || !explained) return;
825	    const s = reviewSite;
826	    s.state = "added";
827	    s.meltT = MELT_DUR;                 // the seal melts; the upgrade wakes up
828	    const anim = s.upgradeModel.userData.anim;
829	    if (anim?.type === "spire") anim.beam.visible = true;
830	    updateSystems();
831	    if (allAdded()) {
832	      // The fifth accept doesn't close the terminal — the finish line is the
833	      // next command at the prompt you're already at, like the real CLI.
834	      termMode = "report";
835	      reviewSite = null;
836	      explained = false;
837	      buffer = "";
838	      termList?.classList.add("hidden");
839	      flashTerminal("all five systems online", true);
840	      showTutorial("Every system accounted for — send the day's report: type `entire dispatch`.", 0);
841	      renderTerminal();
842	    } else {
843	      closeTerminal(false);
844	      showTutorial(`${s.sys.name} online (${addedCount()} / ${sites.length}).`, 5000);
845	    }
846	  }
847	  // The ADD TO SHIP button (terminal CTA is re-rendered, so delegate the click).
848	  termCta?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
849	    if (!active) return;
850	    if (e.target?.id === "db-add-btn") addToShip();
851	  });
852	
853	  // ---------- the finish line: entire dispatch ----------
854	  // "Generate a dispatch summarizing recent agent work" — five drones just
855	  // worked; the report writes itself from their checkpoints.
856	  function sendDispatch() {
857	    reportSent = true;
858	    timerRunning = false;
859	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
860	    if (termList) {
861	      termList.innerHTML =
862	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">DISPATCH</span>` +
863	        `<span class="tl-title">drone bay — day report</span></div>` +
864	        sites.map((s) =>
865	          `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">·</span>` +

1016	        d.model.userData.ring.rotation.z += dt * 6;

1019	          else { d.state = "bay"; d.model.position.copy(d.home); }
1020	        }
1021	      } else if (d.state === "working") {

1024	          s.anchor.position.x + Math.cos(t * 1.4) * 1.2,
1025	          s.anchor.position.y + 6.5 + Math.sin(t * 2.2) * 0.4,

1043	            d.t = 0;
1044	            d.dur = Math.max(1, d.from.distanceTo(d.to) / DRONE_SPEED);
1045	          }
1046	        }
1047	      }
1048	    }

> AGENT

Love this direction. It also kills the old "walking around is annoying" complaint, since "set them off on a track" basically replaces the walk-to-scattered-sites layout with a control view.

Here's the shape I'm picturing:

**Mission Control, not a foot race**
- You're parked at the bay console. Five rails run out from the bay toward the five dark systems (the broken props stay where they are as the finish posts).
- Five agents sit at a starting line. You press to **launch** each one — you decide *when* and in *what order*. Stagger them, or send all five at once. That's the "control when they start" beat.
- Each agent crawls down its rail; a little progress bar fills as it goes. No drone wandering, no you wandering.
- **Powerups = overclock.** You get a small pool of boost charges. Drop one on whichever agent is lagging to surge it forward. The engagement becomes *orchestration* (who to boost, when) instead of *racing the clock*.
- When an agent reaches its system, its work seals → the existing `entire checkpoint explain` → **ADD TO SHIP** flow stays exactly as is. That's still the lesson; the rail/boost layer just feeds it.

The thing your "i dont want to race against them" line forces a decision on: right now the whole level is built around a 150s countdown with a panic-red sky. If we drop the adversarial clock, I need to know what replaces the tension so it doesn't go limp.

Three real forks before I touch code:

> AGENT

Got it. Here's the concrete design with your answers baked in. One unified control: **each system is a number key (1–5)**, and what the key does depends on that agent's state:

**The loop (fixed mission-control view, no walking)**
- Camera sits up at the bay, looking out over the island. All five systems + their rails visible at once.
- **Press 1–5 → launch** that agent. It sets off down its rail toward its system. You pick the order and when.
- **Press it again while it's in flight → boost** (overclock). It surges faster. You get a few charges (I'll start with 3), so you spend them on whoever's lagging against the clock. That's the "make them go faster" — no jargon, just a speed surge with a particle burst.
- Agent reaches its system → work seals (ice-blue beam), same as now.
- **Press that number when it's sealed → review**: the pre-filled `entire checkpoint explain` terminal opens, then ADD TO SHIP. Unchanged — that's still the lesson.
- All five online → `entire dispatch` finishes the day. Unchanged.

**Keeps:** the 150s clock + panic sky (you're beating the clock with smart launch order + boosts, not racing the drones themselves). The systems, props, drones, checkpoint cards, explain flow, dispatch finish.

**Drops:** first-person walking, pointer lock, the bird's-eye M map, walk-up proximity. The HUD shows a small "agents" strip telling you what each number does right now (LAUNCH / BOOST x3 / REVIEW / ONLINE).

This is a big rewrite of `droneBayView.js`. Want me to build it?

maybe the subagent is more car like than drone like since we're doing tracks. i dont know the answer to those..im like like i want the user to be able to do sequential or parallel subagent use so they can put all on the track and start them or just do them one by one.any ideas of how to make this functionally work..idk where boost fits..thats what i was trying to ask earlier

> AGENT

Yeah — cars on a drag-strip is a cleaner fit than drones. Here's a functional version that gives you both sequential and parallel from **one** mechanic, and puts boost somewhere it actually earns its place:

**Layout**
- Fixed camera over a staging deck. Five lanes fan out to the five dark systems (the broken props are the finish lines).
- One car per lane, parked at the start.

**Launch — arm, then green flag**
- **Click a car → it's armed** (engine lit). Click again to un-arm.
- **Space = green flag**: every armed car launches at once.
- Arm all five, then Space → full parallel. Arm one, Space, arm the next, Space → sequential. Mix however you want. That one rule covers both styles, and it's literally "put them on the track and start them."

**Boost — the thing you do while they drive**
- A launched car drives its lane at base speed; reaching its system seals the work (ice beam), same as today.
- Here's where boost fits: after the green flag, without boost you'd just *watch* the cars roll. **Click a moving car → nitro**: it surges, particle trail, costs one charge. You get ~3 charges a run.
- So boost is your catch-up tool against the clock. Play parallel and you barely need it; play sequential and you'll lean on nitro to stay ahead. That's what makes it matter instead of being decoration.

**Unchanged:** the 150s clock + panic sky, the sealed → `entire checkpoint explain` → ADD TO SHIP review, and `entire dispatch` to finish.

The nice side effect: arming all five and dropping one green flag is the fastest clear, so the level quietly rewards parallel subagents — same "let the math nudge them" philosophy that's already in the file, no lecture.

222	            <p class="bf-story">
223	              Restoring the ship's memory surfaced its full pre-crash archive —
224	              every checkpoint the old crew ever banked, frozen dark across the
225	              island. Far too many to read one by one. The ship needs three
226	              specific memories back, and it remembers a little about each.
227	            </p>

232	                <li>At the gold-beam terminal, type <code>entire checkpoint search "&lt;word&gt;"</code>.</li>
233	                <li>The matching block lights a beam — run to it and press <code>E</code> to transmit.</li>

299	            <h1 class="bf-title">The Drone Bay</h1>
300	            <p class="bf-story">
301	              Restoring the ship's memory woke its drone bay — five subagents,
302	              ready for orders. Five systems are still dark — too many to fix
303	              yourself before the window closes.
304	              The subagents work fast and they <strong>improvise</strong>. Every
305	              job they finish is banked under a checkpoint, and the ship won't
306	              power anything it can't account for. That part is yours.
307	            </p>
308	            <div class="bf-mission">
309	              <div class="bf-mission-head">YOUR MISSION</div>
310	              <ol class="bf-steps">
311	                <li>Walk to a dark system (amber beam) and press <code>E</code> — a subagent takes the job while you move on.</li>
312	                <li>When a beam turns ice-blue, the work is sealed under a checkpoint. Walk up — <code>entire checkpoint explain</code> comes pre-filled. Press <code>Enter</code>.</li>
313	                <li>Read what the subagent actually did, then <strong>ADD TO SHIP</strong> (<code>Y</code>).</li>
314	                <li>All five online → send the day's report: <code>entire dispatch</code>, before <strong>0:00</strong>.</li>

379	            <h1 class="bf-title">Launch Clearance</h1>
380	            <p class="bf-story">
381	              The ship is rebuilt. For the first time, you're inside — pilot's
382	              chair, island in the window. But the launch computer won't arm on
383	              work nobody can account for, and most of today's work you never
384	              watched. The ship has questions only the record can answer.
385	            </p>
386	            <p class="bf-story bf-quote">
387	              "I can read the record. Protocol says you have to be the one to
388	              confirm it."
389	            </p>

391	              <div class="bf-mission-head">YOUR MISSION</div>
392	              <ol class="bf-steps">

394	                <li>Pick a way to look it up (<code>1</code>–<code>3</code>): a command, or a skill that runs it for you.</li>
395	                <li>The answer lands on screen. Confirm it for the launch computer.</li>
396	                <li>Five confirms complete the <strong>launch code</strong> — before the window closes at <strong>0:00</strong>.</li>

  "content": "import * as THREE from \"three\";\nimport { createTerrain } from \"./terrain.js\";\nimport { makeBeamTexture, makeIceBlock, makeIdSprite } from \"./memoryProps.js\";\n\n// LEVEL 2 (\"The Drone Bay\") — mission control, not a foot race.\n//\n// The day-to-day agentic loop, played as a starting grid. Five ship systems\n// are dark out across the island; five cars (subagents) wait at the bay. You\n// don't run — you ORCHESTRATE:\n//\n//   click a car            → it arms (engine lit)\n//   press SPACE            → every armed car launches at once (the green flag)\n//   click a car mid-run    → spend a nitro charge, it surges (catch-up tool)\n//   a car reaches its system → its work seals under a checkpoint (ice beam)\n//   click the parked car   → `entire checkpoint explain <id>` is PRE-FILLED\n//   read what it did       → ADD TO SHIP (Y) → system online\n//   all five online        → type `entire dispatch` — the day writes itself\n//\n// Arm one and green-flag at a time = sequential. Arm all five, one green flag\n// = parallel — the fast clear. Nothing forces parallel; the clock just rewards\n// it. Cars IMPROVISE (a bent dish comes back as a signal spire); the transform\n// is what makes you NEED the explanation.\n\nconst TOTAL_TIME = 150;      // seconds for the whole run (tunable)\nconst BASE_SPEED = 1 / 14;   // lane progress per second (un-boosted ≈ 14s)\nconst BOOST_MULT = 2.6;      // nitro speed multiplier\nconst BOOST_DUR  = 2.2;      // seconds a nitro surge lasts\nconst NITRO_MAX  = 3;        // charges per run\nconst LOW_TIME = 45;         // clock turns urgent under this\nconst CRIT_TIME = 15;        // clock goes CRITICAL under this\nconst PANIC_TIME = 45;       // the SKY starts shifting toward panic-red\nconst MELT_DUR = 1.2;        // seconds the ice takes to melt on ADD TO SHIP\n\n// Starting grid — five parallel lanes fanning slightly out to the systems.\nconst START_Z = 64;\nconst FINISH_Z = -34;\nconst LANE = (i) => ({ startX: (i - 2) * 14, finishX: (i - 2) * 24 });\n\n// Sky panic palette — same dread as Level 1's clock.\nconst SKY_CALM  = new THREE.Color(0x2a2350);\nconst SKY_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x6e0f16);\nconst FOG_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x4a0a0e);\nconst DOME_CALM = new THREE.Color(0x3a3168);\nconst DOME_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x7a141c);\nconst SUN_CALM  = new THREE.Color(0xfff1dc);\nconst SUN_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0xff5a3c);\n\n// The five dark systems, what the cars improvise them into, and the story each\n// checkpoint carries. Ids are fixed so the fiction is stable across runs.\n// Exported: Level 3 quizzes the player on this exact record (and uses sys.pos\n// on its own island — left untouched here; this level lays out its own grid).\nexport const SYSTEMS = [\n  {\n    ckpt: \"a1c9e4f72b05\", name: \"IGNITION COILS\",\n    pos: { x: -34, z: -28 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenCoils\", upgrade: \"buildPlasmaRing\",\n    became: \"Plasma Ring\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-1\"],\n      [\"system\", \"ignition coils\"],\n      [\"did\", \"coils beyond saving — rebuilt as a plasma ring from salvaged hull plate\"],\n      [\"session\", \"2 attempts · parts scavenged: hull plate ×3\"],\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    ckpt: \"3d7b0f9c61ae\", name: \"NAV CORE\",\n    pos: { x: 40, z: -18 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenNav\", upgrade: \"buildStarDome\",\n    became: \"Star Dome\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-2\"],\n      [\"system\", \"nav core\"],\n      [\"did\", \"core unrecoverable — remapped from scratch; starfield calibrated to the lavender belt\"],\n      [\"session\", \"1 attempt · 412 stars plotted\"],\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    ckpt: \"f25c8b30d971\", name: \"LONG-RANGE ANTENNA\",\n    pos: { x: 28, z: 38 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenDish\", upgrade: \"buildSignalSpire\",\n    became: \"Signal Spire\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-3\"],\n      [\"system\", \"long-range antenna\"],\n      [\"did\", \"dish unsalvageable — respun the mast into a signal spire; relay handshake at full strength\"],\n      [\"session\", \"3 attempts · the first two fell over\"],\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    ckpt: \"62e0a9d4c8f3\", name: \"LIFE SUPPORT\",\n    pos: { x: -30, z: 36 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenVent\", upgrade: \"buildGardenPod\",\n    became: \"Garden Pod\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-4\"],\n      [\"system\", \"life support scrubbers\"],\n      [\"did\", \"filters dead — replaced with a living filter; two vines scavenged from the crash\"],\n      [\"session\", \"1 attempt · the vines approved\"],\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    ckpt: \"8b47f1e62da0\", name: \"LANDING STRUTS\",\n    pos: { x: 8, z: -44 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenStrut\", upgrade: \"buildGravSkid\",\n    became: \"Grav Skid\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-5\"],\n      [\"system\", \"landing struts\"],\n      [\"did\", \"strut seized solid — swapped for grav skids; technically the ship floats now\"],\n      [\"session\", \"2 attempts · torque spec: vibes\"],\n    ],\n  },\n];\n\nfunction normalizeCmd(s) {\n  return s.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\\s+/g, \" \");\n}\n\n// ---------- prop builders: the broken systems ----------\nconst CHARRED = () => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x2a2e38, roughness: 0.9, metalness: 0.3 });\nconst SCORCH = () => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x3a3f4a, roughness: 0.85, metalness: 0.25 });\n\nfunction withWarnLight(g) {\n  // A small red blinking bulb so broken things read as \"broken\" up close.\n  const bulb = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.22, 10, 10),\n    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x401512, emissive: 0xff3b2e, emissiveIntensity: 1.6 })\n  );\n  bulb.position.y = g.userData.warnY ?? 3.2;\n  g.add(bulb);\n  g.userData.warn = bulb;\n  return g;\n}\nfunction buildBrokenCoils() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n    const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.1 - i * 0.18, 1.2 - i * 0.18, 1.0, 10), i ? CHARRED() : SCORCH());\n    c.position.y = 0.5 + i * 1.05;\n    c.rotation.z = (i % 2 ? -1 : 1) * 0.12 * (i + 1);   // the stack leans, sadly\n    g.add(c);\n  }\n  g.userData.warnY = 3.6;\n  return withWarnLight(g);\n}\nfunction buildBrokenNav() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const ped = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.9, 1.2, 1.4, 8), SCORCH());\n  ped.position.y = 0.7;\n  const core = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1.5, 0), CHARRED());\n  core.position.y = 2.6;\n  core.rotation.set(0.4, 0.2, 0.5);\n  g.add(ped, core);\n  g.userData.warnY = 4.4;\n  return withWarnLight(g);\n}\nfunction buildBrokenDish() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const mast = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.18, 0.24, 3.2, 8), SCORCH());\n  mast.position.y = 1.4;\n  mast.rotation.z = 0.5;                                // bent over\n  const dish = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.9, 1.5, 0.4, 12), CHARRED());\n  dish.position.set(1.6, 0.5, 0);\n  dish.rotation.z = 1.2;                                // face-down in the dirt\n  g.add(mast, dish);\n  g.userData.warnY = 3.0;\n  return withWarnLight(g);\n}\nfunction buildBrokenVent() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const box = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.6, 2.0, 2.0), SCORCH());\n  box.position.y = 1.0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n    const slat = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.2, 0.16, 0.1), CHARRED());\n    slat.position.set(0, 0.6 + i * 0.5, 1.06);\n    slat.rotation.x = 0.5;\n    g.add(slat);\n  }\n  g.add(box);\n  g.userData.warnY = 2.8;\n  return withWarnLight(g);\n}\nfunction buildBrokenStrut() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const leg = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.8, 4.2, 0.8), SCORCH());\n  leg.position.set(0, 1.6, 0);\n  leg.rotation.z = 0.55;                                // collapsed sideways\n  const foot = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.4, 1.6, 0.5, 10), CHARRED());\n  foot.position.set(1.6, 0.25, 0);\n  g.add(leg, foot);\n  g.userData.warnY = 3.4;\n  return withWarnLight(g);\n}\n\n// ---------- prop builders: what the cars improvise them into ----------\nfunction buildPlasmaRing() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const base = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.2, 1.5, 0.7, 10), SCORCH());\n  base.position.y = 0.35;\n  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.8, 0.3, 12, 36),\n    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x6b4a16, emissive: 0xffb86b, emissiveIntensity: 1.4, roughness: 0.4 })\n  );\n  ring.position.y = 3.4;\n  g.add(base, ring);\n  g.userData.anim = { type: \"ring\", ring };\n  return g;\n}\nfunction buildStarDome() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const ped = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.9, 1.2, 1.2, 8), SCORCH());\n  ped.position.y = 0.6;\n  const stars = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n  const pts = new Float32Array(120 * 3);\n  for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {\n    // random points in a dome above the pedestal\n    const r = 1.2 + Math.random() * 1.4;\n    const th = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n    const ph = Math.random() * Math.PI * 0.5;\n    pts[i * 3] = Math.cos(th) * Math.sin(ph) * r;\n    pts[i * 3 + 1] = 3.2 + Math.cos(ph) * r * 0.9;\n    pts[i * 3 + 2] = Math.sin(th) * Math.sin(ph) * r;\n  }\n  stars.setAttribute(\"position\", new THREE.BufferAttribute(pts, 3));\n  const field = new THREE.Points(stars, new THREE.PointsMaterial({\n    color: 0xbfe9ff, size: 0.16, transparent: true, opacity: 0.95,\n    blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false,\n  }));\n  const orbit = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.TorusGeometry(2.0, 0.05, 8, 40),\n    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.7 })\n  );\n  orbit.position.y = 3.6;\n  orbit.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2.4;\n  g.add(ped, field, orbit);\n  g.userData.anim = { type: \"dome\", field, orbit };\n  return g;\n}\nfunction buildSignalSpire(beamTex) {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const spire = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.5, 7.5, 8),\n    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x3a4150, metalness: 0.7, roughness: 0.3, emissive: 0x123a4a, emissiveIntensity: 0.8 })\n  );\n  spire.position.y = 3.75;\n  const beam = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.4, 0.4, 70, 10, 1, true),\n    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n      map: beamTex, color: 0x9af0ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.55,\n      blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide, fog: false,\n    })\n  );\n  beam.position.y = 42;\n  beam.visible = false;          // fires when the upgrade is ADDED\n  g.add(spire, beam);\n  g.userData.anim = { type: \"spire\", beam };\n  return g;\n}\nfunction buildGardenPod() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const tray = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.9, 2.1, 0.6, 12), SCORCH());\n  tray.position.y = 0.3;\n  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {\n    const plant = new THREE.Mesh(\n      new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.28, 0.9 + (i % 3) * 0.35, 6),\n      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1d4a2c, emissive: 0x35d97a, emissiveIntensity: 0.7, roughness: 0.7 })\n    );\n    const a = (i / 5) * Math.PI * 2;\n    plant.position.set(Math.cos(a) * 1.0, 1.0, Math.sin(a) * 1.0);\n    g.add(plant);\n  }\n  const domeGlass = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.SphereGeometry(2.1, 18, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2),\n    new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({\n      color: 0xd8ffe9, metalness: 0, roughness: 0.05, transmission: 0.7,\n      thickness: 1.2, transparent: true, opacity: 0.4,\n    })\n  );\n  domeGlass.position.y = 0.6;\n  g.add(tray, domeGlass);\n  g.userData.anim = { type: \"pod\" };\n  return g;\n}\nfunction buildGravSkid() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const pad = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.7, 1.9, 0.5, 12),\n    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x2c3140, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.35, emissive: 0x123a4a, emissiveIntensity: 0.7 })\n  );\n  pad.position.y = 1.6;\n  const glow = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.5, 0.12, 8, 32),\n    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.65 })\n  );\n  glow.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;\n  glow.position.y = 0.5;\n  g.add(pad, glow);\n  g.userData.anim = { type: \"skid\", pad, glow };\n  return g;\n}\n\n// Level 3 places the finished upgrades on its own island (already online —\n// the questions are about how they got that way). buildSignalSpire takes the\n// shared beam texture; the rest take no args.\nexport const UPGRADE_BUILDERS = {\n  buildPlasmaRing, buildStarDome, buildSignalSpire, buildGardenPod, buildGravSkid,\n};\n\n// ---------- the little worker car ----------\nfunction buildCar() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const bodyMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n    color: 0x2c3140, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.35, emissive: 0x2a8aa6, emissiveIntensity: 0.55,\n  });\n  const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.1, 0.7, 3.4), bodyMat);\n  body.position.y = 1.0;\n  const nose = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.6, 0.5, 0.9), bodyMat);\n  nose.position.set(0, 0.95, -2.0);\n  const cabin = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.4, 0.7, 1.5),\n    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x0c2030, emissive: 0x6fe3ff, emissiveIntensity: 0.55, metalness: 0.4, roughness: 0.3 })\n  );\n  cabin.position.set(0, 1.55, 0.1);\n  const wheelGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.5, 0.5, 0.4, 12);\n  const wheelMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x10141c, roughness: 0.8, metalness: 0.2 });\n  const wheels = [];\n  for (const dx of [-1.05, 1.05]) {\n    for (const dz of [-1.15, 1.15]) {\n      const w = new THREE.Mesh(wheelGeo, wheelMat);\n      w.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;\n      w.position.set(dx, 0.5, dz);\n      wheels.push(w);\n      g.add(w);\n    }\n  }\n  // Nitro flare — a glow under the car, lit only while boosting.\n  const glow = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2.8, 4.2),\n    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n      color: 0xffb86b, transparent: true, opacity: 0, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false,\n    })\n  );\n  glow.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;\n  glow.position.y = 0.12;\n  g.add(body, nose, THREE.Group();\n  const pedestal = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.6, 4.6, 1.6),\n    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1c2230, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.35 })\n  );\n  pedestal.position.y = 2.3;\n  const screen = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2.1, 1.3),\n    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n      color: 0x0c2030, emissive: 0x2a8aa6, emissiveIntensity: 1.4, roughness: 0.3,\n    })\n  );\n  screen.position.set(0, 4.0, 0.82);\n  screen.rotation.x = -0.28;\n  g.add(pedestal, screen);\n  return g;\n}\n\nexport function createDroneBayView(renderer, { onExit, onComplete, onNext, onNewGame } = {}) {\n  const canvas = renderer.domElement;\n\n  // ---------- scene & sky ----------\n  const scene = new THREE.Scene();\n  scene.background = SKY_CALM.clone();\n  scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(SKY_CALM.clone(), 120, 360);\n\n  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(\n    60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 2000\n  );\n  // Fixed mission-control vantage: behind the grid, looking out at the systems.\n  camera.position.set(0, 32, 104);\n  camera.lookAt(0, 2, 4);\n\n  // ---------- lighting ----------\n  scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xcdbcff, 0x3a2f5e, 0.7));\n  const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(SUN_CALM, 1.5);\n  sun.position.set(60, 90, 40);\n  scene.add(sun);\n  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x6a5a92, 0.3));\n\n  const dome = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.SphereGeometry(900, 32, 16),\n    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: DOME_CALM.clone(), side: THREE.BackSide, fog: false })\n  );\n  scene.add(dome);\n\n  // ---------- terrain (the same island) ----------\n  const terrain = createTerrain({ size: 200, segments: 220, maxHeight: 26, seed: 1337 });\n  scene.add(terrain.mesh, terrain.water);\n\n  const beamTex = makeBeamTexture();\n\n  // ---------- the five lanes: a car at the grid, a system at the finish ----------\n  const BUILD = {\n    buildBrokenCoils, buildBrokenNav, buildBrokenDish, buildBrokenVent, buildBrokenStrut,\n    buildPlasmaRing, buildStarDome, buildGardenPod, buildGravSkid,\n    buildSignalSpire: () => buildSignalSpire(beamTex),\n  };\n\n  const lanes = SYSTEMS.map((sys, idx) => {\n    const { startX, finishX } = LANE(idx);\n    const start = new THREE.Vector3(startX, terrain.heightAt(startX, START_Z) + 0.6, START_Z);\n    const finish = new THREE.Vector3(finishX, terrain.heightAt(finishX, FINISH_Z), FINISH_Z);\n\n    // The system (finish line).\n    const anchor = new THREE.Group();\n    anchor.position.copy(finish);\n    const brokenModel = BUILD[sys.broken]();\n    const upgradeModel = BUILD[sys.upgrade]();\n    upgradeModel.visible = false;\n    anchor.add(brokenModel, upgradeModel);\n\n    // Status beam: amber = waiting, cyan = car inbound, ice-blue = sealed.\n    const beam = new THREE.Mesh(\n      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.6, 0.6, 60, 12, 1, true),\n      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n        map: beamTex, color: 0xff8a5c, transparent: true, opacity: 0.3,\n        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide, fog: false,\n      })\n    );\n    beam.position.y = 30;\n    anchor.add(beam);\n\n    // The checkpoint seal: ice + floating trailer id, like Level 1's bank.\n    const ice = makeIceBlock();\n    ice.scale.setScalar(1.25);\n    ice.position.y = 2.6;\n    ice.visible = false;\n    anchor.add(ice);\n    const sprite = makeIdSprite(sys.ckpt);\n    sprite.position.y = 8.4;\n    sprite.visible = false;\n    anchor.add(sprite);\n    scene.add(anchor);\n\n    // The rail — two glowing lines sampled along the terrain so they don't clip.\n    const railMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x35506a, transparent: true, opacity: 0.6 });\n    const railL = new THREE.Line(railGeo(start, finish, -1.2, terrain), railMat);\n    const railR = new THREE.Line(railGeo(start, finish, 1.2, terrain), railMat);\n    scene.add(railL, railR);\n\n    // The car, parked at the grid.\n    const car = buildCar();\n    car.position.copy(start);\n    car.lookAt(finish.x, start.y, finish.z);\n    car.userData.laneIdx = idx;\n    scene.add(car);\n\n    return {\n      sys, idx, start, finish, anchor, brokenModel, upgradeModel, beam, ice, sprite,\n      car, railL, railR, railMat,\n      state: \"staged\",     // staged → running → sealed → added\n      armed: false,\n      prog: 0,             // 0..1 along the lane\n      boostT: 0,           // remaining nitro time\n      meltT: 0,            // ice melt after ADD TO SHIP\n    };\n  });\n\n  function railGeo(a, b, offX, terr) {\n    const N = 24;\n    const pts = [];\n    for (let i = 0; i <= N; i++) {\n      const k = i / N;\n      const x = a.x + (b.x - a.x) * k + offX;\n      const z = a.z + (b.z - a.z) * k;\n      pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(x, terr.heightAt(x, z) + 0.25, z));\n    }\n    return new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts);\n  }\n\n  // ---------- the ship console (behind the grid) ----------\n  const consoleAnchor = new THREE.Group();\n  const cz = START_Z + 12;\n  consoleAnchor.position.set(0, terrain.heightAt(0, cz), cz);\n  consoleAnchor.add(makeConsole());\n  const consoleGlow = new THREE.PointLight(0xffd27a, 5, 28, 2);\n  consoleGlow.position.y = 5;\n  consoleAnchor.add(consoleGlow);\n  scene.add(consoleAnchor);\n\n  // ---------- spark bursts (nitro / seal juice) ----------\n  const sparks = [];\n  function spawnSpark(pos, color = 0x9af0ff, n = 14) {\n    const arr = new Float32Array(n * 3);\n    const vel = [];\n    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n      arr[i * 3] = pos.x; arr[i * 3 + 1] = pos.y; arr[i * 3 + 2] = pos.z;\n      vel.push(new THREE.Vector3((Math.random() - 0.5) * 9, Math.random() * 7, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 9));\n    }\n    const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n    geo.setAttribute(\"position\", new THREE.BufferAttribute(arr, 3));\n    const mat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({\n      color, size: 0.45, transparent: true, opacity: 1,\n      blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false,\n    });\n    const pts = new THREE.Points(geo, mat);\n    scene.add(pts);\n    sparks.push({ pts, geo, mat, vel, life: 0, ttl: 0.7 });\n  }\n\n  // ---------- HUD elements ----------\n  const promptEl = document.getElementById(\"fp-prompt\");\n  const controlsEl = document.getElementById(\"fp-controls\");\n  const crosshair = document.getElementById(\"crosshair\");\n  const tutorialEl = document.getElementById(\"tutorial\");\n  const fpShared = document.getElementById(\"fp-shared\");\n  const termEl = document.getElementById(\"terminal\");\n  const termHint = document.getElementById(\"term-hint\");\n  const termInput = document.getElementById(\"term-input\");\n  const termList = document.getElementById(\"term-list\");\n  const termMsg = document.getElementById(\"term-msg\");\n  const termCta = document.getElementById(\"term-cta\");\n  const dbHud = document.getElementById(\"db-hud\");\n  const briefingEl = document.getElementById(\"db-briefing\");\n  const briefingStartBtn = document.getElementById(\"db-briefing-start\");\n  const countdownEl = document.getElementById(\"db-countdown\");\n  const countdownTime = document.getElementById(\"db-countdown-time\");\n  const systemsEl = document.getElementById(\"db-systems-rows\");\n  const winEl = document.getElementById(\"db-win\");\n  const winSub = document.getElementById(\"db-win-sub\");\n  const failEl = document.getElementById(\"db-fail\");\n\n  let active = false;\n  let started = false;\n  let failed = false;\n  let reportSent = false;       // `entire dispatch` has been run — the win\n  let promptText = null;\n  const taught = new Set();\n  let tutorialTimer = null;\n  let msgTimer = null;\n  let winTimer = null;\n  let nitro = NITRO_MAX;\n\n  // Level countdown — the single source of pressure for the whole run.\n  let timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;\n  let timerRunning = false;\n\n  // Terminal state. Two modes share the one terminal:\n  //  \"review\" — pre-filled `entire checkpoint explain <id>` at a sealed system\n  //  \"report\" — type `entire dispatch` (the finish line)\n  let terminalOpen = false;\n  let termMode = null;\n  let reviewSite = null;        // the sealed lane being reviewed\n  let explained = false;        // Enter pressed — the card is showing\n  let buffer = \"\";\n  let reportDismissed = false;\n\n  const addedCount = () => lanes.filter((l) => l.state === \"added\").length;\n  const allAdded = () => addedCount() >= lanes.length;\n\n  // ---------- raycasting (the mouse is free — click cars) ----------\n  const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();\n  const ndc = new THREE.Vector2();\n  let hoverLane = null;\n\n  function laneAtPointer(e) {\n    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n    ndc.x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;\n    ndc.y = -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;\n    raycaster.setFromCamera(ndc, camera);\n    const hits = raycaster.intersectObjects(lanes.map((l) => l.car), true);\n    if (!hits.length) return null;\n    let o = hits[0].object;\n    while (o && o.userData.laneIdx === undefined) o = o.parent;\n    return o ? lanes[o.userData.laneIdx] : null;\n  }\n\n  // ---------- HUD helpers ----------\n  function setPrompt(text) {\n    if (!promptEl || text === promptText) return;\n    promptText = text;\n    if (text) { promptEl.textContent = text; promptEl.classList.remove(\"hidden\"); }\n    else promptEl.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function showTutorial(text, ms = 5500) {\n    if (!tutorialEl) return;\n    clearTimeout(tutorialTimer);\n    tutorialEl.textContent = text;\n    tutorialEl.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    if (ms > 0) tutorialTimer = setTimeout(() => tutorialEl.classList.add(\"hidden\"), ms);\n  }\n  function teachOnce(key, text, ms) {\n    if (taught.has(key)) return;\n    taught.add(key);\n    showTutorial(text, ms);\n  }\n  function setControls() {\n    if (!controlsEl) return;\n    controlsEl.innerHTML = `\n      <span class=\"control-item\"><span class=\"control-label\">Arm a car</span><span class=\"key key-wide\">click</span></span>\n      <span class=\"control-item\"><span class=\"control-label\">Launch armed</span><span class=\"key\">Space</span></span>\n      <span class=\"control-item\"><span class=\"control-label\">Nitro a runner</span><span class=\"key key-wide\">click</span></span>\n      <span class=\"control-item\"><span class=\"control-label\">Review a finish</span><span class=\"key key-wide\">click</span></span>\n      <span class=\"control-item\"><span class=\"control-label\">Return to orbit</span><span class=\"key\">B</span></span>\n    `;\n    controlsEl.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function hideControls() { controlsEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\"); }\n\n  // ---------- systems status panel ----------\n  const STATE_LABEL = {\n    staged: \"STAGED\", armed: \"ARMED\", running: \"EN ROUTE\", sealed: \"READY TO REVIEW\", added: \"ONLINE\",\n  };\n  function laneClass(l) {\n    if (l.state === \"staged\") return l.armed ? \"armed\" : \"staged\";\n    return l.state;\n  }\n  function updateSystems() {\n    if (!systemsEl) return;\n    const dots = Array.from({ length: NITRO_MAX }, (_, i) =>\n      `<span class=\"db-nitro-dot${i < nitro ? \"\" : \" is-spent\"}\"></span>`).join(\"\");\n    systemsEl.innerHTML =\n      `<div class=\"db-nitro\"><span class=\"db-nitro-label\">NITRO</span><span class=\"db-nitro-dots\">${dots}</span></div>` +\n      lanes.map((l) => {\n        const cls = laneClass(l);\n        const label = cls === \"staged\" && l.armed ? STATE_LABEL.armed : STATE_LABEL[cls === \"armed\" ? \"armed\" : l.state];\n        return `<div class=\"db-sys is-${cls}\">` +\n          `<span class=\"db-sys-name\">${l.sys.name}</span>` +\n          `<span class=\"db-sys-state\">${label}</span></div>`;\n      }).join(\"\");\n  }\n\n  // ---------- level countdown ----------\n  function fmtTime(s) {\n    s = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(s));\n    return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, \"0\")}`;\n  }\n  function updateClock() {\n    if (countdownTime) countdownTime.textContent = fmtTime(timeLeft);\n    countdownEl?.classList.toggle(\"is-low\", timerRunning && timeLeft <= LOW_TIME);\n    countdownEl?.classList.toggle(\"is-critical\", timerRunning && timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME);\n  }\n  function panicFactor() {\n    if (failed) return 1;\n    if (!timerRunning || timeLeft > PANIC_TIME) return 0;\n    let p = (PANIC_TIME - timeLeft) / PANIC_TIME;\n    p *= p;\n    if (timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME) {\n      const throb = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 85);\n      p += 0.14 * throb;\n    }\n    return Math.min(1, p);\n  }\n  function applyPanicSky() {\n    const p = reportSent ? 0 : panicFactor();\n    scene.background.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(SKY_PANIC, p);\n    scene.fog.color.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(FOG_PANIC, p);\n    dome.material.color.copy(DOME_CALM).lerp(DOME_PANIC, p);\n    sun.color.copy(SUN_CALM).lerp(SUN_PANIC, p * 0.85);\n  }\n\n  // ---------- briefing / win / fail / reset ----------\n  function showBriefing() {\n    timerRunning = false;\n    tutorialEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    briefingEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function startLevel() {\n    if (started) return;\n    started = true;\n    briefingEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;\n    timerRunning = true;\n    updateClock();\n    updateSystems();\n    showTutorial(\"Click a car to arm it, then press SPACE to launch. Arm all five and launch together — that's the fast clear.\", 9000);\n  }\n  briefingStartBtn?.addEventListener(\"click\", startLevel);\n\n  function failLevel() {\n    if (failed || reportSent) return;\n    failed = true;\n    timerRunning = false;\n    closeTerminal(false);\n    countdownEl?.classList.remove(\"is-low\", \"is-critical\");\n    tutorialEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    failEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function resetLevel() {\n    failed = false;\n    reportSent = false;\n    reportDismissed = false;\n    nitro = NITRO_MAX;\n    clearTimeout(winTimer);\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      l.state = \"staged\";\n      l.armed = false;\n      l.prog = 0; l.boostT = 0; l.meltT = 0;\n      l.car.position.copy(l.start);\n      l.car.lookAt(l.finish.x, l.start.y, l.finish.z);\n      l.car.userData.glow.material.opacity = 0;\n      l.brokenModel.visible = true;\n      l.upgradeModel.visible = false;\n      const anim = l.upgradeModel.userData.anim;\n      if (anim?.type === \"spire\") anim.beam.visible = false;\n      l.ice.visible = false;\n      l.ice.material.opacity = 0.62;\n      l.sprite.visible = false;\n    }\n    failEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    winEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    updateSystems();\n    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;\n    timerRunning = true;\n    updateClock();\n    showTutorial(\"New attempt — arm the whole grid and drop one green flag.\", 6000);\n  }\n\n  // ---------- launch / boost ----------\n  function toggleArm(l) {\n    if (l.state !== \"staged\") return;\n    l.armed = !l.armed;\n    updateSystems();\n    if (l.armed) teachOnce(\"armed\", \"Armed. Arm more, or press SPACE to send every armed car at once.\", 7000);\n  }\n  function greenFlag() {\n    let launched = 0;\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      if (l.state === \"staged\" && l.armed) {\n        l.state = \"running\"; l.armed = false; l.prog = 0; launched++;\n      }\n    }\n    if (!launched) return;\n    updateSystems();\n    teachOnce(\"launched\", \"They're off — and improvising. Nitro a laggard by clicking it; you've got three charges.\", 8000);\n  }\n  function boost(l) {\n    if (l.state !== \"running\") return;\n    if (nitro <= 0) { flashTerminalOff(\"out of nitro\"); return; }\n    if (l.boostT > 0) return;            // already surging\n    nitro -= 1;\n    l.boostT = BOOST_DUR;\n    spawnSpark(l.car.position.clone(), 0xffb86b, 18);\n    updateSystems();\n  }\n  function flashTerminalOff(text) {\n    // a tiny tutorial-line nudge when there's no terminal to flash\n    showTutorial(text, 1800);\n  }\n  function sealSite(l) {\n    l.state = \"sealed\";\n    l.brokenModel.visible = false;\n    l.upgradeModel.visible = true;       // built — but dormant until ADD TO SHIP\n    l.ice.visible = true;\n    l.ice.material.opacity = 0.62;\n    l.sprite.visible = true;\n    l.car.userData.glow.material.opacity = 0;\n    spawnSpark(l.anchor.position.clone().setY(l.anchor.position.y + 3), 0x8fe3ff, 16);\n    updateSystems();\n    teachOnce(\"sealed\",\n      \"A car finished — its work is sealed under a checkpoint (ice-blue). \"show-ok\" : \"show-err\");\n    msgTimer = setTimeout(() => termMsg.classList.remove(\"show-ok\", \"show-err\"), 3600);\n  }\n  function renderTerminal() {\n    if (!terminalOpen) return;\n    if (termMode === \"review\") {\n      if (!explained) {\n        termHint.textContent = \"# what did the subagent actually do? \"spire\") anim.beam.visible = true;\n    updateSystems();\n    if (allAdded()) {\n      // The fifth accept doesn't close the terminal — the finish line is the\n      // next command at the prompt you're already at, like the real CLI.\n      termMode = \"report\";\n      reviewSite = null;\n      explained = false;\n      buffer = \"\";\n      termList?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n      flashTerminal(\"all five systems online\", true);\n      showTutorial(\"Every system accounted for — send the day's report: type `entire dispatch`.\", 0);\n      renderTerminal();\n    } else {\n      closeTerminal(false);\n      showTutorial(`${s.sys.name} online (${addedCount()} / ${lanes.length}).`, 5000);\n    }\n  }\n  // The ADD TO SHIP button (terminal CTA is re-rendered, so delegate the termList.innerHTML =\n        `<div class=\"term-list-row\"><span class=\"tl-key tl-exp-key\">DISPATCH</span>` +\n        `<span class=\"tl-title\">drone bay — day report</span></div>` +\n        lanes.map((l) =>\n          `<div class=\"term-list-row\"><span class=\"tl-key tl-exp-key\">·</span>` +\n          `<span class=\"tl-title\">${l.sys.name.toLowerCase()} → ${l.sys.became} renderTerminal();\n    if (winSub) winSub.textContent = \"five jobs in parallel — the report wrote itself\";\n    winTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n      closeTerminal(false);\n      tutorialEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n      winEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    }, 4000);\n    onComplete?.();               // Level 2 cleared — opens the launch window\n  }\n  function submitCommand() {\n    const n = normalizeCmd(buffer);\n    buffer = \"\";\n    if (!n) { renderTerminal(); return; }\n    if (/^entire dispatch$/.test(n)) { sendDispatch(); return; }\n    if (/^entire (checkpoint|cp) list$/.test(n)) {\n      if (termList) {\n        termList.innerHTML = lanes.map((l) =>\n          `<div class=\"term-list-row\"><span class=\"tl-id termList.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n      }\n      flashTerminal(\"the raw log — now turn it into the day's report:  entire dispatch\", true);\n      renderTerminal();\n      return;\n    }\n    flashTerminal(\"command not recognized — try:  entire dispatch\", false);\n    renderTerminal();\n  }\n\n  // ---------- input ----------\n  function onCanvasClick(e) {\n    if (!active || !started || Briefing is up — Enter/Space begins the run, nothing else.\n    if (!started) {\n      if (e.code === \"Enter\" || e.code === \"Space\") { startLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // The clock ran out — R retries, N returns to the true beginning.\n    if (failed) {\n      if (e.code === \"KeyR\") { resetLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      if (e.code === \"KeyN\") {  if (reportSent) { e.preventDefault(); return; }\n      if (e.code === \"Enter\") { submitCommand(); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n      if (e.code === \"Backspace\") { buffer = buffer.slice(0, -1); (e.code === \"KeyB\") { onExit?.(); e.preventDefault(); }\n  }\n\n  // ---------- HUD per-frame ----------\n  function refreshHud() {\n    if (!started || terminalOpen) { setPrompt(null); return; }\n function update(dt, t) {\n    // Level countdown — one clock for the whole run.\n    if (active && timerRunning && !failed && !reportSent) {\n      timeLeft = Math.max(0, timeLeft - dt);\n      updateClock();\n      if (timeLeft <= 0) failLevel();\n    }\n    applyPanicSky();\n\n    // Cars: stage idle, drive their lane, park at the finish.\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      const car = l.car;\n      if (l.state === \"staged\") {\n        car.position.y = l.start.y + Math.sin((t + l.idx) * 2) * 0.05;\n        if (l.armed) {\n          const pulse = 0.55 + 0.45 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 6));\n          car.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = pulse;\n          car.userData.cabin.material.emissiveIntensity = pulse;\n        } else {\n          car.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = 0.4;\n          car.userData.cabin.material.emissiveIntensity = 0.45;\n        }\n      } else if (l.state === \"running\") {\n        if (l.boostT > 0) l.boostT = Math.max(0, l.boostT - dt);\n        const boosting = l.boostT > 0;\n        const speed = BASE_SPEED * (boosting ? BOOST_MULT : 1);\n        l.prog = Math.min(1, l.prog + speed * dt);\n        const k = l.prog;\n        const x = l.start.x + (l.finish.x - l.start.x) * k;\n        const z = l.start.z + (l.finish.z - l.start.z) * k;\n        car.position.set(x, terrain.heightAt(x, z) + 0.6, z);\n        const spin = (boosting ? 18 : 7) * dt;\n        for (const w of car.userData.wheels) w.rotation.x += spin;\n        car.userData.glow.material.opacity = boosting ? 0.55 + 0.25 * Math.sin(t * 30) : 0;\n        car.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = boosting ? 1.4 : 0.7;\n        if (boosting && Math.random() < dt * 12) {\n          spawnSpark(car.position.clone().setY(car.position.y + 0.4), 0xffb86b, 4);\n        }\n        if (l.prog >= 1) sealSite(l);\n      }\n\n      // hover lift on interactable cars\n      const interactable = l.state === \"staged\" || l.state === \"running\" || l.state === \"sealed\";\n      if (hoverLane === l && interactable) {\n        car.userData.cabin.material.emissiveIntensity = 1.1;\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Systems: status beams, warning blinks, seals, and live upgrades.\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      if (l.state === \"staged\") {\n        l.beam.material.color.setHex(0xff8a5c);\n        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.24 + Math.sin(t * 1.6 + l.idx) * 0.08;\n        l.beam.visible = true;\n        const warn = l.brokenModel.userData.warn;\n        if (warn) warn.material.emissiveIntensity = Math.sin(t * 5 + l.idx) > 0 ? 1.8 : 0.2;\n      } else if (l.state === \"running\") {\n        l.beam.material.color.setHex(0x6fe3ff);\n        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.4 + Math.sin(t * 6) * 0.18;\n        l.beam.visible = true;\n        const warn = l.brokenModel.userData.warn;\n        if (warn) warn.material.emissiveIntensity = Math.sin(t * 5 + l.idx) > 0 ? 1.8 : 0.2;\n      } else if (l.state === \"sealed\") {\n        l.beam.material.color.setHex(0x8fe3ff);\n        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.42;\n        l.beam.visible = true;\n      } else {\n        l.beam.visible = false;       // added — the upgrade itself is the landmark\n        if (l.meltT > 0) {\n l.upgradeModel.userData.anim;\n        if (anim?.type === \"ring\") {\n          anim.ring.rotation.y += dt * 0.8;\n          anim.ring.position.y = 3.4 + Math.sin(t * 1.6) * 0.2;\n        } else if (anim?.type === \"dome\") {\n          anim.field.rotation.y += dt * 0.25;\n          anim.orbit.rotation.z += dt * 0.6;\n        } else if (anim?.type === \"skid\") {\n          anim.pad.position.y = Built. Level 2 is now a **mission-control starting grid** instead of a first-person walk:

**What's in:**
- Fixed camera over the bay looking out at five lanes, each ending at a dark system. Five subagent **cars** parked on the grid (rails drawn along the terrain).
- **Click a car → arm** it (engine pulses). **Space → green flag** launches every armed car at once. Arm one-then-Space for sequential, arm all five for the fast parallel clear.
- **Click a moving car → nitro** (3 charges/run, amber surge + sparks). Your catch-up tool against the clock — shown as dots in the systems panel.
- Car reaches its system → work seals (ice-blue) → **click it** → the same pre-filled `entire checkpoint explain` → **ADD TO SHIP** → all five → `entire dispatch`.

**What's gone:** first-person walking, pointer lock, the bird's-eye M map, walk-up proximity, the E-to-assign key.

**Kept:** the 150s clock + panic sky, the checkpoint cards, explain/dispatch flow, win/fail screens.

> AGENT

Recorded the redesign in memory. That's the full rebuild — go run it and tell me what feels off.

> DEVELOPER

hmm this is kinda good . i dont feel like any tension though..hmm..dont make any changes yet..lets just analyze

> AGENT

Good instinct to stop and look at it. Here's my honest read of why it's flat.

**The core problem: nothing is scarce, so no decision has a downside.**

The optimal play is a solved 6-click move: arm all five, press Space, wait ~14s, review five. You'll never choose anything else, because parallel *strictly dominates* sequential — there's no reason to ever stagger. A choice that has one obviously-correct answer isn't a choice, and tension lives in choices.

Breaking down the specific culprits:

1. **The clock is enormous.** ~14s of travel + a few seconds per review ≈ 40–50s used out of 150. You've got ~100s of dead cushion, and the panic sky (45s) never even fires. The one tension source in the file is functionally switched off.

2. **Nitro has no job.** We built it as "the thing you do while cars drive," but all five cars are identical and finish together with time to spare. Nothing is ever behind, so there's never a reason to boost. The active mechanic is pointing at a problem that doesn't exist.

3. **The travel time is pure dead air.** After Space, you *watch* for 14 seconds. Watching isn't tension, it's downtime. Any tension has to live either at the launch (a one-time reflex) or at review — and right now neither bites.

**The constraint that shapes any fix:** you told me two things that pull against each other — "I don't want to race against them" and "keep the clock." Together they rule out the easy tension (you-vs-the-cars speed). So the tension has to come from **managing the fleet under a deadline**, not outrunning it. You're a shepherd with a clock, not a competitor.

That actually points at where the juice is hiding:

- **Make the cars imperfect mid-run.** If they don't all behave identically — one stalls, one hits trouble, one lags — then watching becomes *triaging*, and nitro suddenly has a purpose: "car 3 is stuck, spend a charge." That's honest to the real thing too (subagents work at different rates, some get stuck, you step in). It converts the dead 14s into the actual gameplay.
- **There's a latent tension already in the architecture you're not feeling yet: the review pileup.** You can only review one car at a time (one terminal). Launch all five and they all finish at once → they queue while the clock runs. Right now the clock's too loose to feel it, but tighten things and "parallel" stops being free — it dumps a backlog on you. That's a nice counterweight that doesn't punish parallel, just makes it have a shape.

**Off the table** (per your earlier calls): reject/inspect-the-work judgment puzzles and pulling values out of explanations — you killed those as too complicated, so I'm not counting them as levers.

If I had to name the single highest-leverage move: **the cars need to misbehave.** Identical cars + loose clock is the whole flatness. Want to dig into that one, or pressure-test a different lever?

> DEVELOPER

Yeah, I think you found the actual issue: **the cars are already on their tracks**, so the player is mostly just starting a machine.

Dragging could fix that, but only if dragging means **choosing**, not just moving pieces around.

I’d try this:

**Level 2 = Dispatch Grid**
Cars start in a bay at the bottom. Tracks are empty. Each track leads to a broken system.

Player loop:

1. **Drag a subagent car onto a track**
2. **Press Space to launch assigned cars**
3. **Use one boost/problem moment while they run**
4. **Review checkpoint, add to ship**

The key is: tracks should have problems.

Example track problems:

- **Static Gate**: opens/closes, launch timing matters.
- **Corruption Patch**: slows the car unless boosted through it.
- **Locked Track**: needs the Decoder car.
- **Broken Track**: needs the Repair car.
- **Signal Fog**: needs the Scout/Research car.
- **Firewall Track**: needs the Security car.

So dragging becomes meaningful:

> “This track is locked. I should drag Decoder here.”

My favorite simple version:

**Five cars. Five tracks. One visible problem per track.**
Each car has a specialty icon. Each track has a matching problem icon. You drag cars into the lanes, then launch them.

During the run, each lane has **one boost pad**. When the car reaches it, click the lane to fire nitro. That gives you something to do after launch without making it chaotic.

So the game has three satisfying verbs:

- **Drag** to assign
- **Launch** to start
- **Boost** to help

That feels way better than only “click all cars and wait.” are we able to do parallel or sequential..like i can put as many cars on the track as i want??  Yes, exactly. That should be the choice.

I’d make it:

**Cars start in the bay. Tracks are empty.**  
You drag as many cars onto tracks as you want, then launch.

So the player can choose:

**Sequential**
Drag 1 car to 1 track → launch → manage boost/problem → review.  
Safer, easier, but slow.

**Parallel**
Drag 3-5 cars onto tracks → launch them all together.  
Faster, but now you’re juggling boost moments / track problems at the same time.

Best rule: **one car per track**, but you can fill any number of tracks before launching.

That makes the decision interesting:

> “Do I send one car and play it safe, or send all five and try to manage the chaos?”

The current Level 2 already kind of has this idea because you can arm one car or arm all five before pressing Space. But dragging would make that choice feel way more physical and intentional.

The button/prompt could say:

- `1 CAR STAGED`
- `3 CARS STAGED`
- `SPACE: LAUNCH 3`
- `DRAG CARS TO TRACKS`

That’s probably the missing fun: not just watching cars go, but choosing how much parallel work you dare to run at once.

> AGENT

Now you're onto the real fix. Let me separate what's clearly right from the one fork that decides everything.

**What you nailed:** empty tracks + assign. The current version fails because the cars are *already on the rails* — you're flipping a switch, not making a decision. Starting them in a bay and making the player *commit* cars to lanes turns "start the machine" into "compose the run." That's the missing intention, full stop. And "drag as many as you dare, then launch" is exactly the sequential-vs-parallel dare we want — same as today's arm/green-flag, but physical and deliberate instead of abstract.

**The one fork to settle, because it changes the whole level:** do tracks have **specialty locks** (Locked Track *needs* Decoder) or **generic hazards** (Corruption Patch slows *any* car unless you boost through)?

I'd push hard for generic hazards, and not just for simplicity. Three problems with specialty-matching:

1. **It's the "drone management" you killed before.** Matching the right specialist to each track is the assignment puzzle you called too complicated. (It's also basically L3's pick-the-right-tool quiz, moved earlier.)
2. **It blows the 15-second onboarding.** Five specialties × five problem types is a legend the player has to learn before they can even start.
3. **The big one — it guts your own teaching.** This level exists to make `explain` worth reading, and that only works because of the improvisation surprise ("the bow became a sword — what did you DO?"). If the Decoder obviously decoded the Locked Track, you already know what it did. Why open the explanation? Rigid specialties telegraph the outcome and make `explain` skippable.

Generic hazards keep any car on any track (improvisation surprise intact), need almost no onboarding, and — here's the payoff — **they finally give nitro a job.** A hazard mid-lane is the boost moment. Launch one car: nurse it through, easy. Launch five: five hazards hit at once, you've got 3 nitro charges and a clock, and now you're triaging. *That's* the "dare I run all five and juggle" tension you're reaching for. It lives in the during-run boost, not in a pre-launch matching puzzle.

So the dare becomes real on both sides: parallel = fast but you'll botch boosts under load; sequential = safe but you'll bleed the clock if you baby every car one at a time.

**One UX flag:** dragging a 3D car onto a lane with a fixed camera is fiddly to get feeling good. "Click car in bay → click a track" is the identical decision with way less finicky implementation. Same intention, less frustration. Worth considering before we commit to true drag.

So before I touch anything: **specialty locks or generic hazards?** I'm recommending generic hazards. And do you want true drag, or click-car-then-click-track?

> DEVELOPER

lets try true drag and generic hazards

> AGENT

Locked in: true drag + generic hazards. Let me read the current view so I build on what's there rather than guessing.
