1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { createTerrain } from "./terrain.js";
3	import { makeBeamTexture, makeIceBlock, makeIdSprite } from "./memoryProps.js";
4	
5	// LEVEL 2 ("The Drone Bay") — mission control, not a foot race.
6	//
7	// The day-to-day agentic loop, played as a starting grid. Five ship systems
8	// are dark out across the island; five cars (subagents) wait at the bay. You
9	// don't run — you ORCHESTRATE:
10	//
11	//   click a car            → it arms (engine lit)
12	//   press SPACE            → every armed car launches at once (the green flag)
13	//   click a car mid-run    → spend a nitro charge, it surges (catch-up tool)
14	//   a car reaches its system → its work seals under a checkpoint (ice beam)
15	//   click the parked car   → `entire checkpoint explain <id>` is PRE-FILLED
16	//   read what it did       → ADD TO SHIP (Y) → system online
17	//   all five online        → type `entire dispatch` — the day writes itself
18	//
19	// Arm one and green-flag at a time = sequential. Arm all five, one green flag
20	// = parallel — the fast clear. Nothing forces parallel; the clock just rewards
21	// it. Cars IMPROVISE (a bent dish comes back as a signal spire); the transform
22	// is what makes you NEED the explanation.
23	
24	const TOTAL_TIME = 150;      // seconds for the whole run (tunable)
25	const BASE_SPEED = 1 / 14;   // lane progress per second (un-boosted ≈ 14s)
26	const BOOST_MULT = 2.6;      // nitro speed multiplier
27	const BOOST_DUR  = 2.2;      // seconds a nitro surge lasts
28	const NITRO_MAX  = 3;        // charges per run
29	const LOW_TIME = 45;         // clock turns urgent under this
30	const CRIT_TIME = 15;        // clock goes CRITICAL under this
31	const PANIC_TIME = 45;       // the SKY starts shifting toward panic-red
32	const MELT_DUR = 1.2;        // seconds the ice takes to melt on ADD TO SHIP
33	
34	// Starting grid — five parallel lanes fanning slightly out to the systems.

36	const FINISH_Z = -34;
37	const LANE = (i) => ({ startX: (i - 2) * 14, finishX: (i - 2) * 24 });

42	const FOG_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x4a0a0e);
43	const DOME_CALM = new THREE.Color(0x3a3168);
44	const DOME_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x7a141c);
45	const SUN_CALM  = new THREE.Color(0xfff1dc);
46	const SUN_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0xff5a3c);

115	function normalizeCmd(s) {
116	  return s.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
117	}

119	// ---------- prop builders: the broken systems ----------
120	const CHARRED = () => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x2a2e38, roughness: 0.9, metalness: 0.3 });
121	const SCORCH = () => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x3a3f4a, roughness: 0.85, metalness: 0.25 });
122	
123	function withWarnLight(g) {
124	  // A small red blinking bulb so broken things read as "broken" up close.
125	  const bulb = new THREE.Mesh(
126	    new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.22, 10, 10),
127	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x401512, emissive: 0xff3b2e, emissiveIntensity: 1.6 })
128	  );
129	  bulb.position.y = g.userData.warnY ?? 3.2;
130	  g.add(bulb);
131	  g.userData.warn = bulb;
132	  return g;
133	}
134	function buildBrokenCoils() {
135	  const g = new THREE.Group();
136	  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
137	    const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.1 - i * 0.18, 1.2 - i * 0.18, 1.0, 10), i ? CHARRED() : SCORCH());
138	    c.position.y = 0.5 + i * 1.05;
139	    c.rotation.z = (i % 2 ? -1 : 1) * 0.12 * (i + 1);   // the stack leans, sadly
140	    g.add(c);
141	  }
142	  g.userData.warnY = 3.6;
143	  return withWarnLight(g);
144	}
145	function buildBrokenNav() {
146	  const g = new THREE.Group();
147	  const ped = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.9, 1.2, 1.4, 8), SCORCH());
148	  ped.position.y = 0.7;
149	  const core = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1.5, 0), CHARRED());
150	  core.position.y = 2.6;
151	  core.rotation.set(0.4, 0.2, 0.5);
152	  g.add(ped, core);
153	  g.userData.warnY = 4.4;
154	  return withWarnLight(g);
155	}
156	function buildBrokenDish() {
157	  const g = new THREE.Group();
158	  const mast = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.18, 0.24, 3.2, 8), SCORCH());
159	  mast.position.y = 1.4;
160	  mast.rotation.z = 0.5;                                // bent over
161	  const dish = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.9, 1.5, 0.4, 12), CHARRED());
162	  dish.position.set(1.6, 0.5, 0);
163	  dish.rotation.z = 1.2;                                // face-down in the dirt
164	  g.add(mast, dish);
165	  g.userData.warnY = 3.0;
166	  return withWarnLight(g);
167	}
168	function buildBrokenVent() {
169	  const g = new THREE.Group();
170	  const box = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.6, 2.0, 2.0), SCORCH());
171	  box.position.y = 1.0;
172	  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
173	    const slat = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.2, 0.16, 0.1), CHARRED());
174	    slat.position.set(0, 0.6 + i * 0.5, 1.06);
175	    slat.rotation.x = 0.5;
176	    g.add(slat);
177	  }
178	  g.add(box);
179	  g.userData.warnY = 2.8;
180	  return withWarnLight(g);
181	}
182	function buildBrokenStrut() {
183	  const g = new THREE.Group();
184	  const leg = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.8, 4.2, 0.8), SCORCH());
185	  leg.position.set(0, 1.6, 0);
186	  leg.rotation.z = 0.55;                                // collapsed sideways
187	  const foot = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.4, 1.6, 0.5, 10), CHARRED());
188	  foot.position.set(1.6, 0.25, 0);
189	  g.add(leg, foot);
190	  g.userData.warnY = 3.4;
191	  return withWarnLight(g);
192	}

194	// ---------- prop builders: what the cars improvise them into ----------
195	function buildPlasmaRing() {
196	  const g = new THREE.Group();
197	  const base = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.2, 1.5, 0.7, 10), SCORCH());
198	  base.position.y = 0.35;
199	  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(
200	    new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.8, 0.3, 12, 36),
201	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x6b4a16, emissive: 0xffb86b, emissiveIntensity: 1.4, roughness: 0.4 })
202	  );
203	  ring.position.y = 3.4;
204	  g.add(base, ring);
205	  g.userData.anim = { type: "ring", ring };
206	  return g;
207	}
208	function buildStarDome() {
209	  const g = new THREE.Group();
210	  const ped = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.9, 1.2, 1.2, 8), SCORCH());
211	  ped.position.y = 0.6;
212	  const stars = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
213	  const pts = new Float32Array(120 * 3);
214	  for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {
215	    // random points in a dome above the pedestal
216	    const r = 1.2 + Math.random() * 1.4;
217	    const th = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
218	    const ph = Math.random() * Math.PI * 0.5;
219	    pts[i * 3] = Math.cos(th) * Math.sin(ph) * r;
220	    pts[i * 3 + 1] = 3.2 + Math.cos(ph) * r * 0.9;
221	    pts[i * 3 + 2] = Math.sin(th) * Math.sin(ph) * r;
222	  }
223	  stars.setAttribute("position", new THREE.BufferAttribute(pts, 3));
224	  const field = new THREE.Points(stars, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
225	    color: 0xbfe9ff, size: 0.16, transparent: true, opacity: 0.95,
226	    blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false,
227	  }));
228	  const orbit = new THREE.Mesh(
229	    new THREE.TorusGeometry(2.0, 0.05, 8, 40),
230	    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.7 })
231	  );
232	  orbit.position.y = 3.6;
233	  orbit.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2.4;
234	  g.add(ped, field, orbit);
235	  g.userData.anim = { type: "dome", field, orbit };
236	  return g;
237	}
238	function buildSignalSpire(beamTex) {
239	  const g = new THREE.Group();
240	  const spire = new THREE.Mesh(
241	    new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.5, 7.5, 8),
242	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x3a4150, metalness: 0.7, roughness: 0.3, emissive: 0x123a4a, emissiveIntensity: 0.8 })
243	  );
244	  spire.position.y = 3.75;
245	  const beam = new THREE.Mesh(
246	    new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.4, 0.4, 70, 10, 1, true),
247	    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
248	      map: beamTex, color: 0x9af0ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.55,
249	      blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide, fog: false,
250	    })
251	  );
252	  beam.position.y = 42;
253	  beam.visible = false;          // fires when the upgrade is ADDED
254	  g.add(spire, beam);
255	  g.userData.anim = { type: "spire", beam };
256	  return g;
257	}
258	function buildGardenPod() {
259	  const g = new THREE.Group();
260	  const tray = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.9, 2.1, 0.6, 12), SCORCH());
261	  tray.position.y = 0.3;
262	  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
263	    const plant = new THREE.Mesh(
264	      new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.28, 0.9 + (i % 3) * 0.35, 6),
265	      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1d4a2c, emissive: 0x35d97a, emissiveIntensity: 0.7, roughness: 0.7 })
266	    );
267	    const a = (i / 5) * Math.PI * 2;
268	    plant.position.set(Math.cos(a) * 1.0, 1.0, Math.sin(a) * 1.0);
269	    g.add(plant);
270	  }
271	  const domeGlass = new THREE.Mesh(
272	    new THREE.SphereGeometry(2.1, 18, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2),
273	    new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
274	      color: 0xd8ffe9, metalness: 0, roughness: 0.05, transmission: 0.7,
275	      thickness: 1.2, transparent: true, opacity: 0.4,
276	    })
277	  );
278	  domeGlass.position.y = 0.6;
279	  g.add(tray, domeGlass);
280	  g.userData.anim = { type: "pod" };
281	  return g;
282	}
283	function buildGravSkid() {
284	  const g = new THREE.Group();
285	  const pad = new THREE.Mesh(
286	    new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.7, 1.9, 0.5, 12),
287	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x2c3140, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.35, emissive: 0x123a4a, emissiveIntensity: 0.7 })
288	  );
289	  pad.position.y = 1.6;
290	  const glow = new THREE.Mesh(
291	    new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.5, 0.12, 8, 32),
292	    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.65 })
293	  );
294	  glow.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
295	  glow.position.y = 0.5;
296	  g.add(pad, glow);
297	  g.userData.anim = { type: "skid", pad, glow };
298	  return g;
299	}

301	// Level 3 places the finished upgrades on its own island (already online —
302	// the questions are about how they got that way). buildSignalSpire takes the
303	// shared beam texture; the rest take no args.
304	export const UPGRADE_BUILDERS = {
305	  buildPlasmaRing, buildStarDome, buildSignalSpire, buildGardenPod, buildGravSkid,
306	};
307	
308	// ---------- the little worker car ----------
309	function buildCar() {
310	  const g = new THREE.Group();
311	  const bodyMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
312	    color: 0x2c3140, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.35, emissive: 0x2a8aa6, emissiveIntensity: 0.55,
313	  });
314	  const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.1, 0.7, 3.4), bodyMat);
315	  body.position.y = 1.0;
316	  const nose = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.6, 0.5, 0.9), bodyMat);
317	  nose.position.set(0, 0.95, -2.0);
318	  const cabin = new THREE.Mesh(
319	    new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.4, 0.7, 1.5),
320	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x0c2030, emissive: 0x6fe3ff, emissiveIntensity: 0.55, metalness: 0.4, roughness: 0.3 })
321	  );
322	  cabin.position.set(0, 1.55, 0.1);
323	  const wheelGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.5, 0.5, 0.4, 12);
324	  const wheelMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x10141c, roughness: 0.8, metalness: 0.2 });
325	  const wheels = [];
326	  for (const dx of [-1.05, 1.05]) {
327	    for (const dz of [-1.15, 1.15]) {
328	      const w = new THREE.Mesh(wheelGeo, wheelMat);
329	      w.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
330	      w.position.set(dx, 0.5, dz);
331	      wheels.push(w);
332	      g.add(w);
333	    }
334	  }
335	  // Nitro flare — a glow under the car, lit only while boosting.
336	  const glow = new THREE.Mesh(
337	    new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2.8, 4.2),
338	    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
339	      color: 0xffb86b, transparent: true, opacity: 0, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false,
340	    })
341	  );
342	  glow.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
343	  glow.position.y = 0.12;
344	  g.add(body, nose, cabin, glow);
345	  g.userData = { bodyMat, cabin, glow, wheels };
346	  return g;
347	}
348	
349	// The ship console behind the grid (decoration / where the report fires).
350	function makeConsole() {
351	  const g = new THREE.Group();
352	  const pedestal = new THREE.Mesh(
353	    new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.6, 4.6, 1.6),
354	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1c2230, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.35 })
355	  );
356	  pedestal.position.y = 2.3;
357	  const screen = new THREE.Mesh(
358	    new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2.1, 1.3),
359	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
360	      color: 0x0c2030, emissive: 0x2a8aa6, emissiveIntensity: 1.4, roughness: 0.3,
361	    })
362	  );
363	  screen.position.set(0, 4.0, 0.82);
364	  screen.rotation.x = -0.28;
365	  g.add(pedestal, screen);
366	  return g;
367	}
368	
369	export function createDroneBayView(renderer, { onExit, onComplete, onNext, onNewGame } = {}) {
370	  const canvas = renderer.domElement;
371	
372	  // ---------- scene & sky ----------
373	  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
374	  scene.background = SKY_CALM.clone();
375	  scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(SKY_CALM.clone(), 120, 360);
376	
377	  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(
378	    60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 2000
379	  );
380	  // Fixed mission-control vantage: behind the grid, looking out at the systems.
381	  camera.position.set(0, 32, 104);
382	  camera.lookAt(0, 2, 4);
383	
384	  // ---------- lighting ----------
385	  scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xcdbcff, 0x3a2f5e, 0.7));
386	  const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(SUN_CALM, 1.5);
387	  sun.position.set(60, 90, 40);
388	  scene.add(sun);
389	  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x6a5a92, 0.3));
390	
391	  const dome = new THREE.Mesh(
392	    new THREE.SphereGeometry(900, 32, 16),
393	    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: DOME_CALM.clone(), side: THREE.BackSide, fog: false })
394	  );
395	  scene.add(dome);
396	
397	  // ---------- terrain (the same island) ----------
398	  const terrain = createTerrain({ size: 200, segments: 220, maxHeight: 26, seed: 1337 });
399	  scene.add(terrain.mesh, terrain.water);
400	
401	  const beamTex = makeBeamTexture();
402	
403	  // ---------- the five lanes: a car at the grid, a system at the finish ----------
404	  const BUILD = {
405	    buildBrokenCoils, buildBrokenNav, buildBrokenDish, buildBrokenVent, buildBrokenStrut,
406	    buildPlasmaRing, buildStarDome, buildGardenPod, buildGravSkid,
407	    buildSignalSpire: () => buildSignalSpire(beamTex),
408	  };
409	
410	  const lanes = SYSTEMS.map((sys, idx) => {
411	    const { startX, finishX } = LANE(idx);
412	    const start = new THREE.Vector3(startX, terrain.heightAt(startX, START_Z) + 0.6, START_Z);
413	    const finish = new THREE.Vector3(finishX, terrain.heightAt(finishX, FINISH_Z), FINISH_Z);
414	
415	    // The system (finish line).
416	    const anchor = new THREE.Group();
417	    anchor.position.copy(finish);
418	    const brokenModel = BUILD[sys.broken]();
419	    const upgradeModel = BUILD[sys.upgrade]();
420	    upgradeModel.visible = false;
421	    anchor.add(brokenModel, upgradeModel);
422	
423	    // Status beam: amber = waiting, cyan = car inbound, ice-blue = sealed.
424	    const beam = new THREE.Mesh(
425	      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.6, 0.6, 60, 12, 1, true),
426	      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
427	        map: beamTex, color: 0xff8a5c, transparent: true, opacity: 0.3,
428	        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide, fog: false,
429	      })
430	    );
431	    beam.position.y = 30;
432	    anchor.add(beam);
433	
434	    // The checkpoint seal: ice + floating trailer id, like Level 1's bank.
435	    const ice = makeIceBlock();
436	    ice.scale.setScalar(1.25);
437	    ice.position.y = 2.6;
438	    ice.visible = false;
439	    anchor.add(ice);
440	    const sprite = makeIdSprite(sys.ckpt);
441	    sprite.position.y = 8.4;
442	    sprite.visible = false;
443	    anchor.add(sprite);
444	    scene.add(anchor);
445	
446	    // The rail — two glowing lines sampled along the terrain so they don't clip.
447	    const railMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x35506a, transparent: true, opacity: 0.6 });
448	    const railL = new THREE.Line(railGeo(start, finish, -1.2, terrain), railMat);
449	    const railR = new THREE.Line(railGeo(start, finish, 1.2, terrain), railMat);
450	    scene.add(railL, railR);
451	
452	    // The car, parked at the grid.
453	    const car = buildCar();
454	    car.position.copy(start);
455	    car.rotation.y = Math.atan2(-(finish.x - start.x), -(finish.z - start.z));
456	    car.userData.laneIdx = idx;
457	    scene.add(car);
458	
459	    return {
460	      sys, idx, start, finish, anchor, brokenModel, upgradeModel, beam, ice, sprite,
461	      car, railL, railR, railMat,
462	      state: "staged",     // staged → running → sealed → added
463	      armed: false,
464	      prog: 0,             // 0..1 along the lane
465	      boostT: 0,           // remaining nitro time
466	      meltT: 0,            // ice melt after ADD TO SHIP
467	    };
468	  });
469	
470	  function railGeo(a, b, offX, terr) {
471	    const N = 24;
472	    const pts = [];
473	    for (let i = 0; i <= N; i++) {
474	      const k = i / N;
475	      const x = a.x + (b.x - a.x) * k + offX;
476	      const z = a.z + (b.z - a.z) * k;
477	      pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(x, terr.heightAt(x, z) + 0.25, z));
478	    }
479	    return new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts);
480	  }
481	
482	  // ---------- the ship console (behind the grid) ----------
483	  const consoleAnchor = new THREE.Group();
484	  const cz = START_Z + 12;
485	  consoleAnchor.position.set(0, terrain.heightAt(0, cz), cz);
486	  consoleAnchor.add(makeConsole());
487	  const consoleGlow = new THREE.PointLight(0xffd27a, 5, 28, 2);
488	  consoleGlow.position.y = 5;
489	  consoleAnchor.add(consoleGlow);
490	  scene.add(consoleAnchor);
491	
492	  // ---------- spark bursts (nitro / seal juice) ----------
493	  const sparks = [];
494	  function spawnSpark(pos, color = 0x9af0ff, n = 14) {
495	    const arr = new Float32Array(n * 3);
496	    const vel = [];
497	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
498	      arr[i * 3] = pos.x; arr[i * 3 + 1] = pos.y; arr[i * 3 + 2] = pos.z;
499	      vel.push(new THREE.Vector3((Math.random() - 0.5) * 9, Math.random() * 7, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 9));
500	    }
501	    const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
502	    geo.setAttribute("position", new THREE.BufferAttribute(arr, 3));
503	    const mat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
504	      color, size: 0.45, transparent: true, opacity: 1,
505	      blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false,
506	    });
507	    const pts = new THREE.Points(geo, mat);
508	    scene.add(pts);
509	    sparks.push({ pts, geo, mat, vel, life: 0, ttl: 0.7 });
510	  }
511	
512	  // ---------- HUD elements ----------
513	  const promptEl = document.getElementById("fp-prompt");
514	  const controlsEl = document.getElementById("fp-controls");
515	  const crosshair = document.getElementById("crosshair");
516	  const tutorialEl = document.getElementById("tutorial");
517	  const fpShared = document.getElementById("fp-shared");
518	  const termEl = document.getElementById("terminal");
519	  const termHint = document.getElementById("term-hint");
520	  const termInput = document.getElementById("term-input");
521	  const termList = document.getElementById("term-list");
522	  const termMsg = document.getElementById("term-msg");
523	  const termCta = document.getElementById("term-cta");
524	  const dbHud = document.getElementById("db-hud");
525	  const briefingEl = document.getElementById("db-briefing");
526	  const briefingStartBtn = document.getElementById("db-briefing-start");
527	  const countdownEl = document.getElementById("db-countdown");
528	  const countdownTime = document.getElementById("db-countdown-time");
529	  const systemsEl = document.getElementById("db-systems-rows");
530	  const winEl = document.getElementById("db-win");
531	  const winSub = document.getElementById("db-win-sub");
532	  const failEl = document.getElementById("db-fail");
533	
534	  let active = false;
535	  let started = false;
536	  let failed = false;
537	  let reportSent = false;       // `entire dispatch` has been run — the win
538	  let promptText = null;
539	  const taught = new Set();
540	  let tutorialTimer = null;
541	  let msgTimer = null;
542	  let winTimer = null;
543	  let nitro = NITRO_MAX;
544	
545	  // Level countdown — the single source of pressure for the whole run.
546	  let timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
547	  let timerRunning = false;
548	
549	  // Terminal state. Two modes share the one terminal:
550	  //  "review" — pre-filled `entire checkpoint explain <id>` at a sealed system
551	  //  "report" — type `entire dispatch` (the finish line)
552	  let terminalOpen = false;
553	  let termMode = null;
554	  let reviewSite = null;        // the sealed lane being reviewed
555	  let explained = false;        // Enter pressed — the card is showing
556	  let buffer = "";
557	  let reportDismissed = false;
558	
559	  const addedCount = () => lanes.filter((l) => l.state === "added").length;
560	  const allAdded = () => addedCount() >= lanes.length;
561	
562	  // ---------- raycasting (the mouse is free — click cars) ----------
563	  const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
564	  const ndc = new THREE.Vector2();
565	  let hoverLane = null;
566	
567	  function laneAtPointer(e) {
568	    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
569	    ndc.x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;
570	    ndc.y = -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;
571	    raycaster.setFromCamera(ndc, camera);
572	    const hits = raycaster.intersectObjects(lanes.map((l) => l.car), true);
573	    if (!hits.length) return null;
574	    let o = hits[0].object;
575	    while (o && o.userData.laneIdx === undefined) o = o.parent;
576	    return o ? lanes[o.userData.laneIdx] : null;
577	  }
578	
579	  // ---------- HUD helpers ----------
580	  function setPrompt(text) {
581	    if (!promptEl || text === promptText) return;
582	    promptText = text;
583	    if (text) { promptEl.textContent = text; promptEl.classList.remove("hidden"); }
584	    else promptEl.classList.add("hidden");
585	  }
586	  function showTutorial(text, ms = 5500) {
587	    if (!tutorialEl) return;
588	    clearTimeout(tutorialTimer);
589	    tutorialEl.textContent = text;
590	    tutorialEl.classList.remove("hidden");
591	    if (ms > 0) tutorialTimer = setTimeout(() => tutorialEl.classList.add("hidden"), ms);
592	  }
593	  function teachOnce(key, text, ms) {
594	    if (taught.has(key)) return;
595	    taught.add(key);
596	    showTutorial(text, ms);
597	  }
598	  function setControls() {
599	    if (!controlsEl) return;
600	    controlsEl.innerHTML = `
601	      <span class="control-item"><span class="control-label">Arm a car</span><span class="key key-wide">click</span></span>
602	      <span class="control-item"><span class="control-label">Launch armed</span><span class="key">Space</span></span>
603	      <span class="control-item"><span class="control-label">Nitro a runner</span><span class="key key-wide">click</span></span>
604	      <span class="control-item"><span class="control-label">Review a finish</span><span class="key key-wide">click</span></span>
605	      <span class="control-item"><span class="control-label">Return to orbit</span><span class="key">B</span></span>
606	    `;
607	    controlsEl.classList.remove("hidden");
608	  }
609	  function hideControls() { controlsEl?.classList.add("hidden"); }
610	
611	  // ---------- systems status panel ----------
612	  const STATE_LABEL = {
613	    staged: "STAGED", armed: "ARMED", running: "EN ROUTE", sealed: "READY TO REVIEW", added: "ONLINE",
614	  };
615	  function laneClass(l) {
616	    if (l.state === "staged") return l.armed ? "armed" : "staged";
617	    return l.state;
618	  }
619	  function updateSystems() {
620	    if (!systemsEl) return;
621	    const dots = Array.from({ length: NITRO_MAX }, (_, i) =>
622	      `<span class="db-nitro-dot${i < nitro ? "" : " is-spent"}"></span>`).join("");
623	    systemsEl.innerHTML =
624	      `<div class="db-nitro"><span class="db-nitro-label">NITRO</span><span class="db-nitro-dots">${dots}</span></div>` +
625	      lanes.map((l) => {
626	        const cls = laneClass(l);
627	        const label = cls === "staged" && l.armed ? STATE_LABEL.armed : STATE_LABEL[cls === "armed" ? "armed" : l.state];
628	        return `<div class="db-sys is-${cls}">` +
629	          `<span class="db-sys-name">${l.sys.name}</span>` +
630	          `<span class="db-sys-state">${label}</span></div>`;
631	      }).join("");
632	  }
633	
634	  // ---------- level countdown ----------
635	  function fmtTime(s) {
636	    s = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(s));
637	    return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
638	  }
639	  function updateClock() {
640	    if (countdownTime) countdownTime.textContent = fmtTime(timeLeft);
641	    countdownEl?.classList.toggle("is-low", timerRunning && timeLeft <= LOW_TIME);
642	    countdownEl?.classList.toggle("is-critical", timerRunning && timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME);
643	  }
644	  function panicFactor() {
645	    if (failed) return 1;
646	    if (!timerRunning || timeLeft > PANIC_TIME) return 0;
647	    let p = (PANIC_TIME - timeLeft) / PANIC_TIME;
648	    p *= p;
649	    if (timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME) {
650	      const throb = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 85);
651	      p += 0.14 * throb;
652	    }
653	    return Math.min(1, p);
654	  }
655	  function applyPanicSky() {
656	    const p = reportSent ? 0 : panicFactor();
657	    scene.background.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(SKY_PANIC, p);
658	    scene.fog.color.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(FOG_PANIC, p);
659	    dome.material.color.copy(DOME_CALM).lerp(DOME_PANIC, p);
660	    sun.color.copy(SUN_CALM).lerp(SUN_PANIC, p * 0.85);
661	  }
662	
663	  // ---------- briefing / win / fail / reset ----------
664	  function showBriefing() {
665	    timerRunning = false;
666	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
667	    briefingEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
668	  }
669	  function startLevel() {
670	    if (started) return;
671	    started = true;
672	    briefingEl?.classList.add("hidden");
673	    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
674	    timerRunning = true;
675	    updateClock();
676	    updateSystems();
677	    showTutorial("Click a car to arm it, then press SPACE to launch. Arm all five and launch together — that's the fast clear.", 9000);
678	  }
679	  briefingStartBtn?.addEventListener("click", startLevel);
680	
681	  function failLevel() {
682	    if (failed || reportSent) return;
683	    failed = true;
684	    timerRunning = false;
685	    closeTerminal(false);
686	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
687	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
688	    failEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
689	  }
690	  function resetLevel() {
691	    failed = false;
692	    reportSent = false;
693	    reportDismissed = false;
694	    nitro = NITRO_MAX;
695	    clearTimeout(winTimer);
696	    for (const l of lanes) {
697	      l.state = "staged";
698	      l.armed = false;
699	      l.prog = 0; l.boostT = 0; l.meltT = 0;
700	      l.car.position.copy(l.start);
701	      l.car.rotation.y = Math.atan2(-(l.finish.x - l.start.x), -(l.finish.z - l.start.z));
702	      l.car.userData.glow.material.opacity = 0;
703	      l.brokenModel.visible = true;
704	      l.upgradeModel.visible = false;
705	      const anim = l.upgradeModel.userData.anim;
706	      if (anim?.type === "spire") anim.beam.visible = false;
707	      l.ice.visible = false;
708	      l.ice.material.opacity = 0.62;
709	      l.sprite.visible = false;
710	    }
711	    failEl?.classList.add("hidden");
712	    winEl?.classList.add("hidden");
713	    updateSystems();
714	    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;
715	    timerRunning = true;
716	    updateClock();
717	    showTutorial("New attempt — arm the whole grid and drop one green flag.", 6000);
718	  }
719	
720	  // ---------- launch / boost ----------
721	  function toggleArm(l) {
722	    if (l.state !== "staged") return;
723	    l.armed = !l.armed;
724	    updateSystems();
725	    if (l.armed) teachOnce("armed", "Armed. Arm more, or press SPACE to send every armed car at once.", 7000);
726	  }
727	  function greenFlag() {
728	    let launched = 0;
729	    for (const l of lanes) {
730	      if (l.state === "staged" && l.armed) {
731	        l.state = "running"; l.armed = false; l.prog = 0; launched++;
732	      }
733	    }
734	    if (!launched) return;
735	    updateSystems();
736	    teachOnce("launched", "They're off — and improvising. Nitro a laggard by clicking it; you've got three charges.", 8000);
737	  }
738	  function boost(l) {
739	    if (l.state !== "running") return;
740	    if (nitro <= 0) { flashTerminalOff("out of nitro"); return; }
741	    if (l.boostT > 0) return;            // already surging
742	    nitro -= 1;
743	    l.boostT = BOOST_DUR;
744	    spawnSpark(l.car.position.clone(), 0xffb86b, 18);
745	    updateSystems();
746	  }
747	  function flashTerminalOff(text) {
748	    // a tiny tutorial-line nudge when there's no terminal to flash
749	    showTutorial(text, 1800);
750	  }
751	  function sealSite(l) {
752	    l.state = "sealed";
753	    l.brokenModel.visible = false;
754	    l.upgradeModel.visible = true;       // built — but dormant until ADD TO SHIP
755	    l.ice.visible = true;
756	    l.ice.material.opacity = 0.62;
757	    l.sprite.visible = true;
758	    l.car.userData.glow.material.opacity = 0;
759	    spawnSpark(l.anchor.position.clone().setY(l.anchor.position.y + 3), 0x8fe3ff, 16);
760	    updateSystems();
761	    teachOnce("sealed",
762	      "A car finished — its work is sealed under a checkpoint (ice-blue). Click the car to see what it actually did.", 8000);
763	  }
764	
765	  // ---------- terminal ----------
766	  function flashTerminal(text, ok) {
767	    if (!termMsg) return;
768	    clearTimeout(msgTimer);
769	    termMsg.textContent = text;
770	    termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
771	    termMsg.classList.add(ok ? "show-ok" : "show-err");
772	    msgTimer = setTimeout(() => termMsg.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err"), 3600);
773	  }
774	  function renderTerminal() {
775	    if (!terminalOpen) return;
776	    if (termMode === "review") {
777	      if (!explained) {
778	        termHint.textContent = "# what did the subagent actually do? ask the checkpoint";
779	        termInput.textContent = `entire checkpoint explain ${reviewSite.sys.ckpt}`;
780	        termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
781	        if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
782	          `<span class="cta-label">PRESS</span>` +
783	          `<kbd class="cta-key cta-key-yes">Enter</kbd><span class="cta-note">run it</span>`;
784	      } else {
785	        termHint.textContent = "# reviewed — your call";
786	        termInput.textContent = `entire checkpoint explain ${reviewSite.sys.ckpt}`;
787	        termInput.classList.add("is-dim");
788	        if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =
789	          `<button id="db-add-btn" type="button">ADD TO SHIP</button>` +
790	          `<span class="cta-note">or press <kbd class="cta-key cta-key-yes">Y</kbd></span>`;
791	      }
792	    } else if (termMode === "report") {
793	      termHint.textContent = reportSent
794	        ? "# dispatch sent — the day is on the record"
795	        : "# five jobs accepted — send the day's report";
796	      termInput.textContent = buffer;
797	      termInput.classList.remove("is-dim");
798	      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML = reportSent ? "" :
799	        `<span class="cta-label">TYPE</span><span class="cta-cmd">entire dispatch</span>`;
800	    }
801	  }
802	  function openTerminal(mode, site) {
803	    if (terminalOpen) return;
804	    terminalOpen = true;
805	    termMode = mode;
806	    reviewSite = site || null;
807	    explained = false;
808	    buffer = "";
809	    termMsg?.classList.remove("show-ok", "show-err");
810	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
811	    termEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
812	    renderTerminal();
813	  }
814	  function closeTerminal(dismiss) {
815	    if (!terminalOpen) return;
816	    if (dismiss && termMode === "report") reportDismissed = true;
817	    terminalOpen = false;
818	    termMode = null;
819	    reviewSite = null;
820	    explained = false;
821	    buffer = "";
822	    termEl?.classList.add("hidden");
823	    termList?.classList.add("hidden");
824	  }
825	
826	  // ---------- entire checkpoint explain (pre-filled, Enter to run) ----------
827	  function runExplain() {
828	    if (!reviewSite || explained) return;
829	    explained = true;
830	    const s = reviewSite;
831	    if (termList) {
832	      termList.innerHTML =
833	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">checkpoint</span>` +
834	        `<span class="tl-title">${s.sys.ckpt}</span></div>` +
835	        s.sys.card.map(([k, v]) =>
836	          `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">${k}</span>` +
837	          `<span class="tl-title">${v}</span></div>`
838	        ).join("");
839	      termList.classList.remove("hidden");
840	    }
841	    flashTerminal(`explained ${s.sys.ckpt} — ${s.sys.name.toLowerCase()} is now a ${s.sys.became}`, true);
842	    teachOnce("explained",
843	      "That's the story behind the checkpoint — work you didn't watch is never a mystery. If it's good, add it to the ship.", 8000);
844	    renderTerminal();
845	  }
846	
847	  // ---------- ADD TO SHIP ----------
848	  function addToShip() {
849	    if (!reviewSite || !explained) return;
850	    const s = reviewSite;
851	    s.state = "added";
852	    s.meltT = MELT_DUR;                 // the seal melts; the upgrade wakes up
853	    const anim = s.upgradeModel.userData.anim;
854	    if (anim?.type === "spire") anim.beam.visible = true;
855	    updateSystems();
856	    if (allAdded()) {
857	      // The fifth accept doesn't close the terminal — the finish line is the
858	      // next command at the prompt you're already at, like the real CLI.
859	      termMode = "report";
860	      reviewSite = null;
861	      explained = false;
862	      buffer = "";
863	      termList?.classList.add("hidden");
864	      flashTerminal("all five systems online", true);
865	      showTutorial("Every system accounted for — send the day's report: type `entire dispatch`.", 0);
866	      renderTerminal();
867	    } else {
868	      closeTerminal(false);
869	      showTutorial(`${s.sys.name} online (${addedCount()} / ${lanes.length}).`, 5000);
870	    }
871	  }
872	  // The ADD TO SHIP button (terminal CTA is re-rendered, so delegate the click).
873	  termCta?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
874	    if (!active) return;
875	    if (e.target?.id === "db-add-btn") addToShip();
876	  });
877	
878	  // ---------- the finish line: entire dispatch ----------
879	  function sendDispatch() {
880	    reportSent = true;
881	    timerRunning = false;
882	    countdownEl?.classList.remove("is-low", "is-critical");
883	    if (termList) {
884	      termList.innerHTML =
885	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">DISPATCH</span>` +
886	        `<span class="tl-title">drone bay — day report</span></div>` +
887	        lanes.map((l) =>
888	          `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">·</span>` +
889	          `<span class="tl-title">${l.sys.name.toLowerCase()} → ${l.sys.became} (${l.sys.ckpt.slice(0, 6)}…)</span></div>`
890	        ).join("") +
891	        `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-key tl-exp-key">filed</span>` +
892	        `<span class="tl-title">from 5 checkpoints · crew: 1 human, 5 subagents</span></div>`;
893	      termList.classList.remove("hidden");
894	    }
895	    flashTerminal("dispatch sent — look how much got done without you", true);
896	    renderTerminal();
897	    if (winSub) winSub.textContent = "five jobs in parallel — the report wrote itself";
898	    winTimer = setTimeout(() => {
899	      closeTerminal(false);
900	      tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
901	      winEl?.classList.remove("hidden");
902	    }, 4000);
903	    onComplete?.();               // Level 2 cleared — opens the launch window
904	  }
905	  function submitCommand() {
906	    const n = normalizeCmd(buffer);
907	    buffer = "";
908	    if (!n) { renderTerminal(); return; }
909	    if (/^entire dispatch$/.test(n)) { sendDispatch(); return; }
910	    if (/^entire (checkpoint|cp) list$/.test(n)) {
911	      if (termList) {
912	        termList.innerHTML = lanes.map((l) =>
913	          `<div class="term-list-row"><span class="tl-id tl-id-short">${l.sys.ckpt}</span>` +
914	          `<span class="tl-title">subagent fix: ${l.sys.name.toLowerCase()} → ${l.sys.became}</span></div>`
915	        ).join("");
916	        termList.classList.remove("hidden");
917	      }
918	      flashTerminal("the raw log — now turn it into the day's report:  entire dispatch", true);
919	      renderTerminal();
920	      return;
921	    }
922	    flashTerminal("command not recognized — try:  entire dispatch", false);
923	    renderTerminal();
924	  }
925	
926	  // ---------- input ----------
927	  function onCanvasClick(e) {
928	    if (!active || !started || failed || reportSent) return;
929	    if (terminalOpen) return;
930	    const l = laneAtPointer(e);
931	    if (!l) return;
932	    if (l.state === "staged") toggleArm(l);
933	    else if (l.state === "running") boost(l);
934	    else if (l.state === "sealed") openTerminal("review", l);
935	  }
936	  function onCanvasMove(e) {
937	    if (!active || !started || failed || terminalOpen) { hoverLane = null; return; }
938	    hoverLane = laneAtPointer(e);
939	    canvas.style.cursor = (hoverLane && hoverLane.state !== "added") ? "pointer" : "default";
940	  }
941	  function onKeyDown(e) {
942	    if (!active) return;
943	
944	    // Briefing is up — Enter/Space begins the run, nothing else.
945	    if (!started) {
946	      if (e.code === "Enter" || e.code === "Space") { startLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }
947	      return;
948	    }
949	
950	    // The clock ran out — R retries, N returns to the true beginning.
951	    if (failed) {
952	      if (e.code === "KeyR") { resetLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }
953	      if (e.code === "KeyN") { onNewGame?.(); e.preventDefault(); }
954	      return;
955	    }
956	
957	    // While the terminal is open, all keys feed it.
958	    if (terminalOpen) {
959	      if (e.code === "Escape") { closeTerminal(true); e.preventDefault(); return; }
960	      if (termMode === "review") {
961	        if (!explained && e.code === "Enter") { runExplain(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
962	        if (explained && (e.key === "y" || e.key === "Y")) { addToShip(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
963	        e.preventDefault();
964	        return;
965	      }
966	      // report mode — type the dispatch command (the finish line)
967	      if (reportSent) { e.preventDefault(); return; }
968	      if (e.code === "Enter") { submitCommand(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
969	      if (e.code === "Backspace") { buffer = buffer.slice(0, -1); renderTerminal(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
970	      if (e.key && e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {
971	        buffer += e.key; renderTerminal(); e.preventDefault();
972	      }
973	      return;
974	    }
975	
976	    // Mission control.
977	    if (reportSent && e.code === "Enter") { onNext?.(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
978	    if (e.code === "Space") { greenFlag(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
979	    if (e.code === "Enter" && allAdded() && !reportSent) {
980	      reportDismissed = false; openTerminal("report", null); e.preventDefault(); return;
981	    }
982	    if (e.code === "KeyB") { onExit?.(); e.preventDefault(); }
983	  }
984	
985	  // ---------- HUD per-frame ----------
986	  function refreshHud() {
987	    if (!started || terminalOpen) { setPrompt(null); return; }
988	    if (reportSent) { setPrompt("The day is dispatched — press Enter to board the ship"); return; }
989	    if (allAdded()) { setPrompt("All systems online — press Enter to send the day's report: `entire dispatch`"); return; }
990	    if (lanes.some((l) => l.state === "sealed")) {
991	      setPrompt("Ice-blue means finished work — click that car to review it");
992	      return;
993	    }
994	    if (lanes.some((l) => l.state === "running")) {
995	      setPrompt(`Cars en route — click one to spend nitro (${nitro} left)`);
996	      return;
997	    }
998	    if (lanes.some((l) => l.state === "staged" && l.armed)) {
999	      setPrompt("Armed and ready — press SPACE for the green flag");
1000	      return;
1001	    }
1002	    setPrompt("Click a car to arm it");
1003	  }
1004	
1005	  // ---------- per-frame ----------
1006	  function update(dt, t) {
1007	    // Level countdown — one clock for the whole run.
1008	    if (active && timerRunning && !failed && !reportSent) {
1009	      timeLeft = Math.max(0, timeLeft - dt);
1010	      updateClock();
1011	      if (timeLeft <= 0) failLevel();
1012	    }
1013	    applyPanicSky();
1014	
1015	    // Cars: stage idle, drive their lane, park at the finish.
1016	    for (const l of lanes) {
1017	      const car = l.car;
1018	      if (l.state === "staged") {
1019	        car.position.y = l.start.y + Math.sin((t + l.idx) * 2) * 0.05;
1020	        if (l.armed) {
1021	          const pulse = 0.55 + 0.45 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 6));
1022	          car.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = pulse;
1023	          car.userData.cabin.material.emissiveIntensity = pulse;
1024	        } else {
1025	          car.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = 0.4;
1026	          car.userData.cabin.material.emissiveIntensity = 0.45;
1027	        }
1028	      } else if (l.state === "running") {
1029	        if (l.boostT > 0) l.boostT = Math.max(0, l.boostT - dt);
1030	        const boosting = l.boostT > 0;
1031	        const speed = BASE_SPEED * (boosting ? BOOST_MULT : 1);
1032	        l.prog = Math.min(1, l.prog + speed * dt);
1033	        const k = l.prog;
1034	        const x = l.start.x + (l.finish.x - l.start.x) * k;
1035	        const z = l.start.z + (l.finish.z - l.start.z) * k;
1036	        car.position.set(x, terrain.heightAt(x, z) + 0.6, z);
1037	        const spin = (boosting ? 18 : 7) * dt;
1038	        for (const w of car.userData.wheels) w.rotation.x += spin;
1039	        car.userData.glow.material.opacity = boosting ? 0.55 + 0.25 * Math.sin(t * 30) : 0;
1040	        car.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = boosting ? 1.4 : 0.7;
1041	        if (boosting && Math.random() < dt * 12) {
1042	          spawnSpark(car.position.clone().setY(car.position.y + 0.4), 0xffb86b, 4);
1043	        }
1044	        if (l.prog >= 1) sealSite(l);
1045	      }
1046	
1047	      // hover lift on interactable cars
1048	      const interactable = l.state === "staged" || l.state === "running" || l.state === "sealed";
1049	      if (hoverLane === l && interactable) {
1050	        car.userData.cabin.material.emissiveIntensity = 1.1;
1051	      }
1052	    }
1053	
1054	    // Systems: status beams, warning blinks, seals, and live upgrades.
1055	    for (const l of lanes) {
1056	      if (l.state === "staged") {
1057	        l.beam.material.color.setHex(0xff8a5c);
1058	        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.24 + Math.sin(t * 1.6 + l.idx) * 0.08;
1059	        l.beam.visible = true;
1060	        const warn = l.brokenModel.userData.warn;
1061	        if (warn) warn.material.emissiveIntensity = Math.sin(t * 5 + l.idx) > 0 ? 1.8 : 0.2;
1062	      } else if (l.state === "running") {
1063	        l.beam.material.color.setHex(0x6fe3ff);
1064	        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.4 + Math.sin(t * 6) * 0.18;
1065	        l.beam.visible = true;
1066	        const warn = l.brokenModel.userData.warn;
1067	        if (warn) warn.material.emissiveIntensity = Math.sin(t * 5 + l.idx) > 0 ? 1.8 : 0.2;
1068	      } else if (l.state === "sealed") {
1069	        l.beam.material.color.setHex(0x8fe3ff);
1070	        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.42;
1071	        l.beam.visible = true;
1072	      } else {
1073	        l.beam.visible = false;       // added — the upgrade itself is the landmark
1074	        if (l.meltT > 0) {
1075	          l.meltT = Math.max(0, l.meltT - dt);
1076	          l.ice.material.opacity = 0.62 * (l.meltT / MELT_DUR);
1077	          if (l.meltT <= 0) l.ice.visible = false;
1078	        }
1079	        const anim = l.upgradeModel.userData.anim;
1080	        if (anim?.type === "ring") {
1081	          anim.ring.rotation.y += dt * 0.8;
1082	          anim.ring.position.y = 3.4 + Math.sin(t * 1.6) * 0.2;
1083	        } else if (anim?.type === "dome") {
1084	          anim.field.rotation.y += dt * 0.25;
1085	          anim.orbit.rotation.z += dt * 0.6;
1086	        } else if (anim?.type === "skid") {
1087	          anim.pad.position.y = 1.6 + Math.sin(t * 1.8) * 0.25;
1088	        }
1089	      }
1090	    }
1091	
1092	    // Spark bursts.
1093	    for (let i = sparks.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
1094	      const s = sparks[i];
1095	      s.life += dt;
1096	      const pos = s.geo.attributes.position.array;
1097	      for (let j = 0; j < s.vel.length; j++) {
1098	        s.vel[j].y -= 14 * dt;
1099	        pos[j * 3] += s.vel[j].x * dt;
1100	        pos[j * 3 + 1] += s.vel[j].y * dt;
1101	        pos[j * 3 + 2] += s.vel[j].z * dt;
1102	      }
1103	      s.geo.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
1104	      s.mat.opacity = Math.max(0, 1 - s.life / s.ttl);
1105	      if (s.life >= s.ttl) {
1106	        scene.remove(s.pts);
1107	        s.geo.dispose(); s.mat.dispose();
1108	        sparks.splice(i, 1);
1109	      }
1110	    }
1111	
1112	    consoleGlow.intensity = allAdded() && !reportSent ? 8 + Math.sin(t * 4) * 3 : 5;
1113	
1114	    // The report terminal opens itself once every system is online.

1117	        openTerminal("report", null);
1118	      }
1119	    }
1120	
1121	    if (active) refreshHud();

{
  "file_path": "/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/planetfall-seed-signalkit/src/droneBayView.js",
  "content": "import * as THREE from \"three\";\nimport { createTerrain } from \"./terrain.js\";\nimport { makeBeamTexture, makeIceBlock, makeIdSprite } from \"./memoryProps.js\";\n\n// LEVEL 2 (\"The Drone Bay\") — a dispatch grid, not a foot race.\n//\n// Five subagent cars wait in a bay. Five ship systems are dark out across the\n// island, each at the end of its own track. You don't run — you DISPATCH:\n//\n//   drag a car onto a track     → it loads at the starting line\n//   load as many as you dare     → one car, or all five\n//   press SPACE                  → the green flag launches every loaded car\n//   a track has a corruption patch → cars bog down in it and lose time\n//   click a bogged car           → spend a nitro charge, it punches through\n//   a car reaches its system      → its work seals under a checkpoint (ice)\n//   click the parked car          → `entire checkpoint explain <id>` pre-filled\n//   read what it did              → ADD TO SHIP (Y) → system online\n//   all five online               → type `entire dispatch` — the day writes itself\n//\n// The clock is the rescue run: it starts on the green flag and stops the moment\n// every car is home. Sequential dispatch bleeds it; a car left in the bay never\n// comes home; a severe patch left un-boosted stalls and crawls. Parallel +\n// smart nitro is the clear. Reviewing the work afterward is calm, untimed —\n// the lesson (explain) is never something you have to rush.\n\nconst TOTAL_TIME = 50;       // seconds for the rescue run (tunable)\nconst BASE_SPEED = 1 / 12;   // lane progress per second (clean lane ≈ 12s)\nconst BOOST_MULT = 2.6;      // nitro speed multiplier\nconst BOOST_DUR  = 2.2;      // seconds a nitro surge lasts\nconst NITRO_MAX  = 3;        // charges per run (fewer than the 5 cars — choose)\nconst HAZARD_SLOW = 0.30;    // speed multiplier inside a corruption patch\nconst HAZARD_FILL = 3.6;     // seconds in a severe patch before the car stalls\nconst STALL_CRAWL = 0.06;    // speed of a stalled car (basically stuck — boost it)\nconst LOW_TIME = 18;         // clock turns urgent under this\nconst CRIT_TIME = 8;         // clock goes CRITICAL under this\nconst PANIC_TIME = 22;       // the SKY starts shifting toward panic-red\nconst MELT_DUR = 1.2;        // seconds the ice takes to melt on ADD TO SHIP\n\n// Starting grid — five parallel lanes fanning slightly out to the systems,\n// plus a bay behind the line where the cars wait to be dispatched.\nconst START_Z = 64;\nconst FINISH_Z = -34;\nconst BAY_Z = START_Z + 26;\nconst LANE = (i) => ({ startX: (i - 2) * 14, finishX: (i - 2) * 24 });\nconst baySlotX = (i) => (i - 2) * 7;\n\n// Per-lane corruption patch: where on the track, how long, and whether it's bad\n// enough to stall a car. Two severe (must be boosted) + three mild (just slow).\nconst HAZARDS = [\n  { at: 0.34, len: 0.18, severe: true  },  // ignition coils\n  { at: 0.50, len: 0.10, severe: false },  // nav core\n  { at: 0.40, len: 0.18, severe: true  },  // antenna\n  { at: 0.58, len: 0.10, severe: false },  // life support\n  { at: 0.46, len: 0.12, severe: false },  // struts\n];\n\n// Sky panic palette — same dread as Level 1's clock.\nconst SKY_CALM  = new THREE.Color(0x2a2350);\nconst SKY_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x6e0f16);\nconst FOG_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x4a0a0e);\nconst DOME_CALM = new THREE.Color(0x3a3168);\nconst DOME_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x7a141c);\nconst SUN_CALM  = new THREE.Color(0xfff1dc);\nconst SUN_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0xff5a3c);\n\n// The five dark systems, what the cars improvise them into, and the story each\n// checkpoint carries. Ids are fixed so the fiction is stable across runs.\n// Exported: Level 3 quizzes the player on this exact record (and uses sys.pos\n// on its own island — left untouched here; this level lays out its own grid).\nexport const SYSTEMS = [\n  {\n    ckpt: \"a1c9e4f72b05\", name: \"IGNITION COILS\",\n    pos: { x: -34, z: -28 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenCoils\", upgrade: \"buildPlasmaRing\",\n    became: \"Plasma Ring\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-1\"],\n      [\"system\", \"ignition coils\"],\n      [\"did\", \"coils beyond saving — rebuilt as a plasma ring from salvaged hull plate\"],\n      [\"session\", \"2 attempts · parts scavenged: hull plate ×3\"],\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    ckpt: \"3d7b0f9c61ae\", name: \"NAV CORE\",\n    pos: { x: 40, z: -18 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenNav\", upgrade: \"buildStarDome\",\n    became: \"Star Dome\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-2\"],\n      [\"system\", \"nav core\"],\n      [\"did\", \"core unrecoverable — remapped from scratch; starfield calibrated to the lavender belt\"],\n      [\"session\", \"1 attempt · 412 stars plotted\"],\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    ckpt: \"f25c8b30d971\", name: \"LONG-RANGE ANTENNA\",\n    pos: { x: 28, z: 38 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenDish\", upgrade: \"buildSignalSpire\",\n    became: \"Signal Spire\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-3\"],\n      [\"system\", \"long-range antenna\"],\n      [\"did\", \"dish unsalvageable — respun the mast into a signal spire; relay handshake at full strength\"],\n      [\"session\", \"3 attempts · the first two fell over\"],\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    ckpt: \"62e0a9d4c8f3\", name: \"LIFE SUPPORT\",\n    pos: { x: -30, z: 36 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenVent\", upgrade: \"buildGardenPod\",\n    became: \"Garden Pod\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-4\"],\n      [\"system\", \"life support scrubbers\"],\n      [\"did\", \"filters dead — replaced with a living filter; two vines scavenged from the crash\"],\n      [\"session\", \"1 attempt · the vines approved\"],\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    ckpt: \"8b47f1e62da0\", name: \"LANDING STRUTS\",\n    pos: { x: 8, z: -44 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenStrut\", upgrade: \"buildGravSkid\",\n    became: \"Grav Skid\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-5\"],\n      [\"system\", \"landing struts\"],\n      [\"did\", \"strut seized solid — swapped for grav skids; technically the ship floats now\"],\n      [\"session\", \"2 attempts · torque spec: vibes\"],\n    ],\n  },\n];\n\nfunction normalizeCmd(s) {\n  return s.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\\s+/g, \" \");\n}\n\n// ---------- prop builders: the broken systems ----------\nconst CHARRED = () => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x2a2e38, roughness: 0.9, metalness: 0.3 });\nconst SCORCH = () => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x3a3f4a, roughness: 0.85, metalness: 0.25 });\n\nfunction withWarnLight(g) {\n  // A small red blinking bulb so broken things read as \"broken\" up close.\n  const bulb = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.22, 10, 10),\n    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x401512, emissive: 0xff3b2e, emissiveIntensity: 1.6 })\n  );\n  bulb.position.y = g.userData.warnY ?? 3.2;\n  g.add(bulb);\n  g.userData.warn = bulb;\n  return g;\n}\nfunction buildBrokenCoils() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  for (let i = 0; CHARRED() : SCORCH());\n    c.position.y = 0.5 + i * 1.05;\n    c.rotation.z = (i % 2 ? -1 : 1) * 0.12 * (i + 1);   // the stack leans, sadly\n    g.add(c);\n  }\n  g.userData.warnY = 3.6;\n  return const core = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1.5, 0), CHARRED());\n  core.position.y = 2.6;\n  core.rotation.set(0.4, 0.2, 0.5);\n  g.add(ped, core);\n  g.userData.warnY = 4.4;\n  return dish.rotation.z = 1.2;                                // face-down in the dirt\n  g.add(mast, dish);\n  g.userData.warnY = 3.0;\n  return withWarnLight(g);\n}\nfunction buildBrokenVent() {\n  const g leg.rotation.z = 0.55;                                // collapsed sideways\n  const foot = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.4, 1.6, 0.5, 10), CHARRED());\n  foot.position.set(1.6, 0.25, THREE.TorusGeometry(1.8, 0.3, 12, 36),\n    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x6b4a16, emissive: 0xffb86b, emissiveIntensity: 1.4, roughness: 0.4 })\n  );\n  ring.position.y = 3.4;\n  g.add(base, ring);\n  g.userData.anim = { type: \"ring\", ring };\n  return g;\n}\nfunction buildStarDome() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const ped = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.9, 1.2, 1.2, 8), SCORCH());\n  ped.position.y = 0.6;\n  const stars = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n  const pts = new Float32Array(120 * 3);\n  for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {\n    // random points in a dome above the pedestal\n    const r = 1.2 + Math.random() * 1.4;\n    const th = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n    const ph = Math.random() * Math.PI * 0.5;\n    pts[i * 3] = Math.cos(th) * Math.sin(ph) * r;\n    pts[i * 3 + 1] = 3.2 + Math.cos(ph) * r * 0.9;\n    pts[i * 3 + 2] = Math.sin(th) * Math.sin(ph) * r;\n  }\n  stars.setAttribute(\"position\", new }\n  const domeGlass = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.SphereGeometry(2.1, 18, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2),\n    new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({\n      color: 0xd8ffe9, metalness: 0, g.userData.anim = { type: \"skid\", pad, glow };\n  return g;\n}\n\n// Level 3 places the finished upgrades on its own island (already online —\n// the questions are about how they got that way). metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.35, emissive: 0x2a8aa6, emissiveIntensity: 0.55,\n  });\n  const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.1, 0.7, 3.4), bodyMat);\n  body.position.y = 1.0;\n  const 1.05]) {\n    for (const dz of [-1.15, 1.15]) {\n      const w = new THREE.Mesh(wheelGeo, wheelMat);\n      w.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;\n      w.position.set(dx, 0.5, dz);\n      wheels.push(w);\n      g.add(w);\n    }\n  }\n  // Nitro flare — a glow under the car, lit only while boosting.\n  const glow = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2.8, 4.2),\n    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n const disc = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(r, 28), mat);\n  disc.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;\n  const rimMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffae66, transparent: true, opacity: 0.85 ---------- the five lanes: a system + a corruption patch on each track ----------\n  const BUILD = {\n    buildBrokenCoils, buildBrokenNav, buildBrokenDish, buildBrokenVent, buildBrokenStrut,\n    buildPlasmaRing, buildStarDome, buildGardenPod, buildGravSkid,\n    buildSignalSpire: () => buildSignalSpire(beamTex),\n  };\n\n  function railGeo(a, b, offX, terr) {\n    const N = 24;\n    const pts = [];\n    for (let i = 0; i <= N; i++) {\n      const k = i / N;\n      const x = a.x + (b.x - a.x) * k + offX;\n      const z = a.z + (b.z - a.z) * k;\n      pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(x, terr.heightAt(x, z) + 0.25, z));\n    }\n    return new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts);\n  }\n\n  const lanes = SYSTEMS.map((sys, idx) => {\n    const { startX, finishX } = LANE(idx);\n    const start = new THREE.Vector3(startX, terrain.heightAt(startX, START_Z) + 0.6, START_Z);\n    const finish = new THREE.Vector3(finishX, terrain.heightAt(finishX, FINISH_Z), FINISH_Z);\n    const hazard = HAZARDS[idx];\n\n    // The system (finish line).\n    const anchor = new THREE.Group();\n    anchor.position.copy(finish);\n    const brokenModel = BUILD[sys.broken]();\n    const upgradeModel = BUILD[sys.upgrade]();\n    upgradeModel.visible = false;\n    anchor.add(brokenModel, upgradeModel);\n\n    // Status beam: amber = waiting, cyan = car inbound, ice-blue = sealed.\n    const beam = new THREE.Mesh(\n      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.6, 0.6, 60, 12, 1, true),\n      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n        map: beamTex, color: 0xff8a5c, transparent: true, opacity: 0.3,\n        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide, fog: false,\n      })\n    );\n    beam.position.y = 30;\n    anchor.add(beam);\n\n    // The checkpoint seal: ice + floating trailer id, like Level 1's bank.\n    const ice = makeIceBlock();\n    ice.scale.setScalar(1.25);\n    ice.position.y = 2.6;\n    ice.visible = false;\n    anchor.add(ice);\n    const sprite = makeIdSprite(sys.ckpt);\n    sprite.position.y = 8.4;\n    sprite.visible = false;\n    anchor.add(sprite);\n    scene.add(anchor);\n\n    // The rail — two glowing lines sampled along the terrain so they don't clip.\n    const railMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x35506a, transparent: true, opacity: 0.6 });\n    const railL = new THREE.Line(railGeo(start, finish, -1.2, terrain), railMat);\n    const railR = new THREE.Line(railGeo(start, finish, 1.2, terrain), railMat);\n    scene.add(railL, railR);\n\n    // The corruption patch, sitting on the track for the player to read before launch.\n    const hzFrac = hazard.at + hazard.len / 2;\n    const hx = start.x + (finish.x - start.x) * hzFrac;\n    const hzz = start.z + (finish.z - start.z) * hzFrac;\n    const hzMesh = buildHazard(hazard.severe);\n    hzMesh.position.set(hx, terrain.heightAt(hx, hzz) + 0.3, hzz);\n    scene.add(hzMesh);\n\n    return {\n      sys, idx, start, finish, hazard, anchor, brokenModel, upgradeModel, beam, ice, sprite,\n      railL, railR, railMat, hzMesh,\n      carObj: null,            // the car that's been dropped here (null when empty)\n      state: \"empty\",          // empty → loaded → running → sealed → added\n      prog: 0,                 // 0..1 along the lane\n      boostT: 0,               // remaining nitro time\n      corrupt: 0,              // 0..1 corruption fill in a severe patch\n      stalled: false,          // bogged down — needs a nitro to break free\n      hazardCleared: false,    // a nitro punched through the patch\n      meltT: 0, mesh, home, idx: i, state: \"bay\", lane: null };  // bay → loaded → running → done\n  });\n\n  // ---------- the ship console (behind the bay) ----------\n  const consoleAnchor = new THREE.Group();\n  const cz = BAY_Z + 12;\n  consoleAnchor.position.set(0, terrain.heightAt(0, cz), cz);\n  consoleAnchor.add(makeConsole());\n  const consoleGlow = new THREE.PointLight(0xffd27a, 5, 28, 2);\n  consoleGlow.position.y = 5;\n  consoleAnchor.add(consoleGlow);\n  scene.add(consoleAnchor);\n\n  // ---------- spark bursts (nitro / seal juice) ----------\n  const sparks = [];\n  function spawnSpark(pos, color = 0x9af0ff, n = 14) {\n    const arr = new Float32Array(n * 3);\n    const vel = [];\n    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n      arr[i * 3] = pos.x; arr[i * 3 + 1] = pos.y; arr[i * geo.setAttribute(\"position\", new THREE.BufferAttribute(arr, 3));\n    const mat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({\n      color, size: 0.45, transparent: true, opacity: 1,\n      blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false,\n    });\n    const pts = new THREE.Points(geo, mat);\n    scene.add(pts);\n    sparks.push({ pts, geo, mat, vel, life: 0, ttl: 0.7 });\n  }\n\n  // msgTimer = null;\n  let winTimer = null;\n  let nitro = NITRO_MAX;\n\n  // Level countdown — the rescue run. Starts on the green flag, stops when home.\n  let timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;\n  let timerRunning = false;\n  let clockStarted = false;\n\n  // Terminal state. Two modes share the one terminal:\n  //  \"review\" — pre-filled `entire checkpoint explain <id>` at a sealed system\n  //  \"report\" — type `entire dispatch` (the finish line)\n  let terminalOpen = false;\n  let termMode = null;\n  let reviewSite = null;        // the sealed lane being reviewed\n  let explained = false;        // Enter pressed — the card is showing\n  let buffer = \"\";\n  let reportDismissed = false;\n\n  const addedCount = () => lanes.filter((l) => l.state === \"added\").length;\n  const allAdded = () => addedCount() >= lanes.length;\n  const loadedCount = () => lanes.filter((l) => l.state === \"loaded\").length;\n  const anyRunning = () => lanes.some((l) => l.state === \"running\");\n  const anySealed = () => lanes.some((l) => l.state === \"sealed\");\n  const allHome = () => lanes.every((l) => l.state === \"sealed\" || l.state === \"added\");\n\n  // ---------- raycasting / drag ----------\n  const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();\n  const ndc = new THREE.Vector2();\n  const groundPlane = new THREE.Plane(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), 0);\n  let hoverCar = null;\n  let picked = null;            // car under the cursor at mousedown\n  let pickedDraggable = false;\n  let dragging = false;\n  let downX = 0, downY = 0;\n  let dropTarget = null;        // empty lane the dragged car is hovering over\n\n  function setNdc(e) {\n    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n    ndc.x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;\n    ndc.y = -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;\n  }\n  function carAtPointer(e) {\n    setNdc(e);\n    raycaster.setFromCamera(ndc, camera);\n    const hits = raycaster.intersectObjects(cars.map((c) => c.mesh), true);\n    if (!hits.length) return null;\n    let o = hits[0].object;\n    while (o && o.userData.carIdx === undefined) o = o.parent;\n    return o ? cars[o.userData.carIdx] : null;\n  }\n  function groundAt(e) {\n    setNdc(e);\n    raycaster.setFromCamera(ndc, camera);\n    const p = new THREE.Vector3();\n    return raycaster.ray.intersectPlane(groundPlane, p) ? p : null;\n  }\n  function nearestEmptyLane(p) {\n    if (!p) return null;\n    let best = null, bd = 1e9;\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      if (l.state !== \"empty\") continue;\n      const d = Math.abs(p.x - l.start.x);\n      if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = l; }\n    }\n    return (best && bd < 9 && p.z < BAY_Z - 10) ? best : null;\n  }\n\n  // ---------- HUD helpers ----------\n  function setPrompt(text) {\n    if (!promptEl || text === promptText) return;\n    promptText = text;\n    if (text) { promptEl.textContent = text; promptEl.classList.remove(\"hidden\"); }\n    else promptEl.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function showTutorial(text, ms = 5500) {\n    if (!tutorialEl) return;\n    clearTimeout(tutorialTimer);\n    tutorialEl.textContent = text;\n    tutorialEl.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    if (ms > 0) tutorialTimer = setTimeout(() => tutorialEl.classList.add(\"hidden\"), ms);\n  }\n  function teachOnce(key, text, ms) {\n    if (taught.has(key)) return;\n    taught.add(key);\n    showTutorial(text, ms);\n  }\n  function setControls() {\n    if (!controlsEl) return;\n    controlsEl.innerHTML = `\n      <span class=\"control-item\"><span class=\"control-label\">Drag car to track</span><span class=\"key key-wide\">drag</span></span>\n      <span class=\"control-item\"><span class=\"control-label\">Launch loaded</span><span class=\"key\">Space</span></span>\n      <span class=\"control-item\"><span class=\"control-label\">Nitro a runner</span><span class=\"key key-wide\">click</span></span>\n      <span class=\"control-item\"><span class=\"control-label\">Review a finish</span><span class=\"key key-wide\">click</span></span>\n      <span class=\"control-item\"><span class=\"control-label\">Return to orbit</span><span class=\"key\">B</span></span>\n    `;\n    controlsEl.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function hideControls() { controlsEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\"); }\n\n  // ---------- systems status panel ----------\n  const STATE_LABEL = {\n    empty: \"AWAITING CAR\", loaded: \"LOADED\", running: \"EN ROUTE\",\n    stalled: \"BOGGED\", sealed: \"READY TO REVIEW\", added: \"ONLINE\",\n  };\n  function laneClass(l) {\n    if (l.state === \"running\" && l.stalled) return \"stalled\";\n    if (l.state === \"empty\") return \"staged\";\n    if (l.state === \"loaded\") return \"armed\";\n    return l.state;\n  }\n  function updateSystems() {\n    if (!systemsEl) return;\n    const dots = Array.from({ length: NITRO_MAX }, (_, i) =>\n      `<span class=\"db-nitro-dot${i < nitro ? \"\" : \" is-spent\"}\"></span>`).join(\"\");\n    systemsEl.innerHTML =\n      `<div class=\"db-nitro\"><span class=\"db-nitro-label\">NITRO</span><span class=\"db-nitro-dots\">${dots}</span></div>` +\n      lanes.map((l) => {\n        const cls = laneClass(l);\n        const label = (l.state === \"running\" && l.stalled) ? STATE_LABEL.stalled : STATE_LABEL[l.state];\n        return `<div class=\"db-sys is-${cls}\">` +\n          `<span class=\"db-sys-name\">${l.sys.name}</span>` +\n          `<span class=\"db-sys-state\">${label}</span></div>`;\n      }).join(\"\");\n  }\n\n  // ---------- level countdown ----------\n  function fmtTime(s) {\n    s = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(s));\n    return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, \"0\")}`;\n  }\n  function updateClock() {\n    if (countdownTime) countdownTime.textContent = fmtTime(timeLeft);\n    countdownEl?.classList.toggle(\"is-low\", timerRunning && timeLeft <= LOW_TIME);\n    countdownEl?.classList.toggle(\"is-critical\", timerRunning && timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME);\n  }\n  function panicFactor() {\n    if (failed) return 1;\n    if (!timerRunning || timeLeft > PANIC_TIME) return 0;\n    let p = (PANIC_TIME - timeLeft) / PANIC_TIME;\n    p *= p;\n    if (timeLeft <= CRIT_TIME) {\n      const throb = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 85);\n      p += 0.14 * throb;\n    }\n    return Math.min(1, p);\n  }\n  function applyPanicSky() {\n    const p = reportSent ? 0 : panicFactor();\n    scene.background.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(SKY_PANIC, p);\n    scene.fog.color.copy(SKY_CALM).lerp(FOG_PANIC, p);\n    dome.material.color.copy(DOME_CALM).lerp(DOME_PANIC, p);\n    sun.color.copy(SUN_CALM).lerp(SUN_PANIC, p * 0.85);\n  }\n\n  // ---------- briefing / win / fail / reset ----------\n  function showBriefing() {\n    timerRunning = false;\n    tutorialEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    briefingEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function startLevel() {\n    if (started) return;\n    started = true;\n    briefingEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;\n    timerRunning = false;\n    clockStarted = false;\n    updateClock();\n    updateSystems();\n    showTutorial(\"Drag a subagent car from the bay onto a track. Load as many as you dare — then press SPACE for the green flag.\", 9000);\n  }\n  briefingStartBtn?.addEventListener(\"click\", startLevel);\n\n  function failLevel() {\n    if (failed || reportSent) return;\n    failed = true;\n    timerRunning = false;\n    closeTerminal(false);\n    countdownEl?.classList.remove(\"is-low\", \"is-critical\");\n    tutorialEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    failEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function resetLevel() {\n    failed = false;\n    reportSent = false;\n    reportDismissed = false;\n    nitro = NITRO_MAX;\n    clockStarted = false;\n    clearTimeout(winTimer);\n    for (const c of cars) {\n      c.state = \"bay\";\n      c.lane = null;\n      c.mesh.position.copy(c.home);\n      c.mesh.rotation.y = 0;\n      c.mesh.userData.glow.material.opacity = 0;\n    }\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      l.carObj = null;\n      l.state = \"empty\";\n      l.prog = 0; l.boostT = 0; l.corrupt = 0; l.meltT = 0;\n      l.stalled = false; l.hazardCleared = false;\n      l.brokenModel.visible = true;\n      l.upgradeModel.visible = false;\n      const anim = l.upgradeModel.userData.anim;\n      if (anim?.type === \"spire\") anim.beam.visible = false;\n      l.ice.visible = false;\n      l.ice.material.opacity = 0.62;\n      l.sprite.visible = false;\n    }\n    failEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    winEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    updateSystems();\n    timeLeft = TOTAL_TIME;\n    timerRunning = false;\n    updateClock();\n    showTutorial(\"New attempt — load the whole grid and drop one green flag.\", 6000);\n  }\n\n  // ---------- drag: load a car onto a track ----------\n  function beginDrag(car) {\n    // Picking up a loaded car frees its lane again.\n    if (car.state === \"loaded\" && car.lane) {\n      car.lane.carObj = null;\n      car.lane.state = \"empty\";\n      car.lane = null;\n    }\n    updateSystems();\n  }\n  function assignCar(car, lane) {\n    car.state = \"loaded\";\n    car.lane = lane;\n    lane.carObj = car;\n    lane.state = \"loaded\";\n    lane.prog = 0; lane.corrupt = 0; lane.stalled = false; lane.hazardCleared = false;\n    car.mesh.position.copy(lane.start);\n    car.mesh.rotation.y = Math.atan2(-(lane.finish.x - lane.start.x), -(lane.finish.z - lane.start.z));\n    teachOnce(\"loaded\", \"Loaded. Drop more cars on tracks, or press SPACE to send every loaded car at once.\", 7000);\n  }\n  function returnHome(car) {\n    car.state = \"bay\";\n    car.lane = null;\n    car.mesh.position.copy(car.home);\n    car.mesh.rotation.y = 0;\n  }\n\n  // ---------- launch / boost ----------\n  function greenFlag() {\n    let launched = 0;\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      if (l.state === \"loaded\" && l.carObj) {\n        l.state = \"running\";\n        l.carObj.state = \"running\";\n        l.prog = 0; l.corrupt = 0; l.stalled = false; l.hazardCleared = false;\n        launched++;\n      }\n    }\n    if (!launched) return;\n    if (!clockStarted) { clockStarted = true; timerRunning = true; }\n    updateSystems();\n    teachOnce(\"launched\", \"They're off — and improvising. Three charges, five cars.\", 8500);\n  }\n  function boost(l) {\n    if (!l || l.state !== \"running\") return;\n    if (nitro <= 0) { showTutorial(\"Out of nitro — that one rides it out.\", 1800); return; }\n    if (l.boostT > 0) return;            // already surging\n    nitro -= 1;\n    l.boostT = BOOST_DUR;\n    l.hazardCleared = true;              // a nitro punches clean through the patch\n    l.corrupt = 0;\n    l.stalled = false;\n    spawnSpark(l.carObj.mesh.position.clone(), 0xffb86b, 18);\n    updateSystems();\n  }\n  function sealSite(l) {\n    l.state = \"sealed\";\n    if (l.carObj) l.carObj.state = \"done\";\n    l.brokenModel.visible = false;\n    l.upgradeModel.visible = true;       // built — but dormant until ADD TO SHIP\n    l.ice.visible = true;\n    l.ice.material.opacity = 0.62;\n    l.sprite.visible = true;\n    if (l.carObj) l.carObj.mesh.userData.glow.material.opacity = 0;\n    spawnSpark(l.anchor.position.clone().setY(l.anchor.position.y + 3), 0x8fe3ff, 16);\n    updateSystems();\n    if (allHome()) timerRunning = false; // everyone's home — the run is survived\n    teachOnce(\"sealed\",\n      \"A car finished — its work is sealed under a checkpoint (ice-blue). Click the car to see what it actually did.\", 8000);\n  }\n\n  // ---------- terminal ----------\n  function flashTerminal(text, ok) {\n    if (!termMsg) return;\n    clearTimeout(msgTimer);\n    termMsg.textContent = text;\n    termMsg.classList.remove(\"show-ok\", \"show-err\");\n    termMsg.classList.add(ok ? \"show-ok\" : \"show-err\");\n    msgTimer = setTimeout(() => termMsg.classList.remove(\"show-ok\", \"show-err\"), 3600);\n  }\n  function renderTerminal() {\n    if (!terminalOpen) return;\n    if (termMode === \"review\") {\n      if (!explained) {\n        termHint.textContent = \"# what did the subagent actually do? ask the checkpoint\";\n        termInput.textContent = `entire checkpoint explain ${reviewSite.sys.ckpt}`;\n        termInput.classList.remove(\"is-dim\");\n        if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =\n          `<span class=\"cta-label\">PRESS</span>` +\n          `<kbd class=\"cta-key cta-key-yes\">Enter</kbd><span class=\"cta-note\">run it</span>`;\n      } else {\n        termHint.textContent = \"# reviewed — your call\";\n        termInput.textContent = `entire checkpoint explain ${reviewSite.sys.ckpt}`;\n        termInput.classList.add(\"is-dim\");\n        if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML =\n          `<button id=\"db-add-btn\" type=\"button\">ADD TO SHIP</button>` +\n          `<span class=\"cta-note\">or press <kbd class=\"cta-key cta-key-yes\">Y</kbd></span>`;\n      }\n    } else if (termMode === \"report\") {\n      termHint.textContent = reportSent\n        ? \"# dispatch sent — the day is on the record\"\n        : \"# five jobs accepted — send the day's report\";\n      termInput.textContent = buffer;\n      termInput.classList.remove(\"is-dim\");\n      if (termCta) termCta.innerHTML = reportSent ? \"\" :\n        `<span class=\"cta-label\">TYPE</span><span class=\"cta-cmd\">entire dispatch</span>`;\n    }\n  }\n  function openTerminal(mode, site) {\n    if (terminalOpen) return;\n    terminalOpen = true;\n    termMode = mode;\n    reviewSite = site || null;\n    explained = false;\n    buffer = \"\";\n    termMsg?.classList.remove(\"show-ok\", \"show-err\");\n    termList?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    termEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    renderTerminal();\n  }\n  function closeTerminal(dismiss) {\n    if (!terminalOpen) return;\n    if (dismiss && termMode === \"report\") reportDismissed = true;\n    terminalOpen = false;\n    termMode = null;\n    reviewSite = null;\n    explained = false;\n    buffer = \"\";\n    termEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    termList?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n  }\n\n  // ---------- entire checkpoint explain (pre-filled, Enter to run) ----------\n  function runExplain() {\n    if (!reviewSite || explained) return;\n    explained = true;\n    const s = reviewSite;\n    if (termList) {\n      termList.innerHTML =\n        `<div class=\"term-list-row\"><span class=\"tl-key tl-exp-key\">checkpoint</span>` +\n        `<span class=\"tl-title\">${s.sys.ckpt}</span></div>` +\n        s.sys.card.map(([k, v]) =>\n          `<div class=\"term-list-row\"><span class=\"tl-key tl-exp-key\">${k}</span>` +\n          `<span class=\"tl-title\">${v}</span></div>`\n        ).join(\"\");\n      termList.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    }\n    flashTerminal(`explained ${s.sys.ckpt} — ${s.sys.name.toLowerCase()} is now a ${s.sys.became}`, true);\n    teachOnce(\"explained\",\n      \"That's the story behind the checkpoint — work you didn't watch is never a mystery. you're already at, like the real CLI.\n      termMode = \"report\";\n      reviewSite = null;\n      explained = false;\n      buffer = \"\";\n      termList?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n      flashTerminal(\"all five systems online\", true);\n      showTutorial(\"Every system accounted for — send the day's report: type `entire dispatch`.\", 0);\n      renderTerminal();\n    } else {\n      closeTerminal(false);\n      showTutorial(`${s.sys.name} online (${addedCount()} / ${lanes.length}).`, 5000);\n    }\n  }\n  // The ADD TO SHIP button (terminal CTA is re-rendered, so delegate the ----------\n  function sendDispatch() {\n    reportSent = true;\n    timerRunning = false;\n    countdownEl?.classList.remove(\"is-low\", \"is-critical\");\n    if (termList) {\n      termList.innerHTML =\n        `<div class=\"term-list-row\"><span class=\"tl-key tl-exp-key\">DISPATCH</span>` +\n        `<span class=\"tl-title\">drone bay — day report</span></div>` +\n        lanes.map((l) =>\n          `<div class=\"term-list-row\"><span class=\"tl-key tl-exp-key\">·</span>` +\n          `<span class=\"tl-title\">${l.sys.name.toLowerCase()} → ${l.sys.became} (${l.sys.ckpt.slice(0, 6)}…)</span></div>`\n        ).join(\"\") +\n        `<div class=\"term-list-row\"><span class=\"tl-key tl-exp-key\">filed</span>` +\n        `<span class=\"tl-title\">from 5 renderTerminal();\n    if (winSub) winSub.textContent = \"five jobs in parallel — the report wrote itself\";\n    winTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n      closeTerminal(false);\n      tutorialEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n      winEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    }, 4000);\n    onComplete?.();               // Level 2 cleared — opens the launch window\n  }\n  function (/^entire (checkpoint|cp) list$/.test(n)) {\n      if (termList) {\n        termList.innerHTML = lanes.map((l) =>\n          `<div class=\"term-list-row\"><span class=\"tl-id tl-id-short\">${l.sys.ckpt}</span>` +\n          `<span class=\"tl-title\">subagent fix: ${l.sys.name.toLowerCase()} → ${l.sys.became}</span></div>`\n        ).join(\"\");\n        termList.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n      }\n      flashTerminal(\"the raw log — now turn it into the day's report:  entire dispatch\", true);\n      renderTerminal();\n      return;\n    }\n    flashTerminal(\"command not recognized — try:  entire dispatch\", false);\n    renderTerminal();\n  }\n\n  // ---------- input ----------\n  function onPointerDown(e) {\n    if (!active || !started || pickedDraggable = (picked.state === \"bay\" || picked.state === \"loaded\");\n  }\n  function onPointerMove(e) {\n    if (!active || !started || failed || terminalOpen) { canvas.style.cursor = hoverCar = c;\n    if (!c) { canvas.style.cursor = \"default\"; return; }\n    if (c.state === \"bay\" || c.state === \"loaded\") canvas.style.cursor = \"grab\";\n    else if (c.state === \"running\") onPointerUp(e) {\n    if (!picked) return;\n    if (dragging) {\n      const p = groundAt(e);\n      const lane = nearestEmptyLane(p);\n      if (lane) assignCar(picked, lane);\n      else
