onKeyDown(e) {\n    if (!active) return;\n\n    // Briefing is up — Enter/Space begins, nothing else.\n    if (!started) {\n      if (e.code === \"Enter\" || e.code === \"Space\") { startLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // The clock ran out — R retries, N returns to the true beginning.\n    if (failed) {\n      if (e.code === \"KeyR\") { resetLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      if (e.code === \"KeyN\") { onNewGame?.(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // While the terminal is open, all keys feed it.\n    if (terminalOpen) {\n      if (e.code === \"Escape\") { closeTerminal(true); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n      if (termMode === \"review\") {\n        if (!explained && e.code === \"Enter\") { runExplain(); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n        if (explained && (e.key === \"y\" || e.key === \"Y\")) { addToShip(); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n        e.preventDefault();\n        return;\n      }\n      // report mode — type the dispatch command (the finish line)\n      if (reportSent) { e.preventDefault(); return; }\n      if (e.code === \"Enter\") { submitCommand(); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n      if (e.code === \"Backspace\") { buffer = buffer.slice(0, -1); renderTerminal(); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n      if (e.key && e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {\n        buffer += e.key; renderTerminal(); e.preventDefault();\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Mission control.\n    if (reportSent && e.code === \"Enter\") { onNext?.(); e.preventDefault(); refreshHud() {\n    if (!started || terminalOpen) { setPrompt(null); return; }\n    if (reportSent) { setPrompt(\"The day is dispatched — press Enter to board the ship\"); return; }\n    if (allAdded()) { setPrompt(\"All systems online — press Enter to send the day's report: `entire dispatch`\"); return; }\n    if (anySealed() && allHome()) {\n      setPrompt(\"Everyone's home — click an ice-blue car to review what it did\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (anyRunning()) {\n      setPrompt(`Cars en route — click one bogged in a patch to nitro it (${nitro} left)`);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (loadedCount() > 0) {\n      setPrompt(`${loadedCount()} loaded — press SPACE for the green flag, or drag in more`);\n      return;\n    }\n    setPrompt(\"Drag a subagent car onto a track\");\n  }\n\n  // ---------- per-frame ----------\n  function update(dt, t) {\n    // Rescue-run countdown — only live between the green flag and everyone home.\n    if (active && timerRunning && !failed && !reportSent) {\n      timeLeft = Math.max(0, timeLeft - dt);\n      updateClock();\n      if (timeLeft <= 0) failLevel();\n    }\n    applyPanicSky();\n\n    // Cars in the bay — idle bob (skip the one being dragged).\n    for (const c of cars) {\n      if (c === picked && dragging) continue;\n      if (c.state === \"bay\") {\n        c.mesh.position.y = c.home.y + Math.sin((t + c.idx) * 2) * 0.05;\n        c.mesh.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = 0.4;\n        c.mesh.userData.cabin.material.emissiveIntensity = 0.45;\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Lanes: loaded cars idle at the line; l.idx) * 2) * 0.05;\n        const pulse = 0.55 + 0.45 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 6));\n        car.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = pulse;\n        car.userData.cabin.material.emissiveIntensity = pulse;\n      } else if (l.state === \"running\" && l.carObj) {\n        const car = l.carObj.mesh;\n        if (l.boostT > 0) l.boostT = Math.max(0, l.boostT - dt);\n        const hz = l.hazard;\n        const inHaz = !l.hazardCleared && l.prog >= hz.at && l.prog <= hz.at + hz.len;\n\n        // Severe patches corrupt and eventually stall a car that isn't boosted.\n        if (inHaz && hz.severe && l.boostT <= 0) {\n          l.corrupt = Math.min(1, l.corrupt + dt / HAZARD_FILL);\n          if (l.corrupt >= 1 && !l.stalled) {\n            l.stalled = true;\n            updateSystems();\n            teachOnce(\"stall\", \"A car bogged down in a corruption patch — click it to spend a nitro and break it free.\", 7000);\n          }\n        }\n\n        let mult = 1;\n        if (l.boostT > 0) mult = BOOST_MULT;\n        else if (inHaz) mult = l.stalled ? STALL_CRAWL : HAZARD_SLOW;\n\n        l.prog = Math.min(1, l.prog + BASE_SPEED * mult * dt);\n        const k = l.prog;\n        const x = l.start.x + (l.finish.x - l.start.x) * k;\n        const z = l.start.z + (l.finish.z - l.start.z) * k;\n        car.position.set(x, terrain.heightAt(x, z) + 0.6, z);\n\n        const boosting = l.boostT > 0;\n        const spin = (boosting ? 18 : mult < 0.5 ? 2 : 7) * dt;\n        for (const w of car.userData.wheels) w.rotation.x += spin;\n        car.userData.glow.material.opacity = boosting ? 0.55 + 0.25 * Math.sin(t * 30) : 0;\n        car.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = boosting ? 1.4 : l.stalled ? 0.3 : 0.7;\n        if (boosting && Math.random() < dt * 12) {\n          spawnSpark(car.position.clone().setY(car.position.y + 0.4), 0xffb86b, 4);\n        }\n        if (l.prog >= 1) sealSite(l);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // hover lift on an interactable car\n    if (hoverCar && !dragging) {\n      const c = hoverCar;\n      const interactable = c.state === \"bay\" || c.state === \"loaded\" || c.state === \"running\" ||\n        (c.state === \"done\" && c.lane?.state === \"sealed\");\n      if (interactable) c.mesh.userData.cabin.material.emissiveIntensity = 1.1;\n    }\n\n    // Corruption patches: pulse, brighten while a car is suffering, fade when cleared.\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      const hm = l.hzMesh;\n      const ud = hm.userData;\n      if (l.state === \"added\" || l.hazardCleared) {\n        // cleared / done — drain to a faint teal scar\n        ud.mat.color.lerp(new THREE.Color(0x2a8aa6), 0.08);\n        ud.mat.opacity = Math.max(0.05, ud.mat.opacity - dt * 0.6);\n        ud.rimMat.opacity = Math.max(0.1, ud.rimMat.opacity - dt * 0.8);\n      } else {\n        const hot = l.state === \"running\" && (l.stalled || l.corrupt > 0);\n        const base = ud.severe ? 0.42 : 0.3;\n        ud.mat.opacity = base + Math.sin(t * 3 + l.idx) * 0.08 + (hot ? 0.25 : 0);\n        ud.rimMat.opacity = 0.8 + (hot ? 0.2 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 12)) : 0);\n        const sc = 1 + (hot ? 0.06 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 10)) : 0);\n        hm.scale.setScalar(sc);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Drop-target highlight: brighten the rails of the lane under a dragged car.\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      l.railMat.color.setHex(l === dropTarget ? 0x6fe3ff : 0x35506a);\n      l.railMat.opacity = l === dropTarget ? 0.95 : 0.6;\n    }\n\n    // Systems: status beams, warning blinks, seals, and live upgrades.\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      if (l.state === \"empty\" || l.state === \"loaded\") {\n        l.beam.material.color.setHex(l.state === \"loaded\" ? 0x6fe3ff : 0xff8a5c);\n        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.24 + Math.sin(t * 1.6 + l.idx) * 0.08;\n        l.beam.visible = true;\n        const warn = l.brokenModel.userData.warn;\n        if (warn) warn.material.emissiveIntensity = Math.sin(t * 5 + l.idx) > 0 ? 1.8 : 0.2;\n      } else if (l.state === \"running\") {\n        l.beam.material.color.setHex(l.stalled ? 0xff5a3c : 0x6fe3ff);\n        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.4 + Math.sin(t * 6) * 0.18;\n        l.beam.visible = true;\n        const warn = l.brokenModel.userData.warn;\n        if (warn) warn.material.emissiveIntensity = Math.sin(t * 5 + l.idx) > 0 ? 1.8 : 0.2;\n      } else if (l.state === \"sealed\") {\n        l.beam.material.color.setHex(0x8fe3ff);\n        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.42;\n        l.beam.visible = true;\n      } else {\n        l.beam.visible = false;       // added — the upgrade itself is the landmark\n        if (l.meltT > 0) {\n          l.meltT = Math.max(0, l.meltT - dt);\n          l.ice.material.opacity = 0.62 * (l.meltT / MELT_DUR);\n          if (l.meltT <= 0) l.ice.visible = false;\n        }\n        const anim = l.upgradeModel.userData.anim;\n        if (anim?.type === \"ring\") {\n          anim.ring.rotation.y += dt * 0.8;\n          anim.ring.position.y = 3.4 + Math.sin(t * 1.6) * 0.2;\n        } else if (anim?.type === \"dome\") {\n          anim.field.rotation.y += dt * 0.25;\n          anim.orbit.rotation.z += dt * 0.6;\n        } else if (anim?.type === \"skid\") {\n          anim.pad.position.y = 1.6 + Math.sin(t * 1.8) * 0.25;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Spark bursts.\n    for (let i = sparks.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n      const s = sparks[i];\n      s.life += dt;\n      const pos = s.geo.attributes.position.array;\n      for (let j = 0; j < s.vel.length; j++) {\n        s.vel[j].y -= 14 * dt;\n        pos[j * 3] += s.vel[j].x * dt;\n        pos[j * 3 + 1] += s.vel[j].y * dt;\n        pos[j * 3 + 2] += s.vel[j].z * dt;\n      }\n      s.geo.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;\n      s.mat.opacity = Math.max(0, 1 - s.life / s.ttl);\n      if (s.life >= s.ttl) {\n        scene.remove(s.pts);\n        s.geo.dispose(); s.mat.dispose();\n        sparks.splice(i, 1);\n      }\n    }\n\n    consoleGlow.intensity = allAdded() && !reportSent ? 8 + Math.sin(t * 4) * 3 : 5;\n\n    // The report terminal opens itself once every system is online.\n    if (active && started && !failed) {\n      if (!terminalOpen && allAdded() && clearTimeout(winTimer);\n    canvas.removeEventListener(\"mousedown\", onPointerDown);\n    canvas.removeEventListener(\"mousemove\", onPointerMove);\n    window.removeEventListener(\"mouseup\", Conclusion: parallel \"chaos\" tension MUST come from a per-lane failure stake, not the clock. Agreed direction with Rizel: **true drag + generic hazards** (rejected specialty-matching cars/Decoder/Repair — it's the \"drone management\" she already killed, blows the 15s onboarding, and telegraphs outcomes which guts the explain payoff). Built in droneBayView.js: cars now wait in a BAY (BAY_Z=START_Z+26=90), tracks start EMPTY. Verbs: **drag** a car bay→track (loads at line), **Space** green-flags all loaded, **click** a bogged running car to nitro through. Decoupled car pool (`cars[]`, state bay→loaded→running→done) from `lanes[]` (state empty→loaded→running→sealed→added); lane.carObj links them. Drag = mousedown/mousemove(>5px)/mouseup, ground-plane raycast for position, nearestEmptyLane (|Δx|<9 & z<BAY_Z-10) for drop; click vs drag disambiguated by movement. Each lane has a corruption patch (HAZARDS[]: 2 severe + 3 mild, staggered): inside, speed×HAZARD_SLOW(0.30); SEVERE lanes also fill `corrupt` over HAZARD_FILL(3.6s) → STALL (crawl at STALL_CRAWL 0.06, basically stuck) unless boosted; boost (nitro--) clears the patch + un-stalls. NITRO_MAX=3 < 5 cars = the triage choice (cover the 2 severe). **Clock is now the RESCUE RUN**: starts on first green flag (clockStarted), stops the moment allHome() (every lane sealed/added) — so reviewing explanations is calm/untimed (protects the lesson). TOTAL_TIME=50, BASE_SPEED=1/12, LOW_TIME=18/CRIT=8/PANIC=22 — all tunable, NOT yet playtested. Fail modes that create tension: sequential bleeds the clock, a car left in the bay never homes (clock kills you), wasting nitro on mild lanes → severe stalls → fail. Hazard discs (buildHazard) sit on tracks so you can read severity before launch; drop-target lane rails brighten cyan while dragging; is-stalled CSS row = red blink \"BOGGED\". > AGENT

Built and building clean. Here's what's now in Level 2:

**The dispatch grid is live.** Cars start in a bay at the bottom; tracks are empty.

- **Drag** a car from the bay onto a track → it loads at the line. Load one, or all five — that's your sequential-vs-parallel dare, now physical.
- **Space** = green flag, launches everything loaded.
- Every track has a **corruption patch**. Mild ones just slow you. The **two severe ones fill a corruption meter and stall the car** (it crawls, basically stuck) unless you **click it to spend a nitro** and punch through. Three charges, five cars — so you choose who gets rescued.
- Cars seal → click to review (`explain` → ADD TO SHIP) → `entire dispatch`.

**The key change that fixes "no tension":** the clock is now a **rescue run** — it starts on the green flag and *stops the moment every car is home*. That matters because of the math: in parallel, the slowest lane sets the finish time, so an aggregate clock can never threaten a parallel player (boosting the slowest barely moves it). The real stakes had to be **per-lane** — so the severe patches stalling is what creates the "boost it, boost it!" scramble, while the clock punishes the things it's actually good at punishing: dispatching sequentially, dawdling, or leaving a car in the bay (it never comes home → you fail). And because the clock stops once they're all home, **reviewing the explanations stays calm** — the lesson never gets rushed.

**Untuned on purpose** — like you did with the L1 timer, these want a feel pass:
- `TOTAL_TIME = 50` (rescue window)
- `HAZARD_FILL = 3.6` (seconds before a severe lane stalls)
- `NITRO_MAX = 3`, `HAZARD_SLOW = 0.30`, `BASE_SPEED = 1/12`

Run `npm run dev` and feel it. Two things to watch for specifically: (1) does the drag feel good with the fixed camera, or fiddly — that's the part I was most unsure about; (2) are the two severe patches *readable as dangerous* before launch, or do you only learn by getting stalled?

260	  function restartTextAnimation(el) {
261	    if (!el) return;
262	    el.classList.remove("beat-in");
263	    void el.offsetWidth;
264	    el.classList.add("beat-in");
265	  }
266	  function renderBriefingBeat() {
267	    if (!briefingText) return;
268	    briefingText.textContent = BRIEFING_BEATS[briefingIndex] || "";
269	    restartTextAnimation(briefingText);
270	    if (briefingNext) briefingNext.textContent = "to continue";
271	  }
272	  function advanceBriefing() {
273	    if (started) return;
274	    if (briefingIndex < BRIEFING_BEATS.length - 1) {
275	      briefingIndex += 1;
276	      renderBriefingBeat();
277	      return;
278	    }
279	    showModePrompt("tutorial");
280	  }
281	  function showModePrompt(kind) {
282	    const prompt = MODE_PROMPTS[kind];
283	    if (!prompt || !modePrompt) return;
284	    modePromptState = kind;
285	    timerRunning = false;
286	    tutorialEl?.classList.add("hidden");
287	    hideBankLesson();
288	    if (!prompt.keepTerminal) closeTerminal();   // "next" keeps the haul list up
289	    briefingEl?.classList.add("hidden");
290	    if (modeHead) {
291	      modeHead.textContent = prompt.head || "";
292	      modeHead.classList.toggle("hidden", !prompt.head);
293	    }
294	    if (modeAction) modeAction.textContent = prompt.action;
295	    if (modeNote) modeNote.textContent = prompt.note;
296	    modePrompt.dataset.mode = kind;
297	    // Handoff keeps the haul list up, so top-anchor the card to clear it.
298	    modePrompt.classList.toggle("is-handoff", !!prompt.keepTerminal);
299	    modePrompt.classList.remove("hidden");

> AGENT

Done. Level 2's onboarding is now the same style as Level 1 — big, short beats you tap through with Space, instead of the wall-of-text card. Three beats:

1. **"These are your subagents. Hand them work and it gets done — faster when they all run at once."**
2. **"Drag one onto a track to send it off. Send as many as you like, then press Space."**
3. **"You won't watch them work. To see what a subagent did, run: entire checkpoint explain"**

That's the whole pitch you described: subagents do the work (in parallel = faster) → drag them onto a track to dispatch → you don't see what they did, so you check with `entire checkpoint explain`. The corruption-patch/nitro detail is dropped from the intro entirely — it now surfaces as an in-game nudge the first time a car bogs down, so the onboarding stays ~15 seconds.

Same banner widget, kicker, and "Space to continue" cue as L1, so it reads consistent. Builds clean — reload and step through it.

One leftover: the old `#db-briefing-start` button CSS is now dead (harmless, matches nothing). Want me to strip it, or leave it?

> DEVELOPER

lets keep working on it. it needs to show what the problem is..how they solve it..and i dont want it to be long..if it can be like 2 stories that would be great. no emdashes..let's discuss options before you implement

> AGENT

Good direction. Two beats, problem then solution, no em-dashes. The real tension in fitting it to two beats is how much *mechanic* to cram in. Three things compete for that second beat: the verb (drag to send), the parallel idea, and the `explain` command. All three in one line gets dense fast.

My instinct: beat 1 = the problem, beat 2 = subagents solve it in parallel + the `explain` payoff, and let **"drag onto a track"** be taught by the live nudge the moment the grid appears (a verb lands better at the moment you do it than read in advance). That keeps both beats short.

Here are three flavors to react to:

**Option A — plain story**
1. `The ship is grounded. Five of its systems are dark, and you cannot fix them all yourself.`
2. `But your subagents can. They take the work and run it in parallel, then show you what they did with entire checkpoint explain.`

**Option B — frames it as delegation (closest to the real lesson)**
1. `There is more here to fix than you could ever do alone.`
2. `So delegate. Your subagents work in parallel, and entire checkpoint explain shows you what each one did.`

**Option C — punchy / minimal**
1. `Too much is broken. One of you.`
2. `So send your subagents. They fix it in parallel, and entire checkpoint explain tells you how.`

A couple of things I want your read on before I write it:

- **Does the `explain` command belong in beat 2, or should that also wait for the live nudge** (it already fires when a car finishes: "click the car to see what it did")? Dropping it makes beat 2 just "subagents fix it in parallel," which is the cleanest possible two-beat. But you mentioned wanting `explain` named up front, so I left it in above.
- **Tone**: A reads like the game's existing briefings, C is more clipped/dramatic. B best reinforces the actual concept (delegation + review).

Which one's closest, and do you want `explain` in the beat or left to the nudge?

> DEVELOPER

the first ones you give for all the options give no value. we dont want to waste time.

> AGENT

Got it. The scene-setting first beat is dead weight. Both beats should teach. Let me make beat 1 carry the actual concept (the problem it solves) and beat 2 carry the actions.

**Option A**
1. `You have more to fix than time to fix it. Subagents do the work for you, several at once.`
2. `Drag one onto a track to send it off. When it finishes, run entire checkpoint explain to see what it did.`

**Option B** (value-forward)
1. `Subagents do your work in parallel, so five jobs take about the time of one.`
2. `Drag one onto a track to send it off, then run entire checkpoint explain to see what it did.`

**Option C** (problem as a question)
1. `Too much to fix alone? Subagents take the work and run it all at once.`
2. `Drag them onto the tracks to send them, then check the result with entire checkpoint explain.`

Every line now does a job: beat 1 = the problem + why subagents matter (parallel = fast), beat 2 = the two verbs (send, then `explain`). Drag moves into beat 2 here instead of a live nudge, since you wanted both beats to pull weight.

B is the tightest and most concept-true (it states the parallel payoff as a number-ish fact). A spells the problem out more plainly. Which one, or want me to blend?

> DEVELOPER

i fixed the view a little bit but it's still not great..i think i need the island to be bigger..i wanna feel like im on the island for level 2..i feel like i cant really see the beams or the hazards. i feel like there needs to be even more tracks but maybe less cars and maybe the cars can come back and reuse some of them..u get me..like maybe i have like 12 tracks?? and then just 6 cars..idk so theres two things the view/visibility and then amping up the multitasking with more tracks

> AGENT

Two separate problems, and they actually pull on each other, so let me take them together.

## 1. Visibility / "on the island"

The root cause: one fixed camera has to frame the whole strip from the bay (front) to the systems (way back), so everything is far and tiny. And there's a geometry conflict baked in:

- **Beams are vertical.** They read best from a *low, near-horizontal* camera (tall columns of light). Our current high angle squashes them.
- **Hazards are flat discs on the ground.** Those read best from *above*. A low camera turns them into thin ellipses you can barely see.

So "lower the camera to feel on the island" actively *hurts* the flat hazards. The fix is to stop fighting it: drop the camera lower and closer for presence, **make the hazards volumetric** (a crackling dome / rising corruption field instead of a floor decal) so they read at a low angle, and make the beams taller/brighter. "Bigger island" is really a camera-distance feeling more than terrain size (the terrain's already 200 wide; we just see a sliver) so we can also sprinkle props near the tracks to sell presence.

But here's the thing: **if you also want way more tracks, no single fixed angle can show 12 tracks readably.** That forces the real answer:

**A light mission-control camera you can pan and zoom** (drag to look, scroll to zoom). Lean in to watch a car fight through a hazard, pull back to see the whole board. This is *not* the first-person walking you killed in v1, it's an RTS-style eye over a fixed scene. I think the more-tracks ambition basically requires it. Are you good with that, or do you want to stay strictly no-controls?

## 2. More tracks, fewer cars, reuse

I like it, it amps the dispatch fantasy into a real throughput loop: a pool of cars smaller than the jobs, so you send → they finish and drive home → you re-send to the next open track, racing the clock. Cars become a scarce resource you allocate (and nitro helps a bogged one). That's much more multitasking than 5-cars-5-lanes-once.

Two collisions to decide before I build it:

**a) The review phase doesn't scale.** Right now every job makes you run `explain` and ADD TO SHIP, which is what makes reading non-skippable. Do that 12 times and it's a slog. Two ways out:
   - **Keep a few "hero" jobs** (the 5 curated systems with rich explain cards) that you must review, and the *extra* tracks are quick generic repairs that just need a car, no deep review.
   - **Shift the model**: review becomes a *spot-check* (open `explain` on any car you're curious about, optional), and one `entire dispatch` at the end summarizes *all* of it. This actually mirrors reality better (you don't read every agent's work, you sample and let the summary roll it up) and it's what unlocks 10-12 tracks without tedium. Cost: `explain` stops being a forced gate.

**b) Level 3 quizzes the 5 curated systems.** More jobs means inventing more than 5. Cleanest: keep the 5 hero systems for story + L3, add generic "minor repair" tracks on top for the throughput.

My lean: light pan/zoom camera + volumetric hazards, ~6 cars over ~9 tracks (5 hero + 4 generic), cars auto-return after sealing, and the **spot-check + dispatch-summarizes-all** review model. That gets you the busy, can't-do-it-alone feeling and keeps the end clean.

Before I touch code: (1) camera control yes/no, and (2) review as forced-gate-on-a-few vs spot-check-plus-summary? 

(Also parking the onboarding copy from before, we'll lock that after this.)

> DEVELOPER

no leave level 3 alone.and why doesnt the review phase scale. it's a friction point on purpose.

> AGENT

Fair — I was wrong to call that a problem. You're right: the review *is* the friction, and friction is the lesson. Looking at what an agent did before you accept it is the whole point of `explain`, and having that cost scale with how much you delegated is actually good design, not a flaw. Delegate six jobs, you owe six reviews. You should *feel* the oversight. I'll drop the "spot-check + summarize" idea entirely and keep `explain` + ADD TO SHIP as a hard gate on every job.

And L3 stays frozen: the five curated systems keep their checkpoints and cards exactly as they are.

So here's the shape with your constraints locked in:

- **The 5 hero systems stay** (story + L3, untouched).
- **More tracks = add generic repair jobs** on top of those five. They get their own simpler flavor cards to read, so the review friction applies to them too. L3 never sees them.
- **Fewer cars than tracks** → you're forced to reuse. A car seals its job, drives back to the bay on its own, and you re-dispatch it to the next open track. Cars become the scarce resource you're juggling.

That gives a real loop: dispatch a wave → rescue the bogged ones with nitro → review + accept each finish (the friction) → cars come home → re-send them → repeat until every job's done → final `dispatch`. Much busier, genuinely multitasking.

One thing to decide on reuse: a car can drive home and get re-sent **the moment it seals**, *before* you've reviewed that job — so a review backlog can pile up while you keep dispatching. That backlog under the clock is where the multitasking pressure really comes from. Good with that?

Open calls before I build:

1. **Counts.** Your 12/6 is a lot of reading. I'd start at **8 tracks (5 hero + 3 generic) / 4 cars** and we tune up. Want to start smaller or go straight for 12/6?
2. **Camera** (still unanswered, and it gates whether 8-12 tracks are even *visible*): light pan/zoom mission-control eye, yes or no? With this many tracks I don't think a single fixed angle can show them readably — but I'll also make hazards volumetric and beams taller either way so you can actually see them.

> DEVELOPER

so are you okay with entire checkpoint explain still being required

> AGENT

Yeah, I'm genuinely fine with it being required. Required is correct here, and for a reason I should've led with instead of arguing scale: if `explain` is optional, players skip it, and the one thing this level exists to teach is gone. "You delegated, so you owe a look before you accept" only lands if the look is mandatory. So keep the hard gate.

My actual worry was never "required is bad" — it was "required times twelve turns reading into stamping." Those are different things. The first two or three reviews, you read because the card is fresh and you're curious what the agent improvised. By the ninth identical-feeling job you're mashing Enter, Y, Enter, Y without your eyes on the text. The gate is still there, but the *comprehension* it's supposed to force has quietly left. That's the failure mode I want to avoid — not the requirement, the dilution.

And there's a constraint that makes this sharper: you've ruled out judging the work (no reject mechanic, no botched-agent puzzles). So reading is never decision-relevant — you always accept. That's totally fine at a handful of jobs, where you read because it's interesting. But it means the only thing keeping reviews from going on autopilot is **how many there are** and **how worth-reading each card is.** The requirement can't carry that on its own.

So: keep `explain` required, yes. Just treat the job count as the real dial. I think the friction stays *live* at around 5-6 reviews and starts going *ceremonial* north of that. You can still get the reuse/throughput juggle from cars being scarcer than jobs without going all the way to 12 — e.g. 4 cars over 6-7 jobs already forces constant re-dispatching.

Does that distinction land for you — required, but dosage matters — or do you actually want all twelve required and reading-fatigue be damned?

> DEVELOPER

all twelve

> AGENT

Done — twelve tracks, every one requires `explain` + ADD TO SHIP. Your call, I'll build it that way and we'll see how the fatigue actually feels in play rather than in theory.

Here's what's locked:

- **12 tracks**: the 5 hero systems (frozen, L3 untouched) + 7 generic repair jobs, each with its own card to read. All 12 gated by `explain` + ADD TO SHIP.
- **6 cars**, reused. A car seals its job, drives itself back to the bay, and you re-dispatch it. Review backlog allowed — sealed jobs wait for you while cars keep rolling.
- Hazards + nitro carry over.

Pull back for the whole board, lean in to watch a car fight a hazard or to read a beam. Plus volumetric hazards and taller beams so they actually show.

anchor.add(beam);\n\n    const ice = makeIceBlock();\n    ice.scale.setScalar(1.25);\n    ice.position.y = 2.6;\n    ice.visible = false;\n    anchor.add(ice);\n    const sprite = if (allAdded()) {\n      termMode = \"report\";\n      reviewSite = null;\n      explained = false;\n      buffer = \"\";\n      termList?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n      flashTerminal(\"all twelve flashTerminal(\"dispatch sent — look how much got done without you\", true);\n    renderTerminal();\n    if (winSub) winSub.textContent = \"twelve jobs, six cars — the report wrote itself\";\n    winTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n      closeTerminal(false);\n      tutorialEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n      winEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    }, 4000);\n    onComplete?.();\n  }\n  function submitCommand() {\n    const n = normalizeCmd(buffer);\n    buffer = \"\";\n    if (!n) { renderTerminal(); return; }\n    if (/^entire dispatch$/.test(n)) { sendDispatch(); return; }\n    if (/^entire (checkpoint|cp) list$/.test(n)) {\n      if (termList) {\n        termList.innerHTML = lanes.map((l) =>\n          `<div class=\"term-list-row\"><span class=\"tl-id tl-id-short\">${l.data.ckpt}</span>` +\n          `<span class=\"tl-title\">subagent fix: ${l.data.name.toLowerCase()} → ${l.data.became}</span></div>`\n        ).join(\"\");\n        termList.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n      }\n      flashTerminal(\"the raw log — now turn it into the day's report:  entire dispatch\", true);\n      renderTerminal();\n      return;\n    }\n    flashTerminal(\"command not recognized — try:  entire dispatch\", false);\n    renderTerminal();\n  }\n\n  // ---------- input ----------\n  function onPointerDown(e) {\n    if (!active || !started || failed || reportSent || terminalOpen) return;\n    pointerDown = true;\n    dragging = false;\n    dropTarget = null;\n    downX = e.clientX; downY = e.clientY; lastX = e.clientX; lastY = e.clientY;\n    const c = carAtPointer(e);\n    if (c) { picked = c; pickedKind = \"car\"; pickedDraggable = (c.state === \"bay\" || c.state === \"loaded\"); return; }\n    const j = jobAtPointer(e);\n    if (j) { pickedJob = j; pickedKind = \"job\"; return; }\n    pickedKind = \"camera\";\n  }\n  function onPointerMove(e) {\n    if (!active || !started || failed || terminalOpen) { canvas.style.cursor = canvas.style.cursor = \"grabbing\";\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // plain hover\n    const c = carAtPointer(e);\n    if (c) {\n      hoverCar = c;\n      canvas.style.cursor = (c.state === \"bay\" || \"car\" && picked) {\n      if (dragging) {\n        const p = groundAt(e);\n        const lane = nearestEmptyLane(p);\n        if (lane) assignCar(picked, lane);\n        else returnHome(picked);\n      } else if (picked.state === \"running\") {\n        boost(picked.lane);\n      }\n    } else if (pickedKind === \"job\" && pickedJob && moved < 6) {\n      if (pickedJob.state === \"sealed\") \"default\";\n  }\n  function onWheel(e) {\n    if (!active || !started || terminalOpen) return;\n    e.preventDefault();\n    camRadius = clamp(camRadius * (1 + e.deltaY * 0.001), 56, 210);\n  }\n  function onKeyDown(e) {\n    if (!active) return;\n\n    if (!started) {\n      if (e.code === \"Enter\" || e.code === \"Space\") { advanceBriefing(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      return;\n    }\n    if (failed) {\n      if (e.code === \"KeyR\") { resetLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      if (e.code === \"KeyN\") { onNewGame?.(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      return;\n    }\n    if (terminalOpen) if (reportSent) { e.preventDefault(); return; }\n      if (e.code === \"Enter\") { submitCommand(); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n      if (e.code === \"Backspace\") { buffer = buffer.slice(0, -1); (e.code === \"Enter\" && allAdded() && !reportSent) {\n      reportDismissed = false; openTerminal(\"report\", null); e.preventDefault(); return;\n    }\n    if (e.code === \"KeyB\") { onExit?.(); e.preventDefault(); }\n  }\n\n  // ---------- HUD per-frame ----------\n  function refreshHud() {\n    if (!started || terminalOpen) { setPrompt(null); return; }\n    if (reportSent) { setPrompt(\"The day is dispatched — press Enter to board the ship\"); return; }\n    if (allAdded()) { setPrompt(\"All systems online — press Enter to send the day's report: `entire dispatch`\"); return; }\n    if (anyRunning()) {\n      setPrompt(`Cars en route — click a bogged one to nitro it (${nitro} left)`);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (anySealed()) {\n      setPrompt(\"Finished jobs are stacking up — return;\n    }\n    if (openTracks() && carsFree()) {\n      setPrompt(\"Drag a subagent car onto an open track\");\n      return;\n    }\n    setPrompt(\"Drag a car onto a track\");\n  }\n\n  // ---------- per-frame ----------\n  function update(dt, t) {\n    if (active && timerRunning && !failed && !reportSent) {\n      timeLeft = Math.max(0, timeLeft - dt);\n      updateClock();\n      if (timeLeft <= 0) failLevel();\n    }\n    applyPanicSky();\n    updateCamera();\n\n    // Cars: bay idle, returning drive home.\n    for (const c of cars) {\n      if (c === picked && dragging) continue;\n      if (c.state === \"bay\") {\n        c.mesh.position.y = c.home.y + Math.sin((t + c.idx) * 2) * 0.05;\n        c.mesh.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = 0.4;\n        c.mesh.userData.cabin.material.emissiveIntensity = 0.45;\n      } else if (c.state === \"returning\") {\n        c.retProg = Math.min(1, c.retProg + BASE_SPEED * RETURN_MULT * dt);\n        const k = c.retProg;\n        const x = c.retFrom.x + (c.retTo.x - c.retFrom.x) * k;\n        const z = c.retFrom.z + (c.retTo.z - c.retFrom.z) * k;\n        c.mesh.position.set(x, terrain.heightAt(x, z) + 0.6, z);\n        c.mesh.rotation.y = Math.atan2(-(c.retTo.x - c.retFrom.x), -(c.retTo.z - c.retFrom.z));\n        for (const w of c.mesh.userData.wheels) w.rotation.x += 10 * dt;\n        c.mesh.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = 0.5;\n        if (c.retProg >= 1) {\n          c.state = \"bay\";\n          c.mesh.position.copy(c.home);\n          c.mesh.rotation.y = 0;\n          updateSystems();\n          teachOnce(\"reuse\", \"That car's free again — drag it onto another open track.\", 6000);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Lanes: loaded idle at the line; running drive their track.\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      if (l.state === \"loaded\" && l.carObj) {\n        const car = l.carObj.mesh;\n        car.position.y = l.start.y + Math.sin((t + l.idx) * 2) * 0.05;\n        const pulse = 0.55 + 0.45 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 6));\n        car.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = pulse;\n        car.userData.cabin.material.emissiveIntensity = pulse;\n      } else if (l.state === \"running\" && l.carObj) {\n        const car = l.carObj.mesh;\n        if (l.boostT > 0) l.boostT = Math.max(0, l.boostT - dt);\n        const hz = l.hazard;\n        const inHaz = !l.hazardCleared && l.prog >= hz.at && l.prog <= hz.at + hz.len;\n\n        if (inHaz && hz.severe && l.boostT <= 0) {\n          l.corrupt = Math.min(1, l.corrupt + dt / HAZARD_FILL);\n nitro and break it free.\", 7000);\n          }\n        }\n\n        let mult = 1;\n        if (l.boostT > 0) mult = BOOST_MULT;\n        else if (inHaz) mult = l.stalled ? STALL_CRAWL : HAZARD_SLOW;\n\n        l.prog = Math.min(1, l.prog + BASE_SPEED * mult * dt);\n        const k = l.prog;\n        const x = l.start.x + (l.finish.x - l.start.x) * k;\n        const z = l.start.z + (l.finish.z - l.start.z) * k;\n        car.position.set(x, terrain.heightAt(x, z) + 0.6, z);\n\n        const boosting = l.boostT > 0;\n        const spin = (boosting ? 18 : mult < 0.5 ? 2 : 7) * dt;\n        for (const w of car.userData.wheels) w.rotation.x += spin;\n        car.userData.glow.material.opacity = boosting ? 0.55 + 0.25 * Math.sin(t * 30) : 0;\n        car.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = boosting ? 1.4 : l.stalled ? 0.3 : 0.7;\n        if (boosting && Math.random() < dt * 12) {\n          spawnSpark(car.position.clone().setY(car.position.y + l.hzMesh, ud = hm.userData;\n      if (!hm.visible) continue;\n      if (l.state === \"added\" || l.hazardCleared) {\n        ud.shardMat.emissiveIntensity = Math.max(0, ud.shardMat.emissiveIntensity - dt * 1.6);\n        ud.domeMat.opacity = Math.max(0, ud.domeMat.opacity - dt * 0.4);\n        ud.ringMat.opacity = Math.max(0.05, ud.ringMat.opacity - dt * 0.6);\n        ud.shardMat.color.lerp(new ? 1.2 : 0.3);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Drop-target highlight.\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      l.railMat.color.setHex(l === dropTarget ? 0x6fe3ff : 0x35506a);\n      l.railMat.opacity = l === dropTarget ? 0.95 : 0.6;\n    }\n\n    // Status beams + warnings + seals + live upgrades.\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      if (l.state === \"empty\" || l.state === \"loaded\") {\n        l.beam.material.color.setHex(l.state === \"loaded\" ? 0x6fe3ff : 0xff8a5c);\n        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.28 + Math.sin(t * 1.6 + l.idx) * 0.08;\n        l.beam.visible = true;\n        const warn = l.brokenModel.userData.warn;\n        if (warn) warn.material.emissiveIntensity = Math.sin(t * 5 + l.idx) > 0 ? 1.8 : 0.2;\n      } else if (l.state === \"running\") {\n        l.beam.material.color.setHex(l.stalled ? 0xff5a3c : 0x6fe3ff);\n        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.42 + Math.sin(t * 6) * 0.18;\n        l.beam.visible = true;\n        const warn = l.brokenModel.userData.warn;\n        if (warn) warn.material.emissiveIntensity = Math.sin(t * 5 + l.idx) > 0 ? 1.8 : 0.2;\n      } else if (l.state === \"sealed\") {\n        l.beam.material.color.setHex(0x8fe3ff);\n        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.46;\n        l.beam.visible = true;\n      } else {\n        l.beam.visible = false;\n        if (l.meltT > 0) {\n          l.meltT = Math.max(0, l.meltT - dt);\n          l.ice.material.opacity = 0.62 * (l.meltT / MELT_DUR);\n          if (l.meltT <= 0) l.ice.visible = false;\n        }\n        const anim = l.upgradeModel.userData.anim;\n        if (anim?.type === \"ring\") {\n          anim.ring.rotation.y += dt * 0.8;\n          anim.ring.position.y = 3.4 + Math.sin(t * 1.6) * 0.2;\n        } else if (anim?.type === \"dome\") {\n          anim.field.rotation.y += dt * 0.25;\n          anim.orbit.rotation.z += dt * 0.6;\n        } else if (anim?.type === \"skid\") {\n          anim.pad.position.y = 1.6 + Math.sin(t * 1.8) * 0.25;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Spark bursts.\n    for (let i = sparks.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n      const s = sparks[i];\n      s.life += dt;\n      const pos = s.geo.attributes.position.array;\n      for (let j = 0; j < s.vel.length; j++) {\n        s.vel[j].y -= 14 * dt;\n        pos[j * 3] += s.vel[j].x * dt;\n        pos[j * 3 + 1] += s.vel[j].y * dt;\n        pos[j * 3 + 2] += s.vel[j].z * dt;\n      }\n      s.geo.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;\n      s.mat.opacity = Math.max(0, 1 - s.life / s.ttl);\n      if (s.life >= s.ttl) {\n        scene.remove(s.pts);\n        s.geo.dispose(); s.mat.dispose();\n        sparks.splice(i, 1);\n      }\n    }\n\n    consoleGlow.intensity = allAdded() && !reportSent ? 8 + Math.sin(t * 4) * 3 : 5;\n\n    if (active && started && !failed) {\n      if (!terminalOpen && allAdded() && !reportSent && !reportDismissed) {\n        openTerminal(\"report\", null);\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (active) refreshHud();\n  }\n\n  // ---------- lifecycle ----------\n  function enter() {\n    active = true;\n    camTarget.set(0, 0, 14);\n    camRadius = 124; camTheta = 0; camPhi = 1.2;\n    updateCamera();\n    canvas.addEventListener(\"mousedown\", onPointerDown);\n    canvas.addEventListener(\"mousemove\", onPointerMove);\n    canvas.addEventListener(\"wheel\", onWheel, { passive: false });\n    window.addEventListener(\"mouseup\", onPointerUp);\n    window.addEventListener(\"keydown\", onKeyDown);\n    dbHud?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    fpShared?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    crosshair?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    setControls();\n    updateSystems();\n    if (!started) {\n      showBriefing();\n    } else if (reportSent) {\n      winEl?.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    } else if (failed) {\n      resetLevel();\n    } else {\n      timerRunning = clockStarted && !allHome();\n      showTutorial(\"The cars kept rolling while you were in orbit.\", 5000);\n    }\n    updateClock();\n    applyPanicSky();\n  }\n  function exit() {\n    active = false;\n    timerRunning = false;\n    closeTerminal(false);\n hideControls();\n    tutorialEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    termEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    failEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    winEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    briefingEl?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    dbHud?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    fpShared?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n    crosshair?.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n  }\n  function resize() {\n    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;\n    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n  }\n\n  return {\n    scene,\n    get camera() { return camera; },\n    update, enter, exit, resize,\n  };\n}\n"

  "content": "import * as THREE from \"three\";\nimport { createTerrain } from \"./terrain.js\";\nimport { makeBeamTexture, makeIceBlock, makeIdSprite } from \"./memoryProps.js\";\n\n// LEVEL 2 (\"The Drone Bay\") — a dispatch yard at scale.\n//\n// Twelve ship systems are dark out across the island, each at the end of its\n// own track. You have only SIX subagent cars. You don't run — you DISPATCH,\n// and because there are more jobs than cars, you reuse them:\n//\n//   drag a car onto a track     → it loads at the starting line\n//   press SPACE                  → the green flag launches every loaded car\n//   a track has a corruption patch → cars bog down in it and lose time\n//   click a bogged car           → spend a nitro charge, it punches through\n//   a car reaches its system      → its work seals (ice), the car drives home\n//   re-dispatch the freed car      → onto the next open track\n//   click a sealed job            → `entire checkpoint explain <id>` pre-filled\n//   read what it did              → ADD TO SHIP (Y) → system online\n//   all twelve online             → type `entire dispatch` — the day writes itself\n//\n// The clock is the rescue run: it starts on the green flag and stops the moment\n// every job is sealed. Reviewing the work afterward is calm, untimed — but with\n// twelve jobs, the review queue is real work. That friction is the lesson:\n// you delegated it, so you owe every one of them a look before you accept it.\n//\n// The camera is a free mission-control eye: drag the background to orbit, scroll\n// to zoom. Pull back for the whole yard, lean in to watch a car fight a patch.\n\nconst TOTAL_TIME = 80;       // seconds for the rescue run (tunable)\nconst BASE_SPEED = 1 / 12;   // lane progress per second (clean lane ≈ 12s)\nconst RETURN_MULT = 2.4;     // freed cars hustle back to the bay\nconst BOOST_MULT = 2.6;      // nitro speed multiplier\nconst BOOST_DUR  = 2.2;      // seconds a nitro surge lasts\nconst NITRO_MAX  = 6;        // charges per run (tunable)\nconst HAZARD_SLOW = 0.30;    // speed multiplier inside a corruption patch\nconst HAZARD_FILL = 3.6;     // seconds in a severe patch before the car stalls\nconst STALL_CRAWL = 0.06;    // speed of a stalled car (basically stuck — boost it)\nconst LOW_TIME = 22;         // clock turns urgent under this\nconst CRIT_TIME = 10;        // clock goes CRITICAL under this\nconst PANIC_TIME = 28;       // the SKY starts shifting toward panic-red\nconst MELT_DUR = 1.2;        // seconds the ice takes to melt on ADD TO SHIP\n\nconst N_CARS = 6;            // fewer cars than jobs — the reuse pressure\nconst START_Z = 64;\nconst FINISH_Z = -34;\nconst BAY_Z = START_Z + 18;\nconst LANE = (i, n) => ({ startX: (i - (n - 1) / 2) * 9, finishX: (i - (n - 1) / 2) * 14 });\nconst baySlotX = (i, n) => (i - (n - 1) / 2) * 8;\nconst clamp = (v, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v));\n\n// Onboarding — short story beats, advanced with Space (Level 1's style).\nconst BRIEFING_BEATS = [\n  \"These are your subagents. Hand them work and it gets done — faster when they all run at once.\",\n  \"Drag one onto a track to send it off. Send as many as you like, then press Space.\",\n  \"You won't watch them work. To see what a subagent did, run:  entire checkpoint explain\",\n];\n\n// Sky panic palette — same dread as Level 1's clock.\nconst SKY_CALM  = new THREE.Color(0x2a2350);\nconst SKY_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x6e0f16);\nconst FOG_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x4a0a0e);\nconst DOME_CALM = new THREE.Color(0x3a3168);\nconst DOME_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0x7a141c);\nconst SUN_CALM  = new THREE.Color(0xfff1dc);\nconst SUN_PANIC = new THREE.Color(0xff5a3c);\n\n// The five HERO systems — rich cards, fixed ids. Exported and FROZEN: Level 3\n// quizzes the player on this exact record (and uses sys.pos on its own island).\n// Level 2 lays out its own grid and never touches sys.pos.\nexport const SYSTEMS = [\n  {\n    ckpt: \"a1c9e4f72b05\", name: \"IGNITION COILS\",\n    pos: { x: -34, z: -28 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenCoils\", upgrade: \"buildPlasmaRing\",\n    became: \"Plasma Ring\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-1\"],\n      [\"system\", \"ignition coils\"],\n      [\"did\", \"coils beyond saving — rebuilt as a plasma ring from salvaged hull plate\"],\n      [\"session\", \"2 attempts · parts scavenged: hull plate ×3\"],\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    ckpt: \"3d7b0f9c61ae\", name: \"NAV CORE\",\n    pos: { x: 40, z: -18 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenNav\", upgrade: \"buildStarDome\",\n    became: \"Star Dome\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-2\"],\n      [\"system\", \"nav core\"],\n      [\"did\", \"core unrecoverable — remapped from scratch; starfield calibrated to the lavender belt\"],\n      [\"session\", \"1 attempt · 412 stars plotted\"],\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    ckpt: \"f25c8b30d971\", name: \"LONG-RANGE ANTENNA\",\n    pos: { x: 28, z: 38 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenDish\", upgrade: \"buildSignalSpire\",\n    became: \"Signal Spire\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-3\"],\n      [\"system\", \"long-range antenna\"],\n      [\"did\", \"dish unsalvageable — respun the mast into a signal spire; relay handshake at full strength\"],\n      [\"session\", \"3 attempts · the first two fell over\"],\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    ckpt: \"62e0a9d4c8f3\", name: \"LIFE SUPPORT\",\n    pos: { x: -30, z: 36 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenVent\", upgrade: \"buildGardenPod\",\n    became: \"Garden Pod\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-4\"],\n      [\"system\", \"life support scrubbers\"],\n      [\"did\", \"filters dead — replaced with a living filter; two vines scavenged from the crash\"],\n      [\"session\", \"1 attempt · the vines approved\"],\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    ckpt: \"8b47f1e62da0\", name: \"LANDING STRUTS\",\n    pos: { x: 8, z: -44 },\n    broken: \"buildBrokenStrut\", upgrade: \"buildGravSkid\",\n    became: \"Grav Skid\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-5\"],\n      [\"system\", \"landing struts\"],\n      [\"did\", \"strut seized solid — swapped for grav skids; Level 3 never sees them. wider field of view\"],\n      [\"session\", \"2 attempts · recalibrated twice\"],\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    ckpt: \"e4717c5a0d63\", name: \"WATER RECLAIM\",\n    broken: \"buildBrokenNav\", upgrade: \"buildStarDome\", became: \"Dew Still\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-10\"],\n      [\"system\", \"water reclaim\"],\n      [\"did\", \"reclaimer fouled — rebuilt as a dew still pulling from the fog\"],\n      [\"session\", \"1 attempt · the first cup was awful\"],\n    ],\n  },\n  {\n    ckpt: \"9d28b6f1e470\", name: \"DOCKING RING\",\n    broken: \"buildBrokenStrut\", upgrade: \"buildGravSkid\", became: \"Halo Collar\",\n    card: [\n      [\"subagent\", \"subagent-11\"],\n      [\"system\", \"docking ring\"],\n      [\"did\", \"ring warped — reformed into a halo collar; Every third lane is severe (must be boosted);\n// the rest just slow you. Staggered along the track so they don't all hit at once.\nconst HAZARDS = JOBS.map((_, i) => {\n  const severe = i % 3 === 0;\n  return { at: 0.30 + (i % 4) * 0.06, len: severe ? 0.18 : 0.10, severe };\n});\n\nfunction normalizeCmd(s) {\n  return s.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\\s+/g, \" \");\n}\n\n// ---------- prop builders: the broken systems ----------\nconst CHARRED = () => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x2a2e38, roughness: 0.9, metalness: 0.3 });\nconst SCORCH = () => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x3a3f4a, roughness: 0.85, metalness: 0.25 });\n\nfunction withWarnLight(g) {\n  const bulb = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.22, 10, 10),\n    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x401512, emissive: 0xff3b2e, emissiveIntensity: 1.6 })\n  );\n  bulb.position.y = g.userData.warnY ?? 3.2;\n  g.add(bulb);\n  g.userData.warn = bulb;\n  return g;\n}\nfunction buildBrokenCoils() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n    const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.1 - i * 0.18, 1.2 - i * 0.18, 1.0, 10), i ? CHARRED() : SCORCH());\n    c.position.y = 0.5 + i * 1.05;\n    c.rotation.z = (i % 2 ? -1 : 1) * 0.12 * (i + 1);\n    g.add(c);\n  }\n  g.userData.warnY = 3.6;\n  return const core = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1.5, 0), CHARRED());\n  core.position.y = 2.6;\n  core.rotation.set(0.4, 0.2, 0.5);\n  g.add(ped, core);\n  g.userData.warnY = 4.4;\n  return withWarnLight(g);\n}\nfunction buildBrokenDish() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const mast = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.18, 0.24, 3.2, 8), SCORCH());\n  mast.position.y = 1.4;\n  mast.rotation.z = 0.5;\n  const dish = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.9, 1.5, 0.4, 12), CHARRED());\n  dish.position.set(1.6, 0.5, 0);\n  dish.rotation.z = 1.2;\n  g.add(mast, dish);\n  g.userData.warnY = 3.0;\n  return withWarnLight(g);\n}\nfunction buildBrokenVent() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const box = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.6, 2.0, 2.0), SCORCH());\n  box.position.y = 1.0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n    const slat = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.2, 0.16, 0.1), CHARRED());\n    slat.position.set(0, 0.6 + i * 0.5, 1.06);\n    slat.rotation.x = 0.5;\n    g.add(slat);\n  }\n  g.add(box);\n  g.userData.warnY = 2.8;\n  return withWarnLight(g);\n}\nfunction buildBrokenStrut() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const leg = new CHARRED());\n  foot.position.set(1.6, 0.25, 0);\n  g.add(leg, foot);\n  g.userData.warnY = 3.4;\n  return withWarnLight(g);\n}\n\n// ---------- prop builders: what the cars improvise them into ----------\nfunction buildPlasmaRing() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const base = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.2, 1.5, 0.7, 10), SCORCH());\n  base.position.y = 0.35;\n  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.8, 0.3, 12, 36),\n    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x6b4a16, emissive: 0xffb86b, emissiveIntensity: 1.4, roughness: 0.4 })\n  );\n  ring.position.y = 3.4;\n  g.add(base, ring);\n  g.userData.anim = { type: \"ring\", ring };\n  return g;\n}\nfunction buildStarDome() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const ped = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.9, 1.2, 1.2, 8), SCORCH());\n  ped.position.y = 0.6;\n  const stars = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n  const pts = new Float32Array(120 * 3);\n  for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {\n    const r = 1.2 + Math.random() * 1.4;\n    const th = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n    const ph = Math.random() * Math.PI * 0.5;\n    pts[i * 3] = Math.cos(th) * Math.sin(ph) * r;\n    pts[i * 3 + 1] = 3.2 + Math.cos(ph) * r * 0.9;\n    pts[i * 3 + 2] = Math.sin(th) * Math.sin(ph) * r;\n  }\n  stars.setAttribute(\"position\", new THREE.BufferAttribute(pts, 3));\n  const field = = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.TorusGeometry(2.0, 0.05, 8, 40),\n    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.7 })\n  );\n  orbit.position.y = 3.6;\n  orbit.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2.4;\n  g.add(ped, field, orbit);\n  g.userData.anim = { type: \"dome\", field, orbit };\n  return g;\n}\nfunction buildSignalSpire(beamTex) {\n  const g = new beam.visible = false;\n  g.add(spire, beam);\n  g.userData.anim = { type: \"spire\", beam };\n  return g;\n}\nfunction buildGardenPod() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const tray = new 0.9 + (i % 3) * 0.35, 6),\n      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1d4a2c, emissive: 0x35d97a, emissiveIntensity: 0.7, roughness: 0.7 })\n    );\n    const a = (i / 5) * Math.PI * 2;\n    plant.position.set(Math.cos(a) * 1.0, 1.0, Math.sin(a) * 1.0);\n    g.add(plant);\n  }\n  const domeGlass = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.SphereGeometry(2.1, 18, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / domeGlass.position.y = 0.6;\n  g.add(tray, domeGlass);\n  g.userData.anim = { type: \"pod\" };\n  return g;\n}\nfunction buildGravSkid() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const pad = new );\n  pad.position.y = 1.6;\n  const glow = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.5, 0.12, 8, 32),\n    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x6fe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.65 })\n its own island.\nexport const UPGRADE_BUILDERS = {\n  buildPlasmaRing, buildStarDome, buildSignalSpire, buildGardenPod, buildGravSkid,\n};\n\n// ---------- the little worker car ----------\nfunction buildCar() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const bodyMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n    color: 0x2c3140, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.35, emissive: 0x2a8aa6, emissiveIntensity: 0.55,\n  });\n  const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.1, 0.7, 3.4), bodyMat);\n  body.position.y = 1.0;\n  const nose = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.6, 0.5, 0.9), bodyMat);\n  nose.position.set(0, 0.95, -2.0);\n  const cabin = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.4, 0.7, 1.5),\n    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x0c2030, emissive: 0x6fe3ff, glow.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;\n  glow.position.y = 0.12;\n  g.add(body, nose, cabin, glow);\n  g.userData = { bodyMat, cabin, glow, wheels };\n  return g;\n}\n\n// ---------- a volumetric corruption patch (reads at a low camera angle) ----------\nfunction buildHazard(severe) {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const r = severe ? 3.4 : 2.2;\n  const shardMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n    color: 0x3a0a0a, emissive: 0xff3320, emissiveIntensity: 1.4, roughness: 0.5, metalness: 0.2,\n  });\n  const n = severe ? 7 : 5;\n  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n    const h = (severe ? 2.6 : 1.6) * (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.7);\n    const shard = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.5 + Math.random() * 0.4, h, 5), shardMat);\n    const a = (i / n) * Math.PI * 2 + Math.random() * 0.4;\n    const rad = Math.random() * r * 0.7;\n    shard.position.set(Math.cos(a) * rad, h * 0.5, Math.sin(a) * rad);\n    shard.rotation.set((Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5, Math.random() * Math.PI, ring.position.y = 0.15;\n  g.add(ring);\n  g.userData = { shardMat, domeMat, ringMat, severe, r };\n  return g;\n}\n\n// The ship console behind the bay (decoration / where the report fires).\nfunction makeConsole() {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  const pedestal = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.6, 4.6, 1.6),\n    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1c2230, g;\n}\n\nexport function createDroneBayView(renderer, { onExit, onComplete, onNext, onNewGame } = {}) {\n  const canvas = renderer.domElement;\n\n  // ---------- scene & sky ----------\n  const scene = new THREE.Scene();\n  scene.background = SKY_CALM.clone();\n  scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(SKY_CALM.clone(), 140, 420);\n\n  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(\n    60, window.innerWidth / camTheta = 0, camPhi = 1.2;\n  function updateCamera() {\n    const sinPhi = Math.sin(camPhi);\n    camera.position.set(\n      camTarget.x + camRadius * sinPhi * Math.sin(camTheta),\n      camTarget.y + camRadius * Math.cos(camPhi),\n      camTarget.z + camRadius * sinPhi * Math.cos(camTheta)\n    );\n    camera.lookAt(camTarget);\n  }\n  updateCamera();\n\n  // ---------- lighting ----------\n  scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xcdbcff, 0x3a2f5e, 0.7));\n  const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(SUN_CALM, 1.5);\n  sun.position.set(60, 90, 40);\n  scene.add(sun);\n  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x6a5a92, 0.3));\n\n  const dome = new THREE.Mesh(\n    new THREE.SphereGeometry(900, 32, 16),\n    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: DOME_CALM.clone(), side: THREE.BackSide, fog: false })\n  );\n  scene.add(dome);\n\n  // ---------- terrain (the same island) ----------\n  const terrain = createTerrain({ size: 200, segments: 220, maxHeight: 26, seed: 1337 });\n  scene.add(terrain.mesh, terrain.water);\n\n  const beamTex = makeBeamTexture();\n\n  const BUILD = {\n    buildBrokenCoils, buildBrokenNav, buildBrokenDish, buildBrokenVent, buildBrokenStrut,\n const p = new THREE.Vector3();\n    return raycaster.ray.intersectPlane(groundPlane, p) ? p : null;\n  }\n  function nearestEmptyLane(p) {\n    if (!p) return null;\n    let best = null, bd = 1e9;\n termList.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n      }\n      flashTerminal(\"the raw log — now turn it into the day's report:  entire dispatch\", true);\n      renderTerminal();\n      return;\n    }\n    flashTerminal(\"command not recognized — try:  entire dispatch\", false);\n    renderTerminal();\n  }\n\n  // ---------- input ----------\n  function onPointerDown(e) {\n    if (!active || !started || failed || reportSent || terminalOpen) return;\n    pointerDown = true;\n    dragging = false;\n    dropTarget = null;\n    downX = e.clientX; downY = e.clientY; lastX = e.clientX; lastY = e.clientY;\n    const c = carAtPointer(e);\n    if (c) { picked = c; pickedKind = \"car\"; pickedDraggable = (c.state === \"bay\" || c.state === \"loaded\"); return; }\n    const j = jobAtPointer(e);\n    if (j) { \"default\"; return; }\n\n    if (pointerDown && pickedKind === \"car\" && pickedDraggable) {\n      if (!dragging) {\n        if (Math.hypot(e.clientX - downX, e.clientY - downY) > 5) { dragging = canvas.style.cursor = \"grabbing\";\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // plain hover\n    const c = carAtPointer(e);\n    if (c) {\n      hoverCar = c;\n      canvas.style.cursor = (c.state === \"bay\" || \"car\" && picked) {\n      if (dragging) {\n        const p = groundAt(e);\n        const lane = nearestEmptyLane(p);\n        if (lane) assignCar(picked, lane);\n        else returnHome(picked);\n      } else if (picked.state === \"running\") {\n        boost(picked.lane);\n      }\n    } else if (pickedKind === \"job\" && pickedJob && moved < 6) {\n      if (pickedJob.state === \"sealed\") \"default\";\n  }\n  function onWheel(e) {\n    if (!active || !started || terminalOpen) return;\n    e.preventDefault();\n    camRadius = clamp(camRadius * (1 + e.deltaY * 0.001), 56, 210);\n  }\n  function onKeyDown(e) {\n    if (!active) return;\n\n    if (!started) {\n      if (e.code === \"Enter\" || e.code === \"Space\") { advanceBriefing(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      return;\n    }\n    if (failed) {\n      if (e.code === \"KeyR\") { resetLevel(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      if (e.code === \"KeyN\") { onNewGame?.(); e.preventDefault(); }\n      return;\n    }\n    if (terminalOpen) {\n      if (e.code === \"Escape\") { closeTerminal(true); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n      if (termMode === \"review\") {\n        if (!explained && e.code === \"Enter\") { runExplain(); if (reportSent) { e.preventDefault(); return; }\n      if (e.code === \"Enter\") { submitCommand(); e.preventDefault(); return; }\n      if (e.code === \"Backspace\") { buffer = buffer.slice(0, -1); (e.code === \"Enter\" && allAdded() && !reportSent) {\n      reportDismissed = false; openTerminal(\"report\", null); e.preventDefault(); return;\n    }\n    if (e.code === \"KeyB\") { onExit?.(); e.preventDefault(); }\n  }\n\n  // ---------- HUD per-frame ----------\n  function refreshHud() {\n    if (!started || terminalOpen) { setPrompt(null); return; }\n    if (reportSent) { setPrompt(\"The day is dispatched — press Enter to board the ship\"); return; }\n    if (allAdded()) { setPrompt(\"All systems online — press Enter to send the day's report: `entire dispatch`\"); return; }\n    if (anyRunning()) {\n      setPrompt(`Cars en route — click a bogged one to nitro it (${nitro} left)`);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (anySealed()) {\n      setPrompt(\"Finished jobs are stacking up — return;\n    }\n    if (openTracks() && carsFree()) {\n      setPrompt(\"Drag a subagent car onto an open track\");\n      return;\n    }\n    setPrompt(\"Drag a car onto a track\");\n  }\n\n  // ---------- per-frame ----------\n  function update(dt, t) {\n    if (active && timerRunning && !failed && !reportSent) {\n      timeLeft = Math.max(0, timeLeft - dt);\n      updateClock();\n      if (timeLeft <= 0) failLevel();\n    }\n    applyPanicSky();\n    updateCamera();\n\n    // Cars: bay idle, returning drive home.\n    for (const c of cars) {\n      if (c === picked && dragging) c.mesh.userData.cabin.material.emissiveIntensity = 0.45;\n      } else if (c.state === \"returning\") {\n        c.retProg = Math.min(1, c.retProg + BASE_SPEED * RETURN_MULT * dt);\n        const k = c.retProg;\n        const x = c.retFrom.x + (c.retTo.x - c.retFrom.x) * k;\n        const z = c.retFrom.z + (c.retTo.z - c.retFrom.z) * k;\n        c.mesh.position.set(x, terrain.heightAt(x, z) + 0.6, z);\n        c.mesh.rotation.y = Math.atan2(-(c.retTo.x - c.retFrom.x), -(c.retTo.z - c.retFrom.z));\n        for (const w of c.mesh.userData.wheels) w.rotation.x += 10 * dt;\n        c.mesh.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = 0.5;\n        if (c.retProg >= 1) {\n          c.state = \"bay\";\n          c.mesh.position.copy(c.home);\n          c.mesh.rotation.y = 0;\n          updateSystems();\n          teachOnce(\"reuse\", \"That car's free again — drag it onto another open track.\", 6000);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Lanes: loaded idle at the line; running drive their track.\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      if (l.state === \"loaded\" && l.carObj) {\n        const car = l.carObj.mesh;\n        car.position.y = l.start.y + Math.sin((t + l.idx) * 2) * 0.05;\n        const pulse = 0.55 + 0.45 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 6));\n        car.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = pulse;\n        car.userData.cabin.material.emissiveIntensity = pulse;\n      } else if (l.state === \"running\" && l.carObj) {\n        const car = l.carObj.mesh;\n        if (l.boostT > 0) l.boostT = Math.max(0, l.boostT - dt);\n        const hz = l.hazard;\n        const inHaz = !l.hazardCleared && l.prog >= hz.at && l.prog <= hz.at + hz.len;\n\n        if (inHaz && hz.severe && l.boostT <= 0) {\n          l.corrupt = Math.min(1, l.corrupt + dt / HAZARD_FILL);\n nitro and break it free.\", 7000);\n          }\n        }\n\n        let mult = 1;\n        if (l.boostT > 0) mult = BOOST_MULT;\n        else if (inHaz) mult = l.stalled ? STALL_CRAWL : HAZARD_SLOW;\n\n        l.prog = Math.min(1, l.prog + BASE_SPEED * mult * dt);\n        const k = l.prog;\n        const x = l.start.x + (l.finish.x - l.start.x) * k;\n        const z = l.start.z + (l.finish.z - l.start.z) * k;\n        car.position.set(x, terrain.heightAt(x, z) + 0.6, z);\n\n        const boosting = l.boostT > 0;\n        const spin = (boosting ? 18 : mult < 0.5 ? 2 : 7) * dt;\n        for (const w of car.userData.wheels) w.rotation.x += spin;\n        car.userData.glow.material.opacity = boosting ? 0.55 + 0.25 * Math.sin(t * 30) : 0;\n        car.userData.bodyMat.emissiveIntensity = boosting ? 1.4 : l.stalled ? 0.3 : 0.7;\n        if (boosting && Math.random() < dt * 12) {\n          spawnSpark(car.position.clone().setY(car.position.y + - dt * 1.6);\n        ud.domeMat.opacity = Math.max(0, ud.domeMat.opacity - dt * 0.4);\n        ud.ringMat.opacity = Math.max(0.05, ud.ringMat.opacity - dt * 0.6);\n        ud.shardMat.color.lerp(new THREE.Color(0x123a4a), 0.05);\n        if (ud.shardMat.emissiveIntensity < 0.05) hm.visible = false;\n      } else {\n        const hot = l.state === \"running\" && (l.stalled || l.corrupt > 0);\n ? 1.2 : 0.3);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Drop-target highlight.\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      l.railMat.color.setHex(l === dropTarget ? 0x6fe3ff : 0x35506a);\n      l.railMat.opacity = l === dropTarget ? 0.95 : 0.6;\n    }\n\n    // Status beams + warnings + seals + live upgrades.\n    for (const l of lanes) {\n      if (l.state === \"empty\" || l.state === \"loaded\") {\n        l.beam.material.color.setHex(l.state === \"loaded\" ? 0x6fe3ff : 0xff8a5c);\n        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.28 + Math.sin(t * 1.6 + l.idx) * 0.08;\n        l.beam.visible = true;\n        const warn = l.brokenModel.userData.warn;\n        if (warn) warn.material.emissiveIntensity = Math.sin(t * 5 + l.idx) > 0 ? 1.8 : 0.2;\n      } else if (l.state === \"running\") {\n        l.beam.material.color.setHex(l.stalled ? 0xff5a3c : 0x6fe3ff);\n        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.42 + Math.sin(t * 6) * 0.18;\n        l.beam.visible = true;\n        const warn = l.brokenModel.userData.warn;\n        if (warn) warn.material.emissiveIntensity = Math.sin(t * 5 + l.idx) > 0 ? 1.8 : 0.2;\n      } else if (l.state === \"sealed\") {\n        l.beam.material.color.setHex(0x8fe3ff);\n        l.beam.material.opacity = 0.46;\n        l.beam.visible = true;\n      } else {\n        l.beam.visible = false;\n        if (l.meltT > 0) {\n l.upgradeModel.userData.anim;\n        if (anim?.type === \"ring\") {\n          anim.ring.rotation.y += dt * 0.8;\n          anim.ring.position.y = 3.4 + Math.sin(t * 1.6) * 0.2;\n        } else if > AGENT

Built and clean. Level 2 is now a dispatch yard. Reload and have at it.

**What changed:**

- **12 tracks, 6 cars, reuse.** Five hero systems + seven generic repair jobs, all gated by `explain` + ADD TO SHIP (every one of the twelve, as you called it). A car seals its job, drives itself back to the bay, and you re-dispatch it. Cars are the scarce thing you're juggling.
- **Review by clicking the finished job.** Since the car drives off after sealing, you now click the ice-blue *job* at the end of the track to review it. The review queue stacks up while cars keep rolling — that's the multitasking pressure.
- **Free camera.** Drag the background to orbit, scroll to zoom. Pull back for the whole yard, lean in to watch a car fight a patch. (Dragging a *car* still drags the car; dragging empty space moves the camera.)
- **Visibility fixes.** Hazards are now volumetric (glowing shard clusters with a dome, not flat decals) so they read from a low angle, and the beams are taller and brighter.
- **Aggregate status panel.** Twelve named rows wouldn't fit, so the side panel now summarizes: systems online x/12, en route / to review / bogged, and cars free.
- Clock starts on the first green flag, stops once all twelve are sealed. Reviewing stays untimed.

**Untuned, flag what feels off:** `TOTAL_TIME 80`, `NITRO_MAX 6`, 6 cars, ~4 severe patches. With 12 reviews you'll get the first real read on whether the friction holds or turns into stamping — that was the open question, now you'll actually feel it.

Two things still open from before, parked but not forgotten:
1. **Onboarding copy** — you were mid-pick between the 2-beat options (A/B/C). Current build still has the old 3-beat version.
2. The dead `#db-briefing-start` CSS.

Want to lock the onboarding copy next, or play this first?

1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { makeNoise3D, makeFbm } from "./noise.js";
3	
4	// A walkable island: a displaced plane with a radial falloff so the land rises
5	// out of a lavender sea and tapers back into it at the edges. Palette matches
6	// the planet — gold highlands, pale-gold shore, lavender shallows.
7	
8	// Colors as 0..1 rgb triples (vertex colors are linear-ish; kept soft).
9	const C_DEEP = [0.27, 0.21, 0.46];    // lavender lowland near water
10	const C_SHORE = [0.78, 0.66, 0.42];   // pale gold sand
11	const C_GOLD = [0.86, 0.66, 0.27];    // gold midland
12	const C_PEAK = [0.96, 0.86, 0.62];    // bright gold / cream highland
13	
14	function mix(a, b, t) {
15	  return [a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * t, a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t, a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * t];
16	}
17	function ramp(t) {
18	  if (t < 0.18) return mix(C_DEEP, C_SHORE, t / 0.18);
19	  if (t < 0.5) return mix(C_SHORE, C_GOLD, (t - 0.18) / 0.32);
20	  return mix(C_GOLD, C_PEAK, Math.min(1, (t - 0.5) / 0.5));
21	}
22	
23	/**
24	 * Build the island terrain.
25	 *
26	 * @param {object} opts
27	 * @param {number} opts.size     world units across (square)
28	 * @param {number} opts.segments grid resolution
29	 * @param {number} opts.maxHeight peak elevation in world units
30	 * @param {number} opts.seed
31	 * @returns {{ mesh, water, size, maxHeight, heightAt(x,z), radius }}
32	 */
33	export function createTerrain({
34	  size = 200,
35	  segments = 220,
36	  maxHeight = 26,
37	  seed = 1337,
38	} = {}) {
39	  const noise = makeNoise3D(seed);
40	  const fbm = makeFbm(noise, { octaves: 5, lacunarity: 2.1, gain: 0.5 });
41	
42	  const half = size / 2;
43	  const seaLevel = 0; // world-space y of the surrounding water plane
44	
45	  // Raw elevation field in world units for a given world (x,z). Combines fbm
46	  // detail with a smooth radial island mask (1 at centre → 0 past the radius).
47	  const islandRadius = half * 0.86;
48	  function rawHeight(x, z) {
49	    const d = Math.hypot(x, z) / islandRadius;
50	    // Smooth falloff: flat-ish plateau in the middle, sinks below sea at edge.
51	    const mask = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(1 - d * d, 0, 1);
52	    const n = fbm(x * 0.015, 0, z * 0.015) * 0.5 + 0.5; // 0..1, gentle hills
53	    const ridges = fbm(x * 0.05 + 100, 5, z * 0.05 - 100) * 0.5 + 0.5; // finer
54	    const e = (n * 0.7 + ridges * 0.3) * mask;
55	    // Push the shoreline down so the centre clearly stands above the water.
56	    return (e - 0.18) * maxHeight;
57	  }
58	
59	  // Bake the grid once into a Float32 height table for fast bilinear queries.
60	  const n = segments + 1;
61	  const step = size / segments;
62	  const table = new Float32Array(n * n);
63	  for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) {
64	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
65	      const x = -half + i * step;
66	      const z = -half + j * step;
67	      table[j * n + i] = rawHeight(x, z);
68	    }
69	  }
70	
71	  // Bilinear height lookup in world space, clamped to the grid.
72	  function heightAt(x, z) {
73	    const fx = THREE.MathUtils.clamp((x + half) / step, 0, segments);
74	    const fz = THREE.MathUtils.clamp((z + half) / step, 0, segments);
75	    const i0 = Math.floor(fx), j0 = Math.floor(fz);
76	    const i1 = Math.min(i0 + 1, segments), j1 = Math.min(j0 + 1, segments);
77	    const tx = fx - i0, tz = fz - j0;
78	    const h00 = table[j0 * n + i0], h10 = table[j0 * n + i1];
79	    const h01 = table[j1 * n + i0], h11 = table[j1 * n + i1];
80	    const a = h00 + (h10 - h00) * tx;
81	    const b = h01 + (h11 - h01) * tx;
82	    return a + (b - a) * tz;
83	  }
84	
85	  // ---- mesh ----
86	  const geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(size, size, segments, segments);
87	  geo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2); // lay flat on the XZ plane, +Y up
88	  const pos = geo.attributes.position;
89	  const colors = new Float32Array(pos.count * 3);
90	  for (let v = 0; v < pos.count; v++) {
91	    const x = pos.getX(v);
92	    const z = pos.getZ(v);
93	    const y = table[
94	      Math.round((z + half) / step) * n + Math.round((x + half) / step)
95	    ];
96	    pos.setY(v, y);
97	    // Color by height above sea; underwater fringe stays lavender.
98	    const t = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(y / maxHeight + 0.18, 0, 1);
99	    const c = ramp(t);
100	    colors[v * 3] = c[0]; colors[v * 3 + 1] = c[1]; colors[v * 3 + 2] = c[2];
101	  }
102	  geo.setAttribute("color", new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors, 3));
103	  geo.computeVertexNormals();
104	
105	  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(
106	    geo,
107	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
108	      vertexColors: true,
109	      roughness: 0.85,
110	      metalness: 0.15, // a faint sheen so it still feels related to the gold world
111	      flatShading: false,
112	    })
113	  );
114	  mesh.receiveShadow = true;
115	
116	  // ---- surrounding water ----
117	  const water = new THREE.Mesh(
118	    new THREE.PlaneGeometry(size * 4, size * 4),
119	    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
120	      color: 0x4a3a86,        // lavender sea, matching the planet's DEEP tone
121	      transparent: true,
122	      opacity: 0.86,
123	      roughness: 0.25,
124	      metalness: 0.0,
125	    })
126	  );
127	  water.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
128	  water.position.y = seaLevel;
129	
130	  return { mesh, water, size, maxHeight, heightAt, radius: islandRadius, seaLevel };
131	}
132

{
  "command": "cat > /tmp/landcheck.mjs <<'EOF'\nimport { createTerrain } from \"/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/planetfall-seed-signalkit/src/terrain.js\";\nconst t = createTerrain({ size:200, segments:220, maxHeight:26, seed:1337 });\n\nconst N=12, N_CARS=6;\nconst START_Z=30, FINISH_Z=-28, BAY_Z=46;\nconst LANE=(i,n)=>({startX:(i-(n-1)/2)*6, finishX:(i-(n-1)/2)*8});\nconst baySlot=(i,n)=>(i-(n-1)/2)*7;\n\nlet minH=1e9, worst=null;\nfunction chk(x,z,tag){ const h=t.heightAt(x,z); if(h<minH){minH=h;worst=`${tag} (${x.toFixed(0)},${z.toFixed(0)}) h=${h.toFixed(2)}`;} return h; }\n\nfor(let i=0;i<N;i++){\n  const {startX,finishX}=LANE(i,N);\n  chk(startX,START_Z,`start${i}`);\n  chk(finishX,FINISH_Z,`finish${i}`);\n  for(let k=0;k<=6;k++){ const kk=k/6; chk(startX+(finishX-startX)*kk, START_Z+(FINISH_Z-START_Z)*kk, `track${i}`); }\n}\nfor(let i=0;i<N_CARS;i++) chk(baySlot(i,N_CARS),BAY_Z,`bay${i}`);\nchk(0,BAY_Z+8,\"console\");\n\nconsole.log(\"min height across footprint:\", minH.toFixed(2));\nconsole.log(\"lowest point:\", worst);\nconsole.log(minH>1.0 ? \"OK — all on land\" : \"WET — some points at/below water\");\nEOF\nnode /tmp/landcheck.mjs"
}

> AGENT

Done. Reload and look again.

**Fixes:**

- **No more water.** The yard was spread so wide the outer tracks ran off the island into the sea (land only reliably exists within ~radius 60 of center; finishes were at ~84). I compacted the whole thing onto a centered land patch and **verified it against the actual terrain** — sampled `heightAt` across every track, start, finish, and bay slot; the lowest point is 1.54 above the waterline. Nothing drives through water now.
- **Less bird's-eye.** Camera dropped lower and pulled in (height ~21 instead of ~40, closer in), and you can zoom in tighter than before. It reads as standing near the yard, not floating over the island.
- **A real spot for the cars.** Added a glowing depot outline on the ground around the bay so the cars have a marked home that hugs the terrain (no clipping/floating).

The tradeoff: shorter tracks now (the island patch is smaller), so traversal is quicker — that may want a clock retune once you play it.

One thing I want to flag honestly: you opened with "I don't know if I like this." I fixed the concrete complaints (water, camera, the cars' spot), but if the **12-track scale itself** is what's not landing, that's a bigger call than placement — say the word and we can dial it back. Want to play this pass first, or talk scale?

> AGENT

Done. Reload and check the framing.

**What changed:**

- **Beams out of the water.** Systems pulled inland — every beam now sits at min height ~4.2 (solid gold land), confirmed by sampling the real terrain, not guessing. No beam rises from the shallows anymore.
- **A real dock.** The cars now park on a built platform — a deck on stilts with a glowing edge — instead of just sitting on the dirt. They have a literal place.
- **Island fills the view, water gone.** Here's the honest bit: you can't *both* hide all water *and* avoid a bird's-eye angle on a round island — tilt down to hide the sea and it goes top-down; tilt up to feel grounded and the sea comes into frame. So I solved it with **fog** instead of geometry: tightened the haze (80–190) so the distant sea dissolves into the sky color. You get a low, grounded angle AND the water reads as atmosphere, not ocean. I also capped how far you can tilt the camera down when orbiting, so you can't swing it around and find the waterline.
- **Starts on the tracks.** Camera now opens framed on the track corridor — dock and cars in the foreground, tracks running out to the systems.

**Heads up:** the playable patch is smaller now, so tracks are short and cars finish fast. The clock (`TOTAL_TIME 80`) and speeds will probably want a retune once you feel it — tell me if it's too easy/slow and I'll tighten.

Take a look — if the island still doesn't read as "big," the lever is zooming the camera in closer by default (smaller `camRadius`), which I can do in one line.

Done. Reload and look.

**Both changes:**

- **Dock is off to the side.** It's now a pier running down the right (+x) edge of the island, posts and all. The six cars wait in a column on it, facing left toward the track field, and you drag them left onto the track starts. The center of the view is clear for the tracks. (Drop-detection is now distance-based, so dropping a car on a lane works regardless of where the dock sits.)
- **Camera opens on the tracks.** Default view is pulled in closer (radius 88 → 66) and aimed straight down the track corridor at `(0, 1.5, 4)`, theta 0 — so you start looking *along* the twelve tracks toward the systems, with the dock sitting at the right edge of the frame rather than filling the foreground. You can still orbit/zoom from there.

If the dock should be on the **left** instead of the right, that's a one-line flip (`DOCK_X = -46`). And if the camera should sit even tighter on the tracks, I can drop `camRadius` further or nudge the target. Tell me what it looks like.

  "command": "cat > /tmp/landcheck.mjs <<'EOF'\nimport { createTerrain } from \"/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/planetfall-seed-signalkit/src/terrain.js\";\nconst t = createTerrain({ size:200, segments:220, maxHeight:26, seed:1337 });\nconst N=12, N_CARS=6;\nfunction test(label, sx, fx, START_Z, FINISH_Z, DOCK_X, DOCK_Z0, DOCK_DZ){\n  const LANE=(i)=>({startX:(i-(N-1)/2)*sx, finishX:(i-(N-1)/2)*fx});\n  let minFin=1e9,minTrack=1e9,minStart=1e9,minDock=1e9,wT=null;\n  for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const {startX,finishX}=LANE(i);\n    minFin=Math.min(minFin,t.heightAt(finishX,FINISH_Z));\n    minStart=Math.min(minStart,t.heightAt(startX,START_Z));\n    for(let k=0;k<=8;k++){const kk=k/8;const h=t.heightAt(startX+(finishX-startX)*kk,START_Z+(FINISH_Z-START_Z)*kk);if(h<minTrack){minTrack=h;wT=`@${kk.toFixed(2)}`;}}}\n  for(let i=0;i<N_CARS;i++)minDock=Math.min(minDock,t.heightAt(DOCK_X,DOCK_Z0+i*DOCK_DZ));\n  const lo=Math.min(minFin,minTrack,minStart,minDock);\n  console.log(`${lo>2?\"OK \":\"LOW\"} ${label}: beam=${minFin.toFixed(1)} start=${minStart.toFixed(1)} track=${minTrack.toFixed(1)}${wT} dock=${minDock.toFixed(1)} | startW=±${((N-1)/2*sx).toFixed(0)}`);\n}\n// narrower fans, pushed forward\ntest(\"F sx5 fx6 z46..-16 dk44z40\",5,6, 46,-16, 44,40,-6);\ntest(\"G sx4.5 fx6 z48..-14 dk42z42\",4.5,6, 48,-14, 42,42,-6);\ntest(\"H sx4 fx5.5 z50..-12 dk40z44\",4,5.5, 50,-12, 40,44,-6);\ntest(\"I sx5 fx6.5 z44..-20 dk44z38\",5,6.5, 44,-20, 44,38,-6);\ntest(\"J sx4.5 fx6 z46..-18 dk42z40\",4.5,6, 46,-18, 42,40,-6);\nEOF\nnode /tmp/landcheck.mjs"
}

> AGENT

Done. Reload and see.

**What changed:**

- **Tracks now start at the front of the island.** The whole field is shifted forward toward the near shore (start line moved from z=34 to z=50) and the fan is narrower so the outer lanes don't run off into the water. Tracks begin up front and run inward to the systems on the high central ground. Verified on the real terrain — lowest point anywhere is 2.2, beams at 3.3+ (solid land).
- **Zoomed in.** Camera pulled from radius 66 to 52 and aimed at the start of the tracks (z=24), so you open looking down the track corridor from its beginning, dock at the right.

**The honest constraint I keep hitting, so you know the lever:** the **12-lane width** is what fights both of these. The island's good land is a central dome; the front edge is low shore. A wide fan pushed forward hits water, and a wide fan zoomed in pushes the outer lanes off-screen. I narrowed the fan to buy room, but it's now fairly tight (lanes 4 units apart at the start). If you want it pushed even further forward or zoomed even tighter, the clean way is **fewer lanes** — that's the trade. You locked 12 earlier, so I'm not changing it without your say-so; just flagging that's the dial if this still isn't close enough.

Take a look and tell me what's off.
